# b055 report

## wa_fish_whistle: 5 of 5 written
- Index 0 is the drive (Mountain Loop Hwy, about 28 mi from Granite Falls, right on FR 4065 for 2.3 mi). Indexes 1-4 are the legs.
- DEFECT (direction): approach_variants[0].notes says "traverse **east** on the goat trail across the north face". The pins go the other way. The cairned saddle is at -121.51451 and the ledge-access notch is at -121.51736, so the traverse runs WEST/northwest. An independent approach description puts the ledge traverse at about 800 ft northwestward to exactly 48.01376,-121.51736, which is this row's pin 2. That source and the pins agree, so "east" in the variant is wrong. Because of that conflict I left the direction out of leg 2 entirely.
- Minor: the approach prose says "down to Vesper Creek and Vesper Lake (~4,950 ft)" after Headlee Pass (4,720 ft). The lake is higher than the pass, so "down" is misleading.
- Minor: pin 3 (Topout, 6,214 ft) and pin 4 (Summit, 6,221 ft) are essentially the same coordinate, and the two pins give different elevations for the same point. The trailhead pin (48.0248,-121.4778) and approach_logistics trailheadLat/Lng (48.02389,-121.47414) are about 300 m apart. That is small.
- Note: the approach says "Vesper-Wolf col" and pin 1 says "pass toward Copper Lake". These are consistent; both name the saddle with the two big cairns.

## wa_flight_of_the_falcon: 1 written (index 1); index 0 already had text
- DEFECT (time conflict): the pin-1 note says "~3-3.5 hr to the basin", while the approach and approach_variants say about 2 hr to the basin. I left any time out of the leg.
- Observation: the waypoints stop at the Waterfall Basin base. The route itself is on Salish Peak's south face, which is a long scramble beyond (up the left side of Roan Wall, about 6 hr total approach). No pin represents the actual route start or the Salish summit.

## wa_flycatcher_buttress: 2 written (indexes 1, 2); index 0 already had text
- DEFECT (pin placement): the pin 1 "Base of Flycatcher Buttress" (48.5128,-120.6558, 6,900 ft) sits about 20 m from the summit pin (48.51291,-120.65557, 7,760 ft). It is also slightly WEST of the summit, but this is an east-side route. The base pin is effectively on the summit, so the pin is misplaced. The base should be well east and below, near the foot of the east-side gully between the spires. distMi 0.65 is therefore unreliable too.
- Index 1 follows the standard Direct East Buttress approach (Spire Gully, last big tree, traverse right) and then keeps right of the dividing gully to the flat ledge.

## wa_foggy_peak_scramble: 1 written (index 0); indexes 1-6 already had text
- DEFECT (inconsistent "leave the trail" point): approach and approach_variants say to leave the Glacier Basin Trail near 4,200-4,500 ft just above the falls. Pin 4 "Snowline / Leave Maintained Trail" is at 5,000 ft, and pin 3 (the creek crossing) is at 3,600 ft. The pin name and elevation disagree with the prose about where you leave the trail.
- Minor: pin 0 (48.0268,-121.44229) vs approach_logistics trailhead (48.02639,-121.44389) is about 120 m apart, which is fine.

## wa_forbidden_peak_east_ledges: 1 written (index 0, drive-only mode)
- Drive: Cascade River Road from Marblemount, about 22 mi to the pullout below Johannesburg. Mileage sources differ: one says 21.7 mi, NPS says milepost 22.5. I wrote "about 22 miles".
- DEFECT (ordering / mixed direction): the waypoints run in descent order, from the summit rappel horn out to the East Ridge. route_breakdown describes the line in ascent order, from the saddle to the summit. Indexes 1-6 are all marked estimated.
- DEFECT (elevations): route_breakdown and approach put the East Ridge saddle near the Solitary Gendarme at 8,300-8,400 ft. Pin 5 "Gully back to ridge crest" is at 7,800 ft and pin 6 "Rejoin East Ridge" is at 7,300 ft. The East Ridge row's baseFinding gives the high bivy as about 8,000 ft, 300 ft below the gendarme.
- permit is null, but this is a National Park backcountry route (overnights need a park permit).

## wa_forbidden_peak_east_ridge: 4 of 4 written
- DEFECT (descent_text): it says the East Ledges option descends "above the crevassed Boston Glacier before regaining the glacier and the walk back to Boston Basin". The East Ledges row and its route_breakdown say the ledges rejoin the East Ridge crest near the Solitary Gendarme, and you descend the gully south into Boston Basin. The Boston Glacier is on the north side, and you do not walk back across it to Boston Basin.
- DEFECT (permit claim): the pin-0 note says "NW Forest Pass to park, self-issue wilderness permit required". The row's own permit field says no permit is needed for day climbs, and overnight stays need an NPS backcountry permit (Recreation.gov or the Marblemount WIC), not a self-issued one. I wrote only the NPS backcountry-permit line and made no parking-pass claim.
- Minor: the pin-0 note gives about 21.7 mi from Marblemount, and NPS lists the Boston Basin parking at milepost 22.5. Pin 1 camp is at 6,200 ft vs the prose's 6,300-6,600 ft. The day-1 itinerary note says camp is "on the peak's east side", but Boston Basin camp is south of the peak.
- Pin cross-check: East Ridge pin 2 "East end of ridge" (-121.054) is WEST of the East Ledges row's gendarme pin (-121.0505) and rejoin pin (-121.048). The two Forbidden rows disagree about where the Solitary Gendarme notch is. All of these pins are estimated.

<!-- b056.report.md -->
# b056 report

## wa_forbidden_peak_north_ridge (7 wps): 1 written (idx 1)
- idx 0 already had text, so I left it alone.
- idx 2-6 are blank because the pins contradict the route. Sharkfin Tower is at about 48.5006, -121.0412, east of Forbidden. The "Sharkfin Col" pin (48.50492, -121.06469) is about 1.7 km west of the tower and also west of Forbidden's summit. The "Rappel to Boston Glacier", "Boston Glacier crevasse traverse" and "North Ridge crest" pins all sit on a straight line from high camp to the summit, across Forbidden's SOUTH face. The Boston Glacier and the North Ridge are on the north and east side. These look like interpolated (synthetic) pins. A leg written from the prose (northeast up the Quien Sabe to the col, rappel north onto the Boston Glacier, traverse north) would contradict every pin.
- Itinerary day 1 puts high camp at about 7,500 ft below Sharkfin Tower. The camp waypoint and the approach say 6,400 ft.
- The existing idx 0 text and the trailhead note say a "self-issue wilderness permit". The permit field says overnight stays need a backcountry permit from Recreation.gov or the Marblemount WIC. These disagree. The same note is on all four Forbidden rows.

## wa_forbidden_peak_northeast_face (6 wps): 2 written (idx 0, 1)
- idx 2-5 are blank for the same reason: the synthetic camp-to-summit line runs across the south face. The prose route goes via Sharkfin Col (east) and then west on the Boston Glacier (north side).
- The descent_text has a pipeline reference in rendered prose: "(see wa_forbidden_peak_west_ridge for the full descent detail)".
- The climbing_route offers the East Ledges as a descent. The descent_text says only the West Ridge.

## wa_forbidden_peak_northwest_face (7 wps): 2 written (idx 0, 1)
- idx 2-6 are blank. They use the same synthetic pin line on the south face. The prose says the route starts from the Forbidden Glacier's north arm, on the mountain's west and north side.
- The high-camp note says "head northwest across glaciated terrain toward the face". The approach goes the other way, east via the Quien Sabe Glacier and Sharkfin Col, around the Boston Glacier. The note contradicts the route's own approach.
- The approach_variants text says "This route starts from the Forbidden Glacier's north arm, on the mountain's west side". The north arm is on the north and west side, so this is a minor wording problem.

## wa_forbidden_peak_west_ridge (4 wps): 4 written
- The pins are consistent: the camp is south-southwest, the notch is just west of the summit, and the summit is east of the notch.
- The baseFinding calls the notch "the low point on the skyline between Forbidden and Mount Torment, at 8,265 ft". The Torment-Forbidden col is a separate and much lower col. The West Ridge notch is a notch on Forbidden's own west ridge. This needs checking. I did not repeat the claim.
- The descent_text says "downclimb back to the notch, then work down the ridge crest", which is muddled ordering.

## wa_free_mojo (2 wps): 2 written
- The pins and the prose agree.

## wa_gato_negro (3 wps): 0 written (omitted)
- idx 0 already had text.
- idx 1 is blank because the approach sources conflict. The row says to go over Burgundy Col, drop off the east side and traverse south under the east faces. The Whine Spire area page (whose coordinates the pin copies) says to branch off right from the bench toward Silver Star, to the toe of the middle of three rock masses right of the pass, with no col crossing. I could not settle which is right.
- idx 2 is blank. It duplicates idx 1: same coordinates, same elevation, no distMi. That makes it a zero-length leg.
- The idx 0 pin is at 48.55, -120.6306 and approach_logistics has 48.549, -120.630965 (small mismatch). The note says MP 165 while the logistics say milepost 166.
- The idx 1 note says the "topout elevation is approximate". It is the area-page summit elevation.
- The itinerary is a plain string, not the usual days object.

<!-- b057.report.md -->
# b057 report

## wa_ghost_peak_south_route — 0 written (omitted from JSON)
Only index 5 (Luna Cirque high camp, near Lousy Lake) was empty. I left it blank because the records conflict. The approach and the itinerary put the high camp on a moraine on the Crooked Thumb Glacier, reached over Solar Pass. The pin's name, its note, and descent_text put it in Luna Cirque at Lousy Lake. Lousy Lake is a tarn on the Luna Cirque floor, and one party camped on that floor at about 5,500-5,600 ft, not at the pin's 6,400 ft. descent_text also routes the exit "over Pt. 7374 to Challenger Arm", which no other field mentions.
Defects:
- Two different high camps are on file: the Crooked Thumb Glacier moraine and Luna Cirque/Lousy Lake.
- The pin elevation (6,400 ft) looks too high for the cirque floor.
- The existing index 2 direction names "Graybeal" camp. Graybeal is on the Chilliwack, so this is minor.
- descent_text has first-person meta text ("I could not find a trip report…"). It renders.

## wa_glacier_peak_frostbite_ridge — 3 of 3 written
- Index 0: the drive to the North Fork Sauk trailhead.
- Leg 1: written only from what the sources agree on. It says "leave the PCT on an unmarked climbers' path" and does not say where.
- Leg 2: covers the Kennedy Glacier, the 8,800 ft saddle, the ridge, the Rabbit Ears, the ice, and the crater.
Defects:
- **Camp pin.** "Kennedy/Scimitar Glacier Camp" is at 48.13,-121.15 and 6,500 ft. Those are round, likely synthetic coordinates, and they sit on the PCT at Glacier Creek (about 5,300 ft). The prose says the high camp is near 7,200 ft.
- **Where to leave the PCT.** The prose says leave it about 0.7 mi past the Kennedy Creek crossing, "about 3 miles north" of the Kennedy Ridge trail. That is internally inconsistent: the Kennedy row puts the junction 0.25 mi north of Kennedy Creek. Outside sources and the Kennedy row say the climbers' path leaves at Glacier Creek, about 1.5-2 mi north of the junction.
- **Itinerary is a different trip.** It describes a Suiattle/Milk Creek approach and a Sitkum descent, while approach_logistics and the pin use the North Fork Sauk trailhead.
- **Ptarmigan traverse.** The approach and route_breakdown mention traversing the Ptarmigan Glacier's SW slopes and rounding Kennedy Peak. That appears to come from the Milk Creek side, so I omitted it.
- **Waypoint data.** The waypoints have no notes, and the camp and summit have null distMi.

## wa_glacier_peak_kennedy_glacier — 1 of 7 written (index 0 only)
I left all legs blank because the array is spliced. Waypoint 4, "Vista-Kennedy saddle (8,000 ft)", is a Frostbite Ridge feature. The route's own baseFinding says: "If you are standing on a saddle looking for a rock crest… you are on Frostbite. This route never leaves the ice."
Defects:
- **Synthetic-looking pins.** Waypoints 3, 4 and 5 all sit at latitude about 48.111, spaced evenly in longitude. A true Vista-Kennedy saddle would not lie on that line.
- **"Ridge saddle above camp".** Its note ("SSE up the basin to a flat saddle") does not match route_breakdown, which says to take a gully to the crest of Kennedy Ridge and then drop off its right side.
- **Wrong trailhead elevation.** The trailhead pin says 2,350 ft, which is the White Chuck trailhead's elevation. The North Fork Sauk trailhead is about 2,100 ft, as approach_logistics says.
- **distMi fits the White Chuck approach.** distMi 7.7 at the Kennedy Ridge/PCT junction matches the White Chuck approach (about 4 + 1.7 mi). From the North Fork Sauk trailhead the junction is about 18 mi.
- **Junction note.** The note says "turn to follow the PCT", but a party coming from the North Fork Sauk is already on the PCT.
- **Dated closure claims in prose.** These include FR 23 closed since March 19, 2025, and the Suiattle order through 2028.

## wa_glacier_peak_sitkum_glacier — 3 of 3 written
The drive, North Fork Sauk Trail #649 to the PCT junction, and one long leg: Red Pass, down the White Chuck to Sitkum Creek, the climbers' path to Boulder Basin, the Sitkum Glacier, the 9,500 ft saddle, and the summit. The prose and pins agree.
Defect: the summit has null distMi.

## wa_goat_mountain_east_peak — 1 of 1 (drive-only)
The drive: SR 542 east 12.5 mi from the Glacier Public Service Center, then FR 32 for 2.3 mi. No defects.

## wa_golden_horn_north_face — 2 of 2 written
The drive to the Rainy Pass PCT North trailhead, then the PCT to Cutthroat Pass, Granite Pass, the Snowy Lakes spur and the basin. The north face line itself is generic because no beta is on file.
Defects:
- The approach calls the Snowy Lakes spur "signed"; sources say it is a small, unmarked spur.
- The trailhead pin (48.5153,-120.73601) is about 0.25 mi from approach_logistics (48.5181,-120.7331). The difference is minor.
- The row has no descent, breakdown or climbing_route.

<!-- b058.report.md -->
# b058 report

## wa_golden_horn_southwest_route — 2/2 written
- Drive and the full trailhead-to-summit leg (PCT, Snowy Lakes spur, SW flank scree, summit block).
- Defect: `approach` and the variant's `baseFinding` call the Snowy Lakes spur "signed" ("the one piece of signage on the whole day"). Several trip reports describe it as a small unmarked/unlabeled spur. The leg text does not say either way. A climber relying on a sign could walk past it.
- Minor: `grade` is null on a route described as having a short 5th-class/class 4 summit block.

## wa_goode_mountain_megalodon_ridge — 2/2 written
- The leg leaves out where to step off the North Fork trail, because the row's two accounts disagree (an FA clearing before Grizzly Creek vs. past Grizzly at ~mile 14 with a ford). The leg says only "leave it for a steep bushwhack".
- Defect: the hike-out disagrees between fields. `itinerary` day 4 says Park Creek Trail, then "walk back up Bridge Creek on the PCT", ~10 mi. `descent_text` says Park Creek + Thunder Creek to Colonial Creek (or less commonly back to Bridge Creek), 15-20 extra miles.

## wa_goode_mountain_northeast_face — 2/2 written
- The leg is kept generic about the face's character.
- Defect: the row contradicts itself on what the face is. `itinerary` says "roped low-5th-class climbing similar to the Northeast Buttress". `descent_text` says "the sustained steep snow/ice character of the upper face on this route". `route_breakdown` and `climbing_route` are both null, so nothing settles it.

## wa_goode_mountain_southwest_couloir — 6 written (indices 1-6); index 0 already had text, so left ""
- Defect (pin): the "Grizzly Creek crossing" pin is at distMi 10.5, only ~0.5 trail mi past the PCT junction (distMi 10). It sits ~0.7 mi straight-line from the junction pin, so it is also shorter than the straight line. The North Fork trail reaches Walker Park ~2 mi above the junction and Grizzly Creek ~2 mi beyond that (~4 mi). The row's own Megalodon/approach prose puts Grizzly short of the ~mile 13-14 ford. The pin is probably misplaced, or its distMi is wrong. I wrote the leg without mileage so it does not contradict the pin.
- Defect: the moraine camp is at distMi 13, but `approach` puts the North Fork ford (which comes before camp) at "mile 13-14", and the itinerary day 1 is 15 mi. The distMi values look compressed.
- Defect: the Black Tooth Notch pin is a rounded placeholder (48.48, -120.91), probably not measured.
- Inconsistency: `route_breakdown` says the couloir steepens "near the notch between the summit and West Tower" and calls the Black Tooth Notch / upper NE Face finish something "some parties" do. The waypoints, the variant, the shared descent and external reports all route through Black Tooth Notch and then across the upper NE Face. The legs follow the waypoints.
- Minor: the variant says the trailhead is at "about 4,875 ft". The pin and `approach` say 4,400 ft.

## wa_gothic_peak_standard — 3/3 written
- Defect: `approach` puts Gothic Basin at "~4,000 ft" with Foggy Lake ~600 ft higher. `route_breakdown` says the basin is near 5,000 ft, and the lake really sits around 5,200 ft. The 4,000 figure is wrong.
- Defect (direction): the variant `baseFinding` says "if you are on the crest itself you have drifted right off the easy line". But the easy line is the scree on climber's RIGHT of the crest, so being on the crest means drifting LEFT. That note sends a climber the wrong way.
- Inconsistency: the summit block's size is given as ~30 ft (baseFinding), ~100 vertical ft (descent) and "final ~150 ft" (breakdown). The leg says only "short".
- Spelling: "Weeden" (approach/descent) vs "Weden" (official and elsewhere). I used Weden.
- The Foggy Lake waypoint has no elevation, and it is typed "Junction".

## wa_gunrunner — 2/2 written
- The leg follows the row's primary Downey/Bachelor/Dome-Chickamin approach. On the climb it states only what external first-ascent sources support: the traverse starts at the north end, goes over the Northeast and North peaks, through Gunsight Notch, and up the east-side dihedral to Middle Peak.
- Note: the Suiattle River Road has had washout closures (the USFS site showed the trailhead inaccessible as of Dec 2025). I left this out of the text as a dated status.
- Defect: the descent ends on the Blue Glacier (east side), but camp is at the Dome-Chickamin col (west side). Neither `descent_text` nor `itinerary` describes getting back from the Blue Glacier over to the Chickamin/Dome side.
- Minor: the trailhead pin (48.2595, -121.2229) and `approach_logistics` (48.2591, -121.2244) differ by ~120 m.

## wa_gunsight_peak_standard (8 waypoints) — 1 written (index 0)

<!-- b060.report.md -->
# b060 report

## wa_hozomeen_mountain_north_peak_north_route (2/2 written)
- 0: drive via Hope, Exit 168, Flood Hope Rd, Silver Skagit Road about 58 km to Hozomeen. The text says the border gate is closed to vehicles and walkers. That closure dates from 2020 and the NPS Hozomeen page still says "until further notice".
- 1: the whole route is a single leg (the Trail of the Obelisk loop, Howlett Creek, the west flank, the knoll at 5,900 ft, the north ridge, the cirque, the NE ridge ledges and the summit). It matches the approach and route_breakdown.
- Defects: the trailhead card says "(upper loop)", but NPS says the Upper Loop campsites are closed until further notice because of hazard trees. The approach gives no way in except the closed border road; there is no water-taxi variant, unlike the sibling SE Face row. The row also leaves out the separate 2026 Border 2 Fire closure of the Hozomeen area that the SE Face row reports.

## wa_hozomeen_mountain_southeast_face (2/2 written)
- 0: SR 20 to the Ross Dam Trailhead at about MP 134.
- 1: water taxi, then the Hozomeen Lake Trail, the outlet at about 2,400 ft, the drainage east, the chockstone gully, the slab wall, the 6,800 ft camp and the face to the summit. The text gives no trail distance and no rock grade, because the row's fields disagree on both.
- Defects:
  - approach_logistics.peakLat/peakLng (48.982185, -121.012035) are the NORTH Peak's coordinates. The South Peak pin is 48.972745, -120.997915.
  - The itinerary calls the approach basin the "west basin". The approach and baseFinding say the basin is "under the peak's south side", reached by going "east".
  - The trail distance before leaving the trail is "1.5 to 4 mi" in the approach and a flat "1.5 mi" in the itinerary.
  - The grade field says "Class 4 / low 5th", but the itinerary and descent describe a 5.6 headwall.
  - The trailhead card has no elev.
  - The itinerary's dated fire-closure sentence ("updated September 14, 2026") will go stale.

## wa_icy_peak_southwest_route (7/8 written; index 0 already had text)
- Defects:
  - **Direction conflict**: the approach and the approach_variant say to go "east/northeast" from Hannegan Pass to reach Ruth. The pass note says "south", and the pins say south (the pass is at 48.8837, the glacier toe at 48.8683). Ruth really is SSE of the pass. Leg 3 is written without a compass direction. The approach prose is wrong.
  - Card 5, "Ruth-Icy col descent", has elev 6600, but every prose field puts the Ruth-Icy saddle at about 5,700 ft. Card 6, the Icy Glacier traverse at 6900 ft, sits above the 6,500 ft notch the prose describes.
  - Cards 4, 5 and 6 have null lat/lng.
  - The itinerary gives Hannegan Pass as 5,056 ft; the pin and approach say 5,066 ft.
  - The itinerary says the permit comes from the "Glacier ranger station"; the permit field says Marblemount.
  - The note on card 1 says there is no reliable water past Hannegan Camp, while card 4 offers "seasonal snowmelt for water". This is minor.

## wa_indian_head_peak_southwest_slopes (1/1 written; drive-only)
- 0: Mountain Loop Hwy about 16 mi south of Darrington, then FR 49 about 6.5 mi to the road-end trailhead, with a NW Forest Pass.
- Defect: the approach says "17-19.7 mi" on the highway and "6.6-6.7 mi" on FR 49. The published figures are 16 mi and 6.4 mi. The row has only a trailhead pin, which is why it fails the ordering gate.

## wa_indian_mountain_baker_scramble (1/8 written; indices 1-7 already had text)
- 0: SR 542 to FS Road 32 just before the Nooksack bridge, then left at 1.3 mi onto Ruth Creek Road and 5.4 mi to the road end, with a NW Forest Pass.
- Defects in the existing data, not touched:
  - The card 6 pin (48.8886, -121.4006) is NORTH of Graybeal Camp, off the Brush Creek/Whatcom Pass trail, which heads SE. Yet its directions say to stay on the trail "another mile and a half toward Whatcom Pass".
  - The card 6 note says to "angle northwest toward Indian Mountain", but the summit (48.8988, -121.3898) is NE of that pin.
  - The card 5 directions say "roughly 1,250 ft over about two miles", but the pins go from 2,350 to 3,139 ft, which is about 800 ft.
  - The itinerary says the Brush Creek junction is "about 7 mi past Boundary Camp"; the pins put it about 5.8 mi past.
  - Day 2 says the summit is "3,600+ ft above the valley in about a mile". Card 7 says it is a 2,300 ft gain from the leave-trail point and that the peak drops 4,100 ft.

## wa_inspiration_traverse (1/6 written)
- 0: Cascade River Road about 20 mi from Marblemount to the Eldorado parking near MP 20, with an NCNP overnight permit.
- **Ordering fault, so all legs are left blank**: the pins run Eldorado, then Dorado Needle, then Klawatti, Austera and Primus. The row's itinerary and route_breakdown climb Eldorado, then Klawatti, Austera and Primus on day 2, and Dorado Needle last, on day 3 from the McAllister side. The pins put Dorado Needle second.
- Other defects:
  - There are no camp pins (the Inspiration Glacier high camp and the Klawatti col camp).
  - permit is null, but NCNP requires an overnight permit.
  - The descent goes back to the car from the camps, while the last summit pin is Primus, so the order of the traverse's return is unstated.
