## wa_jack_mountain_northeast_glacier (2) — 1 written
- [0] drive to East Bank TH. [1] left "": the approach is one line ("North from May Creek; northeast face via glacier approach"), there is no route description or descent, and I could not confirm a distinct "Northeast Glacier" line.
- Defects: the primary variant (water taxi from the Ross Dam trailhead) and approach_logistics (Ross Dam coordinates, labelled "May Creek (via Ross Lake shuttle)") do not match waypoint 0 (East Bank TH). The trailhead label is a camp, not a trailhead. A "High camp below the Nohokomeen Glacier" bivy is attached to this route's variant.

## wa_jack_mountain_southeast_ridge_direct (2) — 2 written
- [0] drive to Canyon Creek TH. MP 141, 32.8 mi east of Marblemount, NW Forest Pass / interagency pass, and the Pasayten Wilderness permit are confirmed by the USFS site page.
- [1] Canyon Creek, Trail #738, Jerry Glacier, the camp, then the crest.
- Defects: the creek crossing is described two ways, "ford, bridge destroyed" and "log-and-plank crossing", so I wrote only "cross Canyon Creek". The USFS page confirms a tree destroyed the bridge. The saddle is also described two ways: "Crater Mountain / Jerry Glacier saddle, traverse north under Crater" and "saddle between Crater's summit and its east peak". I wrote "the 7,200 ft saddle on Crater Mountain". approach_logistics is null, so there are no trailhead coordinates to check against.

## wa_jack_mountain_southwest_ridge (3) — 2 written
- [0] already filled, left "". [1] East Bank Trail to the junction, rightmost branch, Little Jack. [2] the ridge, following climbing_route.
- Defects: the existing [0] text says the East Bank Trail "runs west from there". It actually drops south to the Ruby Creek bridge first, and the other Jack rows say exactly that. This is minor. The approach says "continue northeast along the crest to the notch, the SW ridge rises directly ahead; stay on crest", but the variant says "follow crest north to the notch, then traverse across to the foot of Jack's SW ridge". These are slightly different lines; I followed the approach and climbing_route, which agree with each other. The variant's "if you are still walking flat ground half an hour past the junction" is a soft marker.

## wa_johannesburg_mountain_cj_couloir (4) — 3 written
- [0] already filled, left "". [1] road, Cascade River, moraine, apron. [2] couloir and choke to the C-J col. [3] East Ridge to the true summit.
- Defects: waypoint 1 "Base of CJ Couloir" is at **elev 3,700 ft, above the 3,600 ft trailhead**, but every piece of prose descends from the road to an apron at about 3,300 ft. It also has **null lat/lng**. Waypoint 2 "East ridge gain" is at 6,500 ft, but the prose puts the C-J col, where the ridge is gained, at about 6,760 ft. Its distMi values (0.7 / 1.4 / 2.2) are also hard to square with "a quarter mile to just under a mile" of road walking plus 3,000 ft of couloir.

<!-- b062.report.md -->
# b062 report

## wa_johannesburg_mountain_northeast_rib_1951_route (2/2 written)
- 0: the drive to Cascade Pass TH (23 mi Cascade River Road to its end; no day-use pass, and an NCNP permit is needed to camp).
- 1: the whole climb from the lot to the summit, taken from the approach, approach_variants and route_breakdown.
- Defect (minor): there are only two pins, so nothing marks the moat, the bivy ledge (~7,100 ft) or the col. The 1951 and 1957 starts are not separated.

## wa_katsuk_peak_gully (1/8 written; indices 1-7 already had text)
- 0: the drive. It is about 45 mi from Marblemount on SR-20, turning between MP 151 and 152, and needs an NW Forest Pass or America the Beautiful pass.
- DEFECT: the `approach` says the trailhead is "~37 miles from Marblemount". It is about 45 mi (Marblemount is near MP 106 and the trailhead is near MP 151). The "~30 miles east of Newhalem" figure is about right.
- DEFECT: the existing leg 6 text ("Grotesque Gendarmes traverse") says to traverse west "toward the low point between Katsuk and Kimtah". Its pin (-120.8817) sits EAST of the Katsuk summit pin (-120.8888), and the Katsuk–Kimtah low point is west of Katsuk. In the Kimtah row the same "Grotesque Gendarmes" are pinned at -120.8946, which is west of Katsuk. So the two rows place one named feature about 1 km apart, and this pin contradicts its own text.
- Minor: waypoint 1 (Granite Creek footlog) has no elevation.

## wa_kimtah_peak_scramble (2/8 written; indices 1-6 already had text)
- 0: the drive (same text as Katsuk).
- 7: from the gully top to the true (east) summit. I left out any compass direction on purpose (see below).
- DEFECT: the waypoint 6 note says the gully reaches "the ridge just west of the summit", and the summit note says to scramble "east along the ridge crest" from there. But the gully pin (-120.9036, 8,100 ft) sits about 0.5 km EAST of the summit pin (-120.9106). Either the gully pin is misplaced or the summit pin is on the west summit; the itinerary mentions east and west summits with the east one true.
- Minor: waypoint 5 (gendarmes, 7,150 ft) sits below the 7,400-7,600 ft bench the leg describes. That fits the 250-300 ft downclimb, so it is not a fault.

## wa_klawatti_peak_southeast_face (5/8 written; index 0 already had text)
- Written: 1 (river crossing and climbers' path to the talus), 2 (boulder field to the Roush Creek camps), 3 (onto the Eldorado Glacier), 4 (to the Eldorado-Inspiration high camp), 7 (schrund, snow face, summit crest).
- Left blank: 5 (Klawatti Glacier crossing) and 6 (bergschrund). The records disagree about which glacier lies between high camp and the face:
  - the `approach` says east across the Inspiration Glacier to Klawatti Col, then directly up the south flank;
  - waypoint 5 says "crossing the crevassed Klawatti Glacier";
  - the itinerary says "Cross the McAllister/Klawatti Glacier below the face".
  The array also has no Klawatti Col waypoint between 4 and 5.
- DEFECT: the existing index-0 text says Cascade River Road ends at the Eldorado trailhead. The road actually continues about 3 more miles to the Cascade Pass trailhead, so a driver told to go "to its end" overshoots.
- DEFECT: waypoint 4's note puts the high camp (48.5315, -121.1252, near Eldorado's east ridge) "near Klawatti Col". The col is about 2.5 km NNE (48.553, -121.106).
- DEFECT: five of eight pins (1, 2, 3, 5, 6) have null lat/lng.
- Inconsistency: the route is named the "southeast face", but the prose describes a south-facing flank above the col, finishing via the "southwest ridge" / "upper southwest side". The summit note puts the snow BEFORE the low-5th rock, while climbing_route and route_breakdown put the rock step at the schrund FIRST. My leg 7 follows the majority.
- Minor: the trailhead elevation is 2,150 here and 2,100 in the SW Buttress row.

## wa_klawatti_peak_sw_buttress (3/4 written; index 0 already had text)
- 1: from the trailhead to the Eldorado ice-cap camp. 2: the Inspiration Glacier traverse NNE to Klawatti Col (the pins agree). 3: the moat, the buttress and the summit crest.
- Minor: the col is 7,900 ft at the pin and 7,800 ft in the prose. The trailhead and summit pins have no distMi.

## wa_koala_krack (2/2 written)
- 0: the drive to the SR-20 hairpin turnout east of Washington Pass. I gave no milepost and no pass requirement, because I could not confirm either.
- 1: boulder field, Kangaroo Pass, the Temple–Little Finger gully, a face route, then Koala Krack to the top.
- Defect (structural): the route is only a summit-block pitch, reached by finishing another line. With two pins the leg necessarily spans the whole approach plus "finish one of the face routes"; there is no notch or base pin.

<!-- b063.report.md -->
# b063 report

## wa_kololo_peaks_standard (7 waypoints): 1 written
- Wrote [0], the drive: Darrington, Mountain Loop Hwy about 16 mi south, then FR 49 for 6.4 mi. [1]-[6] already had text, so they were left alone.
- Defects:
  - [5] "Glacier Gap crevasse crossing" is pinned at 6,433 ft. That number matches "Lake 6433", the camp named in the itinerary, so the name and the elevation are probably two different features.
  - The existing [5] directions say "The maintained trail ends here. Traverse north below the White Mountain ridge into Foam Basin". That describes the White Pass leg, not arriving at Glacier Gap, so the text is shifted one slot.
  - The existing [6] directions say "From the gap gain the flat shoulder at about 6,000 ft". The gap pin itself sits at 6,433 ft, so this sentence contradicts it.
  - `route_breakdown` starts with "White River Trail #1507" and a "south ridge shoulder". That is the east-side approach (Wenatchee side), spliced into a row whose pins and approach are all the west-side North Fork Sauk line.
  - The [4] pin is named "White Pass (PCT junction)", but the approach says #649 meets the PCT about 1/4 mi south of White Pass. They are two separate points.
  - The trailhead elevation disagrees within the row: 2,050 ft in `approach` and on the pin, 2,080 ft in `itinerary`.

## wa_kyes_peak_glaciated_scramble (8 waypoints): 6 written
- Wrote [2]-[7]. [0] and [1] already had text.
- The array follows the Columbia Glacier variant in a consistent order: Virgin Lake, Blanca Lake, glacier toe, glacier, exit, ridge junction, summit. The pins climb northward consistently.
- Research confirmed the logjam crossing at the outlet and the boot path along the west shore of Blanca Lake.
- Defects:
  - The `primary` approach variant and the main `approach` text describe the south-ridge climbers' path from Virgin Lake, and warn that descending to Blanca Lake is "the most common error". This route's pins follow the glacier variant, so on one route page the prose calls the pinned path a mistake.
  - `route_breakdown` mentions "above the northeast base". That matches neither variant.
  - The Blanca Lake pin is labelled "Water" and sits at 3,976 ft. Its `distMi` is 4.0, only 0.5 mi past Virgin Lake at 3.5, which is plausible.

## wa_labor_pains (3 waypoints): 2 written
- Wrote [0] (Blue Lake TH, about 1 mi west of Washington Pass, 5,200 ft) and [1] (the climbers' path to the Chockstone Route start).
- Left [2] (summit) blank. Sources give 5 or 6 pitches and do not confirm whether Labor Pains tops out on the summit.
- Defects:
  - The [1] "Chockstone Route start" pin has exactly the summit coordinates (48.51291,-120.65557), with an elevation of 6,900 ft against 7,760 ft for the summit. It is a fabricated or copied pin.
  - `approach_logistics.trailheadDirection` gives the trailhead as 5,400 ft. The pin and the land-manager figures say 5,200 ft.
  - Pitch counts disagree: 4 entries in `route_breakdown`, 5 in the prose, 6 in another source.
  - One source places the route between the West Face and the Southwest Face. The row says it lies between the Chockstone Route and the West Face. Not resolved.

## wa_last_chance_for_gas_aka_tricky_start (3 waypoints): 0 written, omitted
- [0] already had text.
- Ordering fault: [1] Topout comes before [2] Base. Under the rule for broken arrays, every leg is left blank.
- All three pins share the same coordinate, and the Base pin has no elevation.

## wa_le_conte_mountain_northern_aspect (4 waypoints): 3 written
- Wrote [1] (the Cascade Pass Trail), [2] (south up Mixup Arm to the Cache Glacier, then Cache Col) and [3] (Kool-Aid Lake, Red Ledge, Middle Cascade Glacier, Spider-Formidable col, Yang Yang camp, then the summit route). [0] already had text.
- Defects:
  - `approach` says to continue "north on the Ptarmigan Traverse" from Cascade Pass. That is wrong: the traverse and every pin run south, from Cascade Pass at 48.468 to Le Conte at 48.379. A climber who follows the text would head the wrong way.
  - The `approach` omits the Red Ledge and the Spider-Formidable col between Kool-Aid Lake and Yang Yang Lakes.
  - The waypoint array has no Yang Yang Lakes camp, so leg [3] has to span a multi-day stretch. Missing pins worth adding: Kool-Aid Lake, Spider-Formidable col, Yang Yang Lakes.
  - The route `grade` is null.

## wa_lexington_tower_east_face (2 waypoints): 2 written
- Wrote [0] (about 1.2 mi east of Washington Pass, the hairpin pond pullout) and [1] (the climbers' path, the ledge at the base, the 10 pitches, and the catwalk to the notch).
- Defects:
  - The trailhead elevation disagrees: the pin says 5,450 ft, `approach_logistics` says "~5,100 ft". The hairpin sits well below Washington Pass at 5,477 ft, so 5,450 is probably wrong.
  - The [1] "notch" pin has a null elevation and is typed Summit, although the card is the notch below the summit. It also reuses the peak coordinate exactly.
  - The itinerary gives 6.5 miles car-to-car against an approach of about 1/3 mi. That only fits if the walk-off down the Blue Lake Trail is counted.

<!-- b064.report.md -->
# b064 report: Liberty Bell routes

## wa_liberty_and_injustice_for_all (1 of 2 written)
- [0] the drive to the SR-20 hairpin, written.
- [1] the summit, left blank. The route does NOT reach the summit: it ends at the top of pitch 7 of Thin Red Line, about 200 m up, and the descent rappels the route. A leg that ends on the "Liberty Bell Mountain" summit card would be false.
- DEFECT: the summit waypoint is the wrong endpoint for this route, which has no summit. Its distMi of 2 is also doubtful, because the East Face approach is about 1 mile.

## wa_liberty_bell_beckey_route (2 of 3 written; [0] already had text)
- [1] to the notch, and [2] from the notch up the Beckey Route to the summit.
- DEFECT: the `approach` prose says "follow the Blue Lake Trail north" and turn "left/north". Both pins put Liberty Bell south-east of the trailhead, and the trail runs south from SR-20, so "north" is wrong. The left turn (east) is correct.
- DEFECT: the record conflicts on whether the climbers' trail junction is signed. approach_variants says it is signed "Liberty Bell Trail #727, Climbing Access" with a large cairn. Overexposure's junction waypoint says it is unsigned. I wrote neither claim.
- NOTE: the land manager puts the climbers' branch at about 1.7 mi. The rows say 1.5 mi. I kept "about 1.5 miles" to match the rows.

## wa_liberty_bell_girl_next_door (2 of 2 written)
- [0] the drive to Blue Lake TH, and [1] the trail, the side gully with the cave, the 3 pitches, and the Beckey finish to the summit.
- DEFECT: the trailhead pin elev is 5200. The other Blue Lake TH rows say 5,380 to 5,400.
- No approach_logistics, permit or itinerary on file.

## wa_liberty_bell_independence_route (1 of 3 written)
- [0] the drive to the hairpin, written.
- [1] and [2] left blank. DEFECT: waypoint 1, "Committing mid-wall position" (Hazard), sits at 48.514666,-120.658696, 7300 ft. That is exactly the Liberty Bell-Concord NOTCH coordinate used by Overexposure. It is on the opposite (SW) side of the peak from this East Face route. Because the mid-wall point is undefined and mis-pinned, neither leg can be written honestly.

## wa_liberty_bell_overexposure (1 of 7 written; [1]-[6] already had text)
- [0] the drive, written.
- DEFECT: the trailhead note says "about 1.5 mi west of Washington Pass". The TH is a little under a mile west of the Washington Pass Overlook, and the other rows say ~1 mi. The approach_logistics trailheadDirection also says 1.5 mi.
- DEFECT: the "Stream crossing near meadow" pin (48.51298,-120.658866, 5750 ft) is about 190 m south of the notch pin (7300 ft), almost directly beneath it. A 1,550 ft drop over 190 m cannot be right, so the pin should sit near the Blue Lake Trail, about 1 mi farther west.
- DEFECT: the "Climbers' path junction" waypoint has null lat/lng.
- DEFECT: the existing [2] directions say the junction is "unsigned". That conflicts with the Beckey approach_variants, which says it is signed.
- The itinerary says "approach into the basin below the west face… several rappels back down the route into the approach couloir". That loosely matches descent_text, so it is not a defect.

## wa_liberty_bell_serpentine_crack (2 of 2 written)
- [0] the drive, and [1] the trail, the gully, the left fork, the 4th-class ledges to the slung pine, the 4 pitches, and the arete to the summit.
- The on-file `approach` text is useless ("West, from Washington Pass area near Winthrop"). The start details come from external research.
- MINOR: approach_logistics puts the trailhead at 48.5197,-120.674183 and the peak at 48.51548,-120.65874. Both differ slightly from the pins and from the other rows' 48.518965,-120.67436.

<!-- b065.report.md -->
# b065 report

Total: 16 entries written across 6 routes.

## wa_liberty_bell_sidewinder: 2 of 2
- Index 0 is the Blue Lake TH drive. Index 1 covers the trail, the climbers' path, breaking left at the fork, the climb and the scramble to the summit.
- DEFECT: the trailhead pin says elev 5200, but the USGS ground at that coordinate is about 5,380 ft. wa_liberty_traverse puts the same TH at 5400, which is correct. I left the elevation out of the drive text.

## wa_liberty_bell_thin_red_line: 2 of 3 (index 0 already had text)
- Wrote legs 1 and 2. The summit is reached by easy scrambling, according to route_breakdown and the summit note.
- DEFECT (existing index 0 directions, and the `approach`/variant text): it says the climbers' path "runs east into the forest" from the pond. The TH pin sits on SR-20 at the hairpin (OSM confirms it is on Highway 20; ground is about 5,150 ft). The summit is about 1.1 km due WEST of it. The path LEAVES from the pond's east end, but it climbs west toward the East Face. "Runs east" sends a climber the wrong way. My leg 1 says "from the east end of the pond" and gives no compass direction.
- DEFECT: approach_variants says "this route's stored trailhead coordinate does not agree with the one every other East Face route on this peak carries". That is false or stale: wa_liberty_crack_free carries the identical coordinate (48.51454, -120.64332), and it is on the highway at the hairpin.

## wa_liberty_crack_free: 1 of 2
- Index 0 is the drive to the hairpin pullout plus the walk to the pond. It names no pass, because the hairpin pullout is not a developed trailhead.
- Index 1 (summit) left blank because two on-file fields disagree. The summit note says the route "finishes up the Beckey Route's upper pitches to the true summit". route_breakdown P12 and descent_text say it ends at the bolted alcove near the summit shoulder and rappels into Beckey Gully from there. A leg arriving at the summit would not be supported.

## wa_liberty_traverse: 2 of 2
- Blue Lake TH drive, then the Blue Lake Trail, the cairned junction, the notch gully and the Beckey Route to Liberty Bell's summit. That summit is the only other pin.
- Note: the array has only TH plus Liberty Bell for a five-summit traverse, so Concord, Lexington, NEWS and SEWS have no pins.

## wa_lincoln_peak_north_ridge: 1 of 8 (indices 1-5 and 7 already had text)
- Index 0: the drive via SR-542 and Glacier Creek Road FS 39, about 8 mi to the Heliotrope Ridge TH. Confirmed against the land manager's directions.
- Index 6 (Black Buttes basin camp to NW Face start) left blank because the on-file prose conflicts. `approach` says to cross the lower THUNDER Glacier to the base of the NW face. approach_variants says to stay high and right on the COLEMAN, traverse beneath the Coleman icefall, and NOT cut toward the Thunder Glacier side. The itinerary also calls the base the amphitheater between Lincoln and Assassin Spire, while the variant says the cirque between Colfax and Lincoln.
- DEFECT: the route is named "North Ridge", but every field describes a northwest face/ice line.
- Minor: the TH pin says 3700 ft; the ground is about 3,590 ft.

## wa_little_mac_spire_southwest_route: 6 of 8
- Index 0 is the Goodell Creek drive. Legs 1-5 run TH, creek crossings, cairn, steep path to the streambed, notch, then the drop to Terror Basin camp. They are supported by the approach prose, the itinerary, the notes, and trip reports confirming the cairn/arrow "hard right uphill" at the end of the old road.
- Index 6 (hazard below summit block) and index 7 (summit) left blank because the on-file prose disagrees about how to reach the route. `approach` says to angle north/northeast from camp past the tarn below West McMillan, staying on the McMillan side and not crossing "the Barrier". The itinerary says to go to the saddle east of East McMillan and "traverse above Azure Lake", which lies on the far (north) side of the McMillan Spires. The summit pin is also self-declared approximate.
- DEFECT: waypoint 2 (Terror Creek Cairn) has lat/lng null.
- DEFECT: the prose describes the cairn two ways. The waypoint note puts it at the turn off the Goodell Creek trail. `approach` describes a separate higher "unsigned junction" where the Terror Basin path splits from a Crescent Creek path. The note also calls the Goodell Creek trail "maintained", while `approach` says it is unmaintained.
- Minor: the TH pin says 500 ft; the prose says ~600 ft. The TH pin (48.6828, -121.269) and approach_logistics (48.68664, -121.27121) are about 0.5 km apart. I could not confirm that the trail actually fords two branches of Goodell Creek, so leg 1 does not claim fords.
- The descent_text names a guide company as its source ("A guided-party account ... (Mountain Madness)"). This breaks the no-sources rule if it renders.

<!-- b066.report.md -->
# b066 report

## wa_little_sister_north_face (2/2 written)
- 0: the drive via SR 9, Mosquito Lake Road and FR 38 (checked against land-manager and trail pages), plus the Dec 1–Jun 15 Wallace Creek gate.
- 1: the Green Creek approach to the foot of the face, ending where the glacier meets the wall, as the card names.
- DEFECT: the "North Face (reference point)" pin (48.69241, -121.97649) is the summit coordinate. It is not on the face.
- NOTE: FR 38 currently has a washout and barrier at about MP 7, and FR 12 closes from Aug 2026. I left this out because it is a dated status. The itinerary already mentions a washout at MP 7.

## wa_little_sister_south_couloir (2/2 written)
- 0: the same drive. 1: the approach to the Green Creek Glacier, then the 6,240+ ft SE col, then the south gully to the summit.
- Defects: none noticed.

## wa_little_sister_west_face (1/2 written)
- 0: the drive.
- 1 left blank. The approach prose only says "around to the west side" and "cross the divide", with no line onto the west face. The pin is also the summit coordinate rather than the face (same defect as North Face).

## wa_lizard_mountain_south_route (1/8 written)
- 0: the drive (Cascade River Road, 23 mi). Indexes 1–7 already had text, so I did not overwrite them.
- DEFECT (in an existing leg): leg 7 says "gain the peak's south ridge", but the itinerary has the descent going down the west ridge. Also, the `route_breakdown` gives the start as the Sentinel–Lizard saddle at 6,940 ft, while the approach gives Lizard Col at about 6,800 ft. Two elevations for the start col.
- The approach prose says the Suiattle Road is closed "until at least January 2028". That is a dated claim.

## wa_luna_glacier (2/2 written)
- 0: SR 20 to the Ross Dam Trailhead near MP 134. 1: down to the dam, the lakeshore trail or water taxi to Big Beaver, the Big Beaver Trail to Luna Camp, cross-country into Luna Cirque, then the climb to the summit.
- CONFLICT: three approaches into the cirque are on file. `approach` and `approach_variants` say Access Creek is the modern line, Luna Creek is the historic bushwhack, and the itinerary says Wiley Ridge. The leg names none of them and only says "cross-country into Luna Cirque".
- DEFECT: the approach says the Access Creek confluence is "roughly a mile past Luna Camp". I could not confirm this and did not use it.
- The approach quotes the first-ascent party's description of the brush on the Luna Creek bushwhack. A source is named in the prose: the field reads "Fred Beckey himself described…", and the variant notes repeat the quote with "the first-ascent party" in Beckey's place.
- The $35 water-taxi price in the approach is stale: the current one-way fare to Big Beaver is $110.

## wa_magic_mountain_north_face (3/3 written)
- 0: the drive. 1: the trail to Cascade Pass. 2: Pelton Basin, the Yawning Glacier, the Magic-Pelton Col (6,480 ft), west across the glacier head, then the 600 ft Class 4 face to the NW notch and the summit. This matches the on-file prose and a published route description.
- Defects: none noticed. The `route_breakdown` entries carry pipeline voice ("Sourced anchors:", "Length apportioned within the sourced…").

<!-- b067.report.md -->
# b067 report

- **wa_magic_mountain_northeast_couloir**: 2 written (legs 1, 2). Index 0 already had text, so I left it alone.
  - Defect: route_breakdown and the variant notes call the couloir about 2,000 ft, starting at the Magic–Pelton Col at 6,480 ft. The summit pin is 7,610 ft, which leaves only about 1,130 ft above the col. Either the col height or the couloir length is wrong. I left both numbers out of the leg.
  - Defect: the row's own baseFinding says it is unclear which northeast line or col this is (NW-shoulder col, Alliteration Col or Magic–Pelton col). The leg follows the approach prose (Magic–Pelton col), which is the line every on-file field agrees on.
  - Note: the existing index-0 text suggests starting from the "Eldorado parking area" in spring. That is a dated, seasonal tip; it is not wrong.
- **wa_magic_mountain_northwest_ridge**: 2 written (legs 1, 2). Index 0 already had text, so I left it alone. No defects found.
- **wa_magic_mountain_southwest_cirque**: 3 written (drive plus 2 legs). No defects found.
- **wa_magic_mountain_west_ridge**: 3 written (drive plus 2 legs).
  - Defect: the pitch count disagrees with itself. The approach variant says "Seven pitches" (route_breakdown also has 7 entries), but the itinerary says "four pitches or so". The leg names no pitch count.
- **wa_marvin_s_ear** (drive-only): 1 written (drive via the Mountain Loop Hwy and FS 4065).
  - Minor: approach_logistics gives the trailhead elevation as about 2,340 ft; the pin says 2,350 ft. This is trivial.
- **wa_mcgregor_mountain_scramble**: 1 written (index 0: the ferry and shuttle to High Bridge; there is no drive). Legs 1-6 already had text, so I left them alone.
  - Minor: the approach prose says the summit is at "mile 6.8 of 7.7", but the summit pin's distMi is 7.8.
  - Minor: the scramble waypoint's note says "Class 3", while the leg text says "class 2-3".
- All three Kool-Aid Lake–side Magic routes share one summit pin (48.4461, -121.0414), and their legs are consistent with it.

<!-- b068.report.md -->
# b068 report

## wa_mcmillan_spire_west_southwest_ridge — 2 of 2 written
- [0] drive to Goodell Creek / Upper Goodell Group Camp trailhead (SR-20, ~MP 119, just west of Newhalem). [1] TH to summit as one leg, taken from the on-file approach/approach_variants.
- Defect (minor): the array has only TH and summit, so the whole 2–3 day approach sits in one leg. A Terror Basin camp pin, a glacier pin and a base-of-ridge pin would match the sibling West Ridge row.

## wa_mcmillan_spire_west_west_ridge — 1 written (index 5); 7 already filled
- [5] Terror Basin camp to Terror Glacier crossing: the traverse over benches and slabs to the lake outlet, then slabs onto the glacier (from the on-file approach). The pins agree: the glacier lies NNW of camp.
- Defect: the index-6 col pin (7,200 ft Inspiration–McMillan col) has null lat/lng. Note also that the leg-4 text (already filled) describes the 6,200 ft notch, which is not a pin of its own.

## wa_mojo_rising — 2 of 2 written
- [0] drive to Blue Lake TH (south side of SR-20, ~1 mi west of Washington Pass, between MP 161–162, ~42 mi east of Newhalem). [1] Blue Lake Trail to the NW clearing, climbers' path to the west-face basin, start 20 ft downhill of the chockstone chimney, finish on the Southwest Rib.
- Defect (minor): the TH pin elevation is 5,400 ft. Published figures put the Blue Lake TH near 5,200 ft. Not verified against the ground.

## wa_morning_star_peak_standard — 2 of 2 written
- [0] Mountain Loop Hwy ~17 mi past Verlot, right onto FS Road 4065 (Sunrise Mine Rd) 2.2 mi to its end. [1] Whole scramble line from the on-file approach/route_breakdown.
- Defect: the trailhead waypoint has no `elev`. The approach says 2,350 ft and approach_logistics says ~2,340 ft (2,340 is the published figure).

## wa_mount_ann_scramble — 0 written (omitted from JSON); index 0 already filled
- All legs 1–7 left blank because the waypoints are in the wrong order. Lake Ann (i=3, 3.9 mi) is listed BEFORE the 4,900 ft saddle (i=4, 4.1 mi). The route's own prose and trip reports agree the scramble leaves the trail at the saddle above and NW of the lake, before the trail drops to the lake, so a party never visits the lake on this route. The Lake Ann pin (48.8260, -121.6551) is only ~0.1 mi from the saddle pin. It probably does not sit on the lake itself, which lies below the saddle toward Shuksan; I did not confirm this.
- Defect: the Swift Creek Trail 607 junction pin is at distMi 1, elev 4,000. The land manager puts the signed 607 junction ~2.5 mi from the trailhead at the basin bottom. The pin labelled "Swift Creek meadow crossing" at 2.5 mi is where that junction should be, so the junction pin and its distance look wrong.

## wa_mount_arriva_scramble — 5 of 7 written
- [0] drive (SR-20 ~45 mi east of Marblemount, south side between MP 151–152). [1] Granite Creek footlog at ~300 yd, then forest to the Easy Pass Creek crossing. [2] avalanche paths on Ragged Ridge to the pass. [3] over the pass into Fisher Creek Basin. [6] notch to summit via the dark-dike defile and the left-trending ramp (from on-file baseFinding).
- [4] basin → talus apron and [5] apron → 7,800 ft notch left BLANK because the pins and the prose describe different lines. The on-file approach, approach_variants and itinerary go up-valley to the head of Fisher Creek, climb the ~1,000 ft headwall couloir to Silent Lakes (~7,000 ft, the camp), traverse west ~1 mi to a 7,200 ft saddle, then cross a talus bowl to the notch. None of Silent Lakes, the headwall or the 7,200 saddle is a waypoint. The pins instead run from a basin pin just south of Easy Pass (5,600 ft) to a "talus and snow apron below the SW slopes" (6,000 ft) and straight to the notch. That reads like a different, direct line that the prose never describes.
- Defect: the Easy Pass note says "leave the maintained tread here and drop west off-trail". The prose and the itinerary instead descend into Fisher Creek Basin and leave the trail near the Fisher Camp junction. The basin pin's note ("Fisher Creek Trail continues down-valley to Fisher Camp ~2 mi further") also disagrees with the approach, which turns UP-valley near Fisher Camp.
- Defect: the pin "Easy Pass Creek footlog crossing" is at 1.2 mi. The published footlog is the Granite Creek crossing ~300 yards from the trailhead; Easy Pass Creek is crossed several times higher up. The note's "on a log... sketchy/submerged" may belong to the Granite Creek footlog. My leg-1 text names both crossings without claiming a footlog at the pin.
- Defect: the climbing_route note says "The source text describes nothing further about this ground." This is pipeline/meta voice in a rendered field.

<!-- b069.report.md -->
# b069 report: Mount Baker (6 routes)

Drives checked against the land-manager and trail-association pages for Boulder Ridge and Heliotrope Ridge. FS 39 status was also checked.

## wa_mount_baker_boulder_glacier (2/2 written)
- 0: the drive (SR 20 MP 82 to Baker Lake Rd FS 11 for 18 mi, then left on FS 1130 for 1 mi, then left on FS 1131 to the road end). The online source confirms the turn sequence.
- 1: the full trail plus glacier leg to Grant Peak. It gives no camp elevation on purpose (see defects).
- Defects: the camp elevation is stated four ways. `approach` says ~5,500 ft. The itinerary says 5,500-5,700. baseFinding says the ridge runs into snow "near 6,300 ft". The variant notes admit that "two elevations are published". There is only a TH and a summit pin, so the 8 mi leg is one big jump with no camp pin. The itinerary says "climb the glacier alongside the cleaver", but route_breakdown says the line goes "south of the cleaver" and merges with it above 9,200. These are compatible, but the wording is loose.

## wa_mount_baker_boulder_park_cleaver (1 written; 0 already had text)
- 1: the trail to camp, across the lower glacier NW to the cleaver toe, along the cleaver to the 9,300 ft buttress, then the finishes to Grant Peak.
- Defects: the rows disagree about which glacier is crossed to reach the cleaver. The itinerary says "Cross the Park Glacier" and descent_text says "reverse the cleaver and Park Glacier terrain". The approach_variant, baseFinding and route_breakdown say "across the lower Boulder Glacier, northwest". My leg names neither glacier ("the lower glacier"). The existing index-0 drive omits the highway (SR 20 / Baker Lake Rd start) and the left turns. It is not wrong, just thin.

## wa_mount_baker_cockscomb_ridge (4 written; 0 already had text)
- 1 TH to Hogsback camp, 2 camp to ridge base (east-northeast across the Coleman, then the Roosevelt), 3 base to Cockscomb Tower, 4 tower to summit. The pins agree with the direction of travel: east, then south up the ridge.
- Defects: the "Heliotrope Ridge Camp" pin (48.7893,-121.86777, 5,900 ft) is a different coordinate from the Coleman-Deming "Hogsback Camp" pin (48.788,-121.85, 6,000 ft) for the same camp, about 0.8 mi apart. approach_logistics peakLat/Lng (48.7797,-121.8204) is not the summit coordinate the other Baker rows use (48.776797,-121.814467).

## wa_mount_baker_coleman_deming (3 written; 0 already had text)
- 1 TH to Hogsback, 2 Hogsback to saddle, 3 saddle to Roman Wall to Grant Peak.
- Defects (important): the EXISTING index-0 text says "FS 39 is closed to vehicles through October 2026 after washouts". That is now stale. Glacier Creek Road reopened on Aug 20, 2026 after the repair closure. `approach_logistics.seasonalNotes` carries the same stale claim. Both should be rewritten as durable text. The "Black Buttes Saddle" pin is at 9,200 ft, but every piece of prose puts the Colfax-Baker saddle at ~9,000 ft. The name "Black Buttes Saddle" is also unusual; the prose calls it the Colfax saddle. The trailhead elevation is stored as 3,650 (`approach`, `trailhead_elevation_ft`) vs 3,700 (pin, trailheadDirection).

## wa_mount_baker_easton_glacier (2 written; 0 already had text)
- 1 TH to Sandy Camp via Railroad Grade 603.2, 2 Sandy Camp to Sherman Crater rim to Roman Wall to Grant Peak.
- Defects: the Sandy Camp pin has lat/lng = null, so the card cannot be placed on the map. The existing index-0 drive names no highway or turn sequence (SR 20 / Baker Lake Rd / FS 12 / FS 13 are missing). It only says "the end of Forest Road 13". The junction distance is given as ~2 mi (`approach`, at Morovitz) vs 2.25 mi (variant). Spelling is inconsistent: Sulfur/Sulphur and Schreiber's/Schriebers. approach_logistics peakLat/Lng (48.7773,-121.8132) differs from the shared summit coordinate.

## wa_mount_baker_north_ridge (4/4 written)
- 0: the drive (SR 542, ~1 mi past the Glacier Public Service Center, right on FS 39 for ~8 mi, left at the junction, <0.5 mi to the TH), plus the pass. 1: TH to camp. 2: camp east across the Coleman to the double bergschrund at the ridge base. 3: schrund, gully, ice cliff, then the summit.
- Defects: `approach` and route_breakdown[0] say the crossing goes east "below Colfax Peak". Colfax is ~1.2 mi SOUTH of the camp-to-ridge-base line (the base pin is 48.7897,-121.84, due east of camp). That phrase looks copied from the Coleman-Deming route and contradicts the pins, so I left it out. The ridge-base pin is 6,900 ft, while the prose has the ridge crest reached via a 40° gully from the schrund and gives no number for the base. That is not verified. The camp pin duplicates Cockscomb's "Heliotrope Ridge Camp" and differs from Coleman-Deming's Hogsback pin.

## Cross-route
- The fork on Trail 677 is described consistently everywhere: the climbers' path goes RIGHT up the Hogsback, and the Heliotrope Ridge trail continues left to the overlook. The online source confirms this.

<!-- b070.report.md -->
# b070 report

## wa_mount_baker_park_glacier_headwall (2 of 2 written)
- [0] Drive via WA-542 to Artist Point (pin matches Artist Point). [1] Artist Point to Grant Peak via Ptarmigan Ridge, Portals, Rainbow and Park Glaciers, then the headwall (taken from the on-file approach, variant and itinerary).
- Defects: `approach` says "Southeast via Chain Lakes Trail", but Artist Point is NORTHEAST of Baker. The approach puts Camp Kiser at ~7,000 ft while the variant notes calls that elevation suspect. Day-1 miles are 4.5 in the itinerary and ~5 to Camp Kiser in the variant. A 2-pin array has no Portals camp pin, though the route is a 2-day trip with a camp there.

## wa_mount_baker_squak_glacier (drive-only, 1 of 3 written)
- [0] Drive via SR-20 MP 82, FS 11, FS 12 and FS 13 to Schriebers Meadow; mileages checked online. Pin 48.7068,-121.8129 matches the trailhead.
- Defect: the **Crag View Camp pin (48.797, -121.875) is wrong**. It sits NORTHWEST of Baker's summit, on the Heliotrope/Coleman side, about 6 mi from where Crag View should be. Crag View (Pt 6513) is on the south/southeast flank above the Scott Paul Trail, south of the summit (48.777). The camp pin is north of the summit while the trailhead is south of it, so the pin contradicts the route's own prose. This is probably why the route fails the ordering gate.

## wa_mount_blum_north_ridge (3 of 3 written)
- [0] Drive on Baker Lake Road to the Baker River Trailhead. [1] Baker River Trail, suspension bridge and the climbers' path 175 yd downstream (confirmed online: the path is on the far bank, before the Blum Creek stringer bridge), up Blum Creek Ridge to the lakes. [2] Northernmost lake, then the NW shoulder, the glacial basin, the moat gully and the ridge to the summit.
- Note: `approach_logistics` is null on this row. The Blum Lakes pin is the MIDDLE lake (same coordinates as the South Ridge camp), while this route uses the northernmost lake; the leg is worded from the basin. `permit` is null although overnight stays need an NOCA backcountry permit, as the South Ridge row says.

## wa_mount_blum_south_ridge (7 of 8 written; [0] already had text)
- All legs come from on-file prose plus online confirmation of the bridge and path geometry.
- Defects: the [4] pin is 4,900 ft while the prose says the south-side contour is around 5,100 ft, and its note says "drop easily" into a basin at 5,003 ft. These are minor. The [0] directions say "roughly 774 feet" while the pin elevation is 800. Waypoint [3] is typed Hazard but is a route segment; it is unpinned, as are [2] and [4].

## wa_mount_buckindy_scramble (0 written; [0] already had text)
- All legs were left blank because of a **conflict between the pins and the prose**. The waypoints describe a valley route: the roadbed to its end at 2 mi, then a Kindy Creek crossing, then a "Kindy Creek Cirque" at 5 mi in one day. The on-file `approach` and variant describe the open northern approach differently: a climbers' trail up KINDY RIDGE to a ~4,800 ft saddle south of Pt 5116 (the Snowking start), then south through the Buckindy high country. That is a 2-3 day approach, with camp a second or third night out. The two do not agree on the line, and nothing credible was found supporting a Kindy Creek valley route.
- More defects: the approach puts the pullout "about 2 miles up" FR 1570, but the [1] "road end" is another 2 mi of walking. The existing [0] text carries a dated closure claim (FR 26 closure order "until January 1, 2028") that will go stale. "Kindy/Goat Creek Glacier" is unverified naming. Pins [1]-[5] have no coordinates.

## wa_mount_challenger_challenger_glacier (0 written; [0] already had text)
- **This is a spliced array**, so every leg was left blank. Index 3 is the Chilliwack ford and Easy Ridge departure, but index 4 is Whatcom Pass, which is the other approach. Index 5 (Perfect Pass) has a note describing arrival off Easy Ridge. The array mixes the Easy Ridge and Whatcom Pass approaches.
- More defects: the [0] pin (-121.5927) and `approach_logistics.trailheadLng` (-121.5894) differ by about 250 m, which is minor. The [5] Perfect Pass note says "descending roughly 1,000 ft off Easy Ridge around the Imperfect Impasse", while the variant describes an ~800 ft drop to ~4,400 ft at "the Impasse". The itinerary gives Perfect Pass as 6,300 ft and the pin gives 6,200. The itinerary also says "About 11 hours car-to-camp round trip", which should be camp-to-camp. `peakLat` 48.8365 differs from the summit pin 48.8356.

<!-- b071.report.md -->
# b071 report

## wa_mount_chaval_scramble — 1 written (leg 1); 0 pre-filled; 2, 3 blank
- Leg 1 (TH -> cliff/gully band) is supported by the on-file prose and an independent trip report (south side of Illabot Creek, turn up at the first major side creek, trend SE, cliffs ~4,300 ft) and matches the pins (SE of TH). The on-file "cross to the north side of the drainage" detail was left out: the independent account does not cross Illabot Creek.
- Legs 2 and 3 left blank because the prose contradicts the pins. Pin 2 "Base of west face ramp" (48.404, -121.2996, 5,920 ft) is about 3.6 km NE of the summit (48.376, -121.325, which is correct per peak lists). 5,920 ft is the elevation the baseFinding gives to the ridge PASS, which comes before the ramp, so the pin is misnamed or misplaced. From there the baseFinding says "cross to the north side of that ridge and traverse east" to the west face, but the summit lies SW of every pin, so "east" heads away from it.
- Pin 1 sits only about 0.65 km from the TH, yet its distMi is 1.5 and it is 1,800 ft higher.
- The pre-filled index-0 text says the path follows Illabot Creek. One trip report says the fisherman's path parallels Arrow Creek. Not resolved; flagging only.
- Itinerary day 1 says "old logging road, then fisherman's trail", while the other fields say the path leaves the road directly. Minor.
- The permit field names the Glacier Peak Wilderness. That is plausible but was not checked.

## wa_mount_custer_standard — 7 written (legs 1-7); 0 left blank; index 0 pre-filled
- Pin 2 (border marker) has elev 3,600 ft. Trip reports put the border about 500 ft above a 2,350-ft start, so it is probably about 2,800-2,900 ft.
- The approach puts the Depot Creek Falls headwall at ~4,800 ft, but the falls pin is at 3,800 ft. A trip report gives ~1,600 ft of gain from about 2,350 ft to the base of the falls, which supports the pin. No elevation was written into the leg.
- Distances to the border conflict: the approach says "200 yards" from the trailhead, while the itinerary says "roughly a mile in" from the car. They measure from different starting points, so no distance was written.
- Itinerary day 2 describes a different line ("upper basin below Custer's west ridge ... gain the west ridge crest, traverse right/east to the summit"). That contradicts the approach, climbing_route, descent and pins, which all agree on the notch south of the summit, the crux gully and the South Ridge. The legs follow the majority. The itinerary needs fixing.
- route_breakdown[0] says "3,500-6,000 ft elevation gain", a wide and odd range.

## wa_mount_despair_east_route — 1 written (index 0, the drive); legs 1-7 blank
- **Pin geometry is scrambled against the prose and the real map.** Mount Triumph is at about 48.706, -121.355.
  - Pin 3 "Thornton Pass (camp knob)" (48.7155) is NORTH of Triumph and of the Triumph Pass pin. In the prose, the camp is south of Thornton Pass near Thornton Lakes, and you contour north to reach Triumph Pass.
  - Pin 2 "Thornton Ridge" at 5,300 ft sits about 450 m south of Triumph's 7,270-ft summit.
  - Pin 5 "Lake Regret" (48.6955, -121.3504) is about 1.5 km SOUTH of Triumph Pass, in the Thornton Lakes basin. The prose reaches it by descending a NORTH-facing snowfield beyond the pass.
- The pin 1 note says the trail crosses Thornton Creek in the 3,800-ft hanging meadow and that you leave the trail there. The approach says the creek crossing comes about 1 mile in, low down, and the hanging meadow is higher up off-trail. Because of this conflict, leg 1 is also blank.
- Permit: the TH note says "self-issue wilderness permit". The permit field says an NCNP backcountry permit, reserved. The itinerary.cal says "No permits needed". These three disagree.
- The itinerary's last schedule line reads "~18 hrs car-to-car... camp-to-camp." That is pipeline text a climber would see.

## wa_mount_fury_east_mongo_ridge — 1 written (index 0); leg 1 blank
- Leg 1 (TH -> West Fury summit) would have to compress a 5-day expedition into one card, and the prose contradicts itself on the key step. The approach says "from East Fury's summit, descend NORTH along the connecting ridge and down to the base of the Southwest Buttress". The variants, baseFinding and itinerary say descend into the upper Goodell Creek basin, which is to the south/southwest. Left blank.
- approach_logistics is empty, and the trailhead distMi is null.
- The TH note says "~7mi walk around Ross Lake to Big Beaver Trailhead". No other field mentions a walking alternative.

## wa_mount_fury_east_southeast_glaciers — 7 written (legs 1-7); index 0 pre-filled
- Luna Camp mileage conflicts. The pin 3 note says Luna Camp is "about 11.5 mi up the trail from the landing" (and distMi 17.4 - 5.9 = 11.5). The approach says the Big Beaver Trail runs 11.5 mi in total and passes Luna Camp at about the 4.7-hour mark, which puts Luna Camp before 11.5 mi. No mileage was written.
- The Big Beaver crossing conflicts. The pin 4 note says an unbridged ford that can run thigh-deep. The approach says a large (~15-inch) log crossing. The leg says "cross on a log or expect a cold ford".
- Access Creek gain conflicts. The pin 5 note says ~1,500 ft; the approach says 1,900 ft (2,400 -> 4,300); the pins say 2,600 -> 4,300 = 1,700 ft. No number was written.
- Luna Pass at 7,200 ft (pin 6 note) versus the approach's "3-4 miles of heather" from a 6,100-ft col. That mileage seems long and was omitted.
- The pre-filled index 0 and the TH note claim a Northwest Forest Pass is required at Ross Dam Trailhead. The resort's directions page does not mention one, so this is unverified and possibly wrong for a Ross Lake NRA lot. Worth checking.
- Water taxi detail written from the resort's own directions: at the gravel road turn right (left crosses the dam), and the Resort Phone post is on the left.

## wa_mount_goode_northeast_buttress — 1 written (index 0, drive-only)
- The approach field reads "South-southeast via PCT descent, then east up North Fork Bridge Creek". North Fork Bridge Creek runs WEST/northwest from the PCT junction toward Goode, and the pins agree (the junction is at -120.85, camp at -120.90). "East" is wrong.
- Pin 5 "SW Couloir Exit" (48.485, -120.875) is about 2.6 km EAST of the summit (-120.9109). A southwest couloir exit cannot be east of the peak, so the pin is misplaced.
- Pin 4 "Black Tooth Notch" (48.48, -120.91) has only 2-decimal coordinates and looks like a placeholder.
- The permit field is empty, though the itinerary.cal mentions an NPS backcountry permit.

<!-- b072.report.md -->
# b072 report

## wa_mount_herman_standard_scramble (drive-only, 7 pins) — 1 written
- Index 0 drive written (SR 542 about 56 mi from Bellingham, then the Bagley Lakes lot at Heather Meadows, NW Forest Pass). Indexes 1-6 were left blank because the mode is drive-only.
- Defect: the waypoint at index 2 is labelled as being on the Chain Lakes switchbacks toward Herman Saddle, but its distance and coordinates put it before the saddle, while index 1 (Bagley Lakes, 48.854) sits south of the trailhead and of index 2. The pin order zigzags, which is consistent with the ordering gate failing.

## wa_mount_logan_fremont_glacier (7 pins) — 7 written
- All entries written. Online sources confirm Junction Camp is at the Fisher Creek Trail junction, about 10 mi in.
- Defect: `approach` says "Follow Thunder Creek Trail **north**" from Colonial Creek. The trail runs **south** (the pins go from 48.69 down to 48.51). My text says south.
- Defect: the trailhead note says a "Self-issue wilderness permit required". Overnight stays in North Cascades NP need a backcountry permit from Recreation.gov or the Wilderness Information Center, not a self-issue one.
- Defect: the variant's baseFinding says the Douglas Glacier route comes "from Easy Pass and the Douglas Creek alder on the **west**". Easy Pass, Fisher Basin and Douglas Creek all lie east and northeast of Logan, and the Douglas Glacier pin in wa_mount_logan_r2 (-120.934) sits east of the summit (-120.952).
- Minor: the pin is named "Point 8248" at 8,248 ft, but the prose puts the saddle camp at about 8,000-8,250 ft.

## wa_mount_logan_r1 (2 pins: trailhead, summit) — 2 written
- Drive written, plus one leg that summarises the whole route: Thunder Creek, then Fisher Creek, then the drainage to the lake camp, Christmas Tree Col, the Banded Glacier, the Banded-Douglas Col, the NE ridge and the summit block. Online sources confirm the "9 mi + 3 mi, past the log bridge with a handrail, then up the lake's drainage" description. I used about 10 mi, because Junction Camp, at the Fisher Creek junction, is about 10 mi in.
- Defect: the row has only 2 pins. There is no pin for the lake camp or the cols.
- Defect: the trailhead and summit notes are empty or undefined.
- The itinerary is written for the Easy Pass start, but the pins use the Thunder Creek trailhead. Both starts reach the same camp, so this is not a conflict for the leg.
- Note: online trip reports say the lake is larger than earlier reports describe, and camping at it may not work.

## wa_mount_logan_r2 (3 pins) — 2 written (index 0 already had text)
- Index 0 was left blank because it already had text. Legs 1 and 2 are written from the row's own prose.
- Defect: the Douglas Glacier pin has no elevation, a null distMi and no note, and the summit pin has distMi 0. The distance chain is broken.
- Defect: the baseFinding says this route comes "from Easy Pass and the Douglas Creek drainage on the **west**". It comes from the east (see above).
- Defect: the itinerary (days 1-2) differs from the approach. The itinerary has an off-trail descent to a camp at about 4,800 ft, a rock ramp of about 300 ft "above camp", and a boot-pack of the final 1,000 ft. The approach has an alder bushwhack, a gully to slabs, the Douglas Glacier, then a traverse to the Banded. My text uses only the approach and variant prose.
- Minor: the trailhead pin (48.5872, -120.8016) and approach_logistics (48.5879, -120.8029) differ by about 100 m.

## wa_mount_pilchuck_standard_route (2 pins) — 2 written
- Drive confirmed online: Mountain Loop Highway about 10 mi to Verlot, then about 1 mi on, then right onto FR 42 for 6.8 mi. Online sources say about 4.7 mi of the road is gravel and the last 1.9 mi is paved. NW Forest Pass or America the Beautiful pass.
- Defect: the `approach` says FR 42 is "a mix of paved and rough" and the trailhead is at "roughly 3,000-3,100 ft", while the pin says 3,000. This is minor.
- Defect: both pins have undefined notes.

## wa_mount_prophet_east (8 pins) — 8 written
- All entries written. Online sources confirm the Ross Dam leg: 0.8 mi down to a service road, left, then right to the dam, cross it, then about 6 mi along the west shore to Big Beaver Creek.
- Defect: the "Off-trail departure" pin (index 3) has **null lat/lng**. Its elevation of 2,500 ft also conflicts with a departure from the valley trail near the camp at 1,720 ft. The pin is meant to sit between the creek crossing and the camp post, at the trail's elevation.
- Defect: the trailhead note says a "self-issue NPS wilderness permit required well ahead". That contradicts itself, and overnight stays need a reserved or walk-up backcountry permit.
- Minor: the itinerary puts the high camp "near 6,700 ft", but the saddle camp pin is at 6,600.

<!-- b073.report.md -->
# b073 report

## wa_mount_prophet_southwest_rib — 2/2 written
- [0] The drive to Ross Dam TH, near SR-20 milepost 134 (land-manager page confirms the MP, the 0.8 mi descent, the west-shore route and the backcountry permit for overnight stays).
- [1] TH to summit, a single leg covering the approach to Luna Camp and the rib, written from route_breakdown.
- Defect: the array has only 2 pins for a 2-3 day route. There is no Luna Camp pin, which is the base camp, and no pins at the 5,450 ft crux, the 7,420 ft saddle or the cliff band, so leg 1 has to carry about 20 miles in one sentence. The route is worth adding pins for.

## wa_mount_pugh_pika_slab — 2/3 written
- [0] Already had text, so it was left "".
- [1] Lake Metan and [2] Stujack Pass written.
- Defect: the array ends at Stujack Pass. The route's objective, the Pika Slab crag band above the Class 3 step 30-45 min above the pass, has no pin.
- Minor: the drive says FR-2095 runs about 1.5 mi to the trailhead, but the sibling route wa_mount_pugh_stujack says "about a mile". The Lake Metan pin (48.1375,-121.3962) is about 350 m from the sibling route's Lake Metan pin (48.1392,-121.3997).

## wa_mount_pugh_stujack — 3/4 written
- [0] Already had text, so it was left "".
- [1], [2] and [3] written. The summit pin is SE of the pass, which matches the description (left out of the notch, then the east side of the ridge).
- Minor: Lake Metan is typed "Junction". The summit pin reads 7,207.8 ft while the itinerary says 7,201 ft. The FR-2095 distance conflicts with the Pika Slab row, as noted above.

## wa_mount_saul_se_route — 6/7 written
- [0] Already had text, so it was left "".
- [1] to [6] written. The order is sequential and the distances are consistent: TH to the lake is about 4 mi in a straight line, against 6 mi on the trail plus the bushwhack.
- Defect: pins 1-3 (bridge, leave-trail point, snow line) have null lat/lng.
- Defect: the note on pin 5 (base of the upper east face, 6,200 ft) says three lines diverge there, including the northeast ridge. But the approach and baseFinding say the NE ridge is reached by working around a hidden cliff band NORTH OF THE LAKE, so that line splits off at the lake, not at 6,200 ft. Leg 6 therefore names only the direct slabs.
- Minor: day 1 of the itinerary says 2,200 ft over 1.5 mi for the bushwhack, while the approach says about 2,000 ft. The approach says "Indian Creek bridge" without saying where that bridge is, so I did not write a crossing of Indian Creek.

## wa_mount_sefrit_bloody_head_couloir — 1/1 written (drive-only)
- [0] SR-542 for about 13 mi past Glacier, then FR-32 for 5.3 mi to the road end at about 3,120 ft (confirmed online). This matches the pin and the trailhead named in approach_logistics.
- Note: the array has only the trailhead pin. There are no pins for the 3,450 ft leave-trail point or the 4,000 ft couloir base.

## wa_mount_sefrit_southwest_ridge — 5/6 written
- [0] Already had text, so it was left "".
- [1] to [5] written from the approach, the notes and baseFinding. Pins 3-5 run east toward the summit, which is consistent.
- DEFECT: route_breakdown does not describe this route. Entry 3 says "Final 100-150 feet of exposed Class 3 traversing, mixed rock and snow; cornice hazard near summit". That is the Wall Street / NW Glacier line ("Class 2 with one exposed ~100 ft Class 3 traverse"), not this route's Class 4 gully. Entry 2 ("occasional steep snow couloirs") also fits the north side. The breakdown appears to belong to a sibling route and contradicts the route's own grade (Class 4).
- Defect: the distMi values are impossible. The leave-trail point is at 1.7 mi and 2,700 ft, the traverse at 2.0 mi and 3,800 ft, and the meadows at 2.4 mi and 5,800 ft: about 3,100 ft of gain in 0.7 mi, mostly brushy traverse. Separately, the TH-to-meadows straight line alone is about 2.2 mi. I did not use the mileages in the legs.
- Suspicious: the coordinates on pins 3-4 have long repeating fractions (…37931, /29), which suggests they were averaged or synthesized rather than taken from a source. Pins 1-2 have null lat/lng.
- Minor: the note on pin 3 refers to "the parallel south-side route off this trailhead", which matches no other Sefrit route; both siblings are on the north side, reached from Ruth Creek.

<!-- b074.report.md -->
# b074 report

Ground elevations were read from USGS EPQS (3DEP) at each pin.

## wa_mount_shuksan_fisher_chimneys: 2 written (idx 1, 2)
- idx 0 already had text, so I left it alone.
- DEFECT: the pin for idx 1 "White Salmon Glacier Lower Bivouac" (48.812, -121.625) is in the wrong place. The ground there is 5,602 ft, but the card claims 6,700 ft. The pin also sits about 2 km SOUTH of the Lake Ann area. The real bivy at the top of the chimneys is north-east of Lake Ann, below Winnie's Slide. I wrote the leg to the named bivy because all the row's prose agrees about it. The pin should be moved.

## wa_mount_shuksan_hanging_glacier: 0 written
- idx 0 already had text.
- DEFECT (broken array): idx 1 "Hanging Glacier" (Junction) and idx 2 "Hanging Glacier" (Feature) have the same coordinates (48.8353, -121.6019, ground 8,169 ft). A 0-length duplicate leg means the array is broken, so I left every leg blank.
- DEFECT (two approaches spliced): the trailhead, approach and approach_variants all use the White Salmon Creek side. The itinerary (days 1 and 3) uses the Lake Ann / Fisher Chimneys approach instead. descent_text says the route goes back out to the White Salmon trailhead.

## wa_mount_shuksan_northwest_arete: 2 written (idx 0 drive, idx 1)
- DEFECT / conflict: two pins in the catalog are named for the same White Salmon hairpin trailhead, and they differ.
  - This route's pin is 48.8595, -121.648, with ground at 3,267 ft. That is about 0.8 km east of the hairpin and about 300 ft lower, probably partway down the old road.
  - The white_salmon_glacier route's pin is 48.8589, -121.659, with ground at 3,573 ft. That matches the ~3,500 ft hairpin.
  - The drive I wrote goes to the hairpin and the start of FS 3075. That is the trailhead the card names.
- The route has no descent_text or route_breakdown. The idx 1 leg sums up the climb from the approach prose only.

## wa_mount_shuksan_sulphide_glacier: 3 written (idx 1-3)
- idx 0 already had text.
- DEFECT: the camp pin for idx 2 (48.79, -121.595) has ground at 4,952 ft, but the card claims 6,245 ft. That is about 1,300 ft too low, roughly at the level of the Shannon Ridge crest and below the ~5,400 ft pass. The camps the prose describes are at about 6,200 and 6,500 ft, further north. I wrote the leg to the named camp from the consistent prose. The pin needs moving.
- Minor: the timberline junction pin has ground at 4,335 ft against the 4,600 ft claimed. Its distMi of 2 is also low compared with the prose (about 2.75 mi).

## wa_mount_shuksan_white_salmon_glacier: 1 written (idx 0 drive only)
- DEFECT (ordering): idx 5 "White Salmon Glacier" (Feature) comes AFTER the summit, but a party crosses the glacier before it. Because the order is broken, I left all legs blank.
- DEFECT: the "Hell's Highway" pin (48.835, -121.606) is NORTH of the summit (48.8314). Hell's Highway leads from the Upper Curtis onto the Sulphide Glacier, on the south-west side of the summit pyramid, so this pin looks misplaced. Its ground is 7,479 ft against the 7,800 ft claimed.
- Minor: the "White Salmon Glacier toe" pin is typed "Water", and its ground is 4,890 ft against the 5,200 ft claimed.
- The drive matches the gate pin, which sits at 3,573 ft (the hairpin).

## wa_mount_spickard_silver_glacier: 1 written (idx 0, the approach to Silver Lake camp via the Ross Lake water taxi and Silver Creek)
- DEFECT (two approaches spliced): the array runs Silver Lake camp -> Custer-Spickard saddle -> summit. Published route descriptions put the wide gully to the 7,380 ft Custer-Spickard saddle on the OUZEL LAKE (Depot Creek) approach, climbing north-east from Ouzel Lake. From Silver Lake, parties instead go round the north side of the lake to its south-west end, then head south over moraine to the glacier toe at about 7,400 ft, and there is no saddle on that line. The row's approach, baseFinding and route_breakdown all wrongly send Silver Lake parties up "the wide gully on the saddle's north side". I left the legs blank.
- Also: approach_logistics names the Depot Creek (BC) trailhead, while idx 0 is the Silver Lake camp that the Ross Lake / Silver Creek variant reaches. The pins for idx 0 and idx 1 have no coordinates, and the grade is null.

## wa_mount_stickney_scramble (7 wps) — 6 written (idx 1-6)

The approach prose gives ~6 mi to the lake, but the summit distMi is 6.5 and the itinerary says 13.5 mi round trip, so the lake figure is too high for its slot. The gully pin (47.9094,-121.6208) sits slightly WEST of the lake pin, while the summit is to the SE. That is plausible for the "gully just west of the summit hump", but the coordinates are rough.

## wa_mount_terror_north_face (7 wps) — 6 written (idx 1-6)
- idx 0 already had text.
- DEFECT (pin): idx 3, "~6,200' notch above Terror Basin", is pinned at 48.770167,-121.304239, about 0.3 mi SSW of Terror's summit (48.7743,-121.2996). That is on Terror's south flank above Crescent Creek Basin, not the Terror Basin saddle several miles south. The pin is wrong. My leg text follows the prose and states no compass direction.
- DEFECT (note vs approach): the idx 1 card is named "Terror Creek cairned crossing", and its note says the cairn marks a "log crossing of Terror Creek". The route's own approach says to take the HARD RIGHT at the cairn up the Terror Basin path, NOT the Terror Creek track, which is a separate approach to Crescent Creek Basin. Online sources tie the logjam crossing of Terror Creek to that Crescent Creek line. My leg 1 ends at the cairn and mentions no crossing. The note probably conflates the two approaches.
- Distance mismatch: idx 1 distMi is 3.5, but the approach prose says 4-4.5 mi to the cairn. I put no mileage in leg 1.
- Leg 5 (camp → crux) compresses a whole day (the Barrier, Crescent Creek Basin, the Otto-Himmel col, the Mustard Glacier, the lower face) because there are no intermediate waypoints. The array is missing a Crescent Creek Basin camp pin and a base-of-face pin.
- idx 0 directions name the "Upper Goodell Creek Group Camp" lot, but the pin (48.68276,-121.26928) differs by ~450 m from approach_logistics.trailheadLat/Lng (48.68664,-121.27121).

## wa_mount_terror_southeast_face (2 wps) — 2 written
- DEFECT (identity): the route is NAMED "Southeast Face", but every on-file field (wp0 note, approach_variants baseFinding, itinerary day 3, route_breakdown) describes the EAST RIDGE from the Terror-Degenhardt saddle. Either the name or the content is wrong. My leg avoids naming either and says "four-pitch ridge".
- DEFECT (itinerary vs approach): itinerary day 1 says "the Terror Creek climber's path — brushy... with a creek crossing — up to a bench camp around 6,000 ft". The approach says the Terror Basin climbers' path to a 5,800 ft camp. I used only what both agree on (the cairned climbers' path to a Terror Basin camp).
- The wp0 pin (48.6828,-121.2693) differs from approach_logistics (48.68664,-121.27121) by ~450 m. Both are at the Goodell Creek group camp area, so I wrote the drive.
- The grade on file is "Grade II, 5.6", but baseFinding says "Grade III-IV 5.6".

- The approach says "Same as the North Buttress" with high camp in Crescent Creek Basin, but it does not say which path leads into the basin (the Terror Basin plus Barrier route or the Terror Creek route). My leg does not name one.

- This row's approach (cross Terror Creek, flagged track straight into Crescent Creek Basin) is a different line from the north-face/SE rows (Terror Basin path, then over the Barrier). That is not a contradiction within the row; it just shows the two approaches exist.

# b077 report

## wa_neve_glacier_west_ridge (Snowfield Peak), 2/2 written
- Index 0: the drive to the Pyramid Lake TH at SR-20 milepost 126.8. Confirmed online: no parking pass is needed, and you park on the north side with the trail across the road.
- Index 1: the whole approach plus the climb, condensed from the row's own approach, baseFinding and climbing_route.
- Defects:
  - The WP0 note says "Pyramid-Colonial col". All the prose says Colonial-Neve col.
  - The WP0 note and approach_logistics say 6.4 mi east of Newhalem; approach says "about 7 miles".
  - approach_variants.baseFinding says you leave the ice near **7,700 ft** and top out on the ridge 300-400 ft below the summit. The itinerary and climbing_route say you leave the snow at **~8,200 ft**, which is only ~150 ft below the 8,351 ft summit. I left the elevation out of the leg.

## wa_news_nw_corner (North Early Winters Spire), 2/2 written
- Index 0: the Blue Lake TH drive. Index 1: the approach plus a pitch summary from the prose.
- Defect: approach_variants puts the trailhead at ~5,200 ft; every other field says ~5,400 ft.

## wa_nooksack_tower_beckey_route (Nooksack Tower N Face), 5 of 6 empty slots written; legs 6 and 7 blank; index 0 already had text, so I skipped it
- Legs 1-5 written: TH to the off-trail ford, ford to Price Lake, lake to the 5,900 ft bivy, bivy to the Price Glacier crossing (prose saddle step), and crossing to the couloir.
- Legs 6 and 7 are blank: "Nooksack Bivouac saddle" (7,900 ft) and the leg to the summit. No prose puts a saddle on the line. The saddle pin sits NW of the summit, not between the tower and the ridge toward Shuksan as its note says. Its elevations also don't fit the prose: 6,500 ft schrund + 800 ft couloir + ~10 pitches / ~1,200 ft of 4th class should not leave only 385 ft from saddle to summit, and the "steep gully" pin is at 7,300 ft.
- **DEFECT (existing WP0 directions, and approach + approach_variants):** they call the turn before the bridge "Nooksack River Road (Forest Service Road 32)". Online it is Hannegan Pass Road #32; Nooksack Cirque Road #34 forks right off it after about a mile and ends at Ruth Creek. approach_logistics has it right ("FR-34 off Hannegan Pass Road (FR-32)"). The existing WP0 text should be corrected.
- Defect, geometry: the prose has the bivy on the "ridgeline between the Price and East Nooksack Glaciers". The bivy pin (-121.6077) is west of the Price Glacier crossing pin and well west of the tower, but the East Nooksack Glacier lies east of the tower. Either the prose names the wrong glacier pair or the pin is off.
- Defect, possibly a misplaced pin: Price Lake (3,911 ft) to the bivy (5,900 ft) is ~0.35 mi horizontally for 2,000 ft of gain, very steep. The WP2 note also has the boot path going up the lake's *north* side, but the bivy is SSW of the lake. I left side-of-lake wording out of leg 3.
- The prose's step of dropping off a saddle onto the Price Glacier main lobe has no pin. The pins climb steadily.

## wa_nooksack_tower_south_face, 2/2 written
- Index 0: the drive, written with the correct road numbers (32 then 34, to the road end at Ruth Creek, no pass). Confirmed online.
- Index 1: the approach plus the route, condensed from approach_variants.
- WP0 and WP1 have no elevation or distMi.
- The same East Nooksack/Price glacier-geometry doubt applies as on the Beckey route above. The leg only repeats the row's own "traverse onto the East Nooksack Glacier" decision.

## wa_north_face_3 (Lexington Tower N Face), 5/5 legs written; index 0 already had text
- **DEFECT:** approach_variants says "trend **left** of the line most parties take toward the Liberty Bell-Concord notch" to reach the Concord-Lexington gully. Lexington is south of Concord, and the pins put the Concord-Lexington gully south (climber's right) of the Liberty Bell-Concord gully, so "left" looks reversed. The "boot path along its left side" may also be suspect. Leg 2 names the target gully without a left/right.

# b078 report

## wa_north_ridge_2 (Whatcom Peak, North Ridge): 2 written (legs 1, 2)
- Index 0 already had text, so it was left "".
- Defect: the `approach` / primary `approach_variants` prose says to "turn up the Brush Creek Trail junction and climb through US Cabin Camp, Graybeal Camp and Whatcom Camp". The real order is US Cabin Camp, then the cable car or ford of the Chilliwack, then the Brush Creek junction (~mile 11.9), then Graybeal. US Cabin is on the Chilliwack, below the junction. The leg I wrote uses the correct order.

It is not at the North Ridge Notch or the basin. A leg to it cannot arrive where the card names, and a leg from it to the summit would be meaningless, so both legs are left "". The pin also has no elevation and no note.

- Leg 1 runs from the trailhead to the summit through McAllister Camp, the Thunder Creek ford or log, the ridge, the Borealis toe bivy, the lake and the glacier, the ridge, and the summit.
- Inconsistency: `approach` says "past McAllister and Tricouni Camps, up Lucky Ridge", while the itinerary and the online sources leave the trail at McAllister Camp, fording or log-crossing Thunder Creek there. I wrote the McAllister version, which the sources agree on, and did not name Tricouni.

## wa_north_ridge_7 (Aiguille de l'M, North Ridge): 1 written (drive)
- Leg 1 is left "". The waypoint is "Summit Aiguille de l'M", but the row's own baseFinding says the route ends on a subsidiary tower and most parties deliberately do not stand on the true high point. A leg would not arrive at the point the card names.

The Eldorado (Eldorado Creek) trailhead is DOWN-road, at about mile 20, below the Boston Basin pullout at about mile 22-23. The drive text I wrote leaves Eldorado out.

## wa_north_ridge_west_side (Molar Tooth, North Ridge - West Side): 2 written (drive, leg 1)
- The pins agree with the PCT/Porcupine Creek line (the summit is NE of the trailhead).
- Defect: the Cutthroat Lake variant's bivy notes ("Cutthroat Lake basin...") talk about Cutthroat Peak's south-side routes and "the usual North Ridge approach" from the SR 20 pullout. That text was copied from the Cutthroat Peak row and is about a different peak.

## wa_north_twin_sister_scramble (North Twin Sister): 3 written (legs 1, 2, 5)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Legs 3 (fire ring to "Katie Gap") and 4 (Katie Gap to the headwall) are left "". "Katie Gap" is a name one party gave in a single 2024 trip report: a notch at about 6,300 ft that they found when their cairned line on the LEFT of the ridge ran into cliffs. The waypoint note calls it "the route's well-known routefinding crux" and says climbers "drop through" it. The source says they went "up and over the ridge through a notch". The pin has no coordinates. It is not part of the standard line in the row's own climbing_route (crest, then right of the crest to the headwall, then over the false summit). I could not confidently place it or describe the legs on either side of it.
- Note defect: the waypoint 2 note calls the fire ring "treeline near 4,100 ft". The approach prose puts the fire ring in the clearcut, followed by a band of brush and then TIMBER at the base of the west ridge, so the fire ring is below the timber, not at treeline.
- The existing index-0 drive text reads as two different Middle Fork bridges merged into one ("Drive Mosquito Lake Road ... to the bridge over the Middle Fork ... then continue on Forest Road 38. Park at the yellow gate just before the bridge"). It does not match the approach prose (Road 38 west, past the Clearwater Creek crossing at about 4.5 mi, right fork down to the gate). I did not overwrite it.

<!-- b079.report.md -->
# b079 report

## wa_north_twin_sister_west_ridge (2/2 written)
- 0: drive via SR 542 → Mosquito Lake Rd → Middle Fork Rd (Road 38) → gated bridge (mileages from published directions; pin 48.7669,-122.0394 at ~1,200 ft agrees with the bridge elevation in trip reports). Permit sentence kept generic (landowner recreation permit), matching the row's own permit field.
- 1: logging roads → climbers' trail → ridge crest → summit.
- Defect (minor): `approach` says Mosquito Lake Road "from Acme/Welcome", but the commonly given drive comes in from SR 542 at Welcome; the Acme approach is the alternative from the south. Not contradictory, just ambiguous. Landowner is now Weyerhaeuser per recent reports; the row's permit text does not name one (fine).
- Descent text has the glissade rejoining the trail "near 4,600 ft" while route_breakdown puts the end of the roads at 3,200-3,700 ft and reports put the ridge at ~4,100 ft. Not a contradiction, noted only.

## wa_northeast_face_direct (2/2 written)
- 0: Cascade River Road drive from Marblemount (23 mi).
- 1: the full Cascade Pass → Cache Col → Kool-Aid Lake → Red Ledge → Middle Cascade Glacier → Formidable Glacier → NE face line, ending at the summit card.
- Defect: `itinerary` says the descent is "via the South Route back to camp", but `descent_text` says descend to the Spider-Formidable col and recross the Middle Cascade Glacier and Red Ledge to Kool-Aid Lake, and `approach`/variant say the South Route from Yang Yang Lakes is the descent. Three fields describe the descent differently.
- Only 2 waypoints for a multi-day, 7-mile glaciated route: no Cascade Pass, Cache Col or Kool-Aid Lake pins.

## wa_northeast_ridge_1963_route (2 of 4 empty slots written; index 0 already had text)
- 1: back down road, embankment, ford of the river. 2: moraine and snowfield to the couloir foot.
- 3 (couloir base → Hanging Glacier Traverse) left "": CONFLICT. Waypoint 2's note says "Gain rock just before the C-J couloir narrows; the NE Ridge (1963) climbs the rib right of the couloir". The approach variant's baseFinding says the opposite: "stay in the couloir. Do not break out onto rock low down... The Northeast Rib lines leave the couloir far lower, before it narrows". It also says to climb until the hanging glacier appears, then take the leftmost rib. The note describes what the variant calls the wrong route.
- 4 (→ summit) left "" because it depends on the contested rib choice.
- Defects: (a) the waypoint 2 note contradicts the variant, as above. (b) The "Base of C-J Couloir" pin is at 5,200 ft, but the prose says serious ground starts around 3,500 ft within an hour of the car. The couloir foot is likely much lower than 5,200 ft, or the pin sits partway up the couloir. (c) The "Hanging Glacier Traverse" card describes a "rising traverse beneath the hanging glaciers", while the variant has the party climb the couloir to the hanging glacier and then up the leftmost rib. The card's wording does not match the variant's line. (d) The existing index 0 text includes "this route's season is tied to it", which is meta-ish and only half a drive. It was not overwritten.

## wa_northwest_buttress (2 of 2 empty slots written; index 0 already had text)
- 1: Bedal Creek Trail #705 to the washout and the flagged climbers' path exit near 3,500 ft. This matches the variant's coordinate (48.0560,-121.3631), which is the pin.
- 2: forest → saddle → meadows → dark-rock corner → 9 pitches → hand crack → summit.
- Defect: the approach and variant give the Bedal-Sloan saddle as 5,300 ft. `descent_text` returns to the "5,800 ft saddle area" after the glacier. That is either a different saddle that is never named, or one of the two elevations is wrong.
- Descent text cites a source ("Per a documented Bedal Basin–to–Corkscrew descent account"). This is pipeline/citation voice in rendered prose.

## wa_northwest_face_2 (2/2 written)
- 0: SR 20 hairpin pullout east of Washington Pass. 1: Kangaroo Pass → Temple/Little Finger gully → bench 200 ft below the notch → 5 pitches.
- Minor: the waypoint pin (48.5143,-120.6425) and approach_logistics (48.51454,-120.64332) differ by ~80 m. The pin elevation is 5,160 ft vs ~5,100 ft in logistics. Both are minor.

- 1: Blue Lake Trail → climbers' path → basin → corner start right of Mojo Rising → 6 pitches.
