# b080 report

## wa_northwest_mox_peak_standard (2/2 written)
- [0] drive written. Chilliwack Lake Road from TCH Exit 119 (Sardis), ~32 mi to the unsigned Depot Creek Road near the south end of the lake, then 2.5 mi high-clearance. Confirmed online.
- [1] TH to summit written as one compressed leg, taken from the row's approach prose and baseFinding.
- Defect: only 2 pins for a 4-day route. There are no pins for Lake Ouzel, the Redoubt Glacier saddle high camp, or the headwall base, so the single leg has to carry the whole trip.
- Minor: the row says "31.7 miles", which is the distance to the Depot Creek turn. Another description puts the turn at 32.6 mi. I wrote "about 32".

## wa_northwest_ridge (Dorado Needle): nothing written, route omitted
- [0] already has text.
- ORDERING FAULT: [6] "Northwest Ridge approach point (NW of Dorado Needle)", a Base, comes AFTER [5], the summit. Following the rule, I left every leg blank.
- [3] "Inspiration–McAllister Glacier col" has null lat/lng.
- Suspect pin: [1] "Eldorado Creek Basin camp" is at lng -121.1689. That is about 3 km west of the trailhead (-121.1176) and of the Eldorado/Inspiration high camp (-121.125), which looks too far west for the Eldorado basin. Worth checking.
- [3]'s note says to cross "north from Inspiration over this col onto the McAllister". The prose instead goes "through/near the Tepeh Towers" saddle. These are probably the same feature, but it is unconfirmed.

## wa_northwest_ridge_2 (Boston Peak NW Ridge): 3/8 written ([1]-[3])
- [0] already has text.
- [1]-[3] cover the standard Boston Basin trail: mining road to the mine site, then the climbers' path with stream crossings. The last big creek crossing is just below lower camp. Confirmed online.
- Pin geometry defect: [2] "South Fork Boston Creek crossing" (48.4988) sits about 0.4 km NORTH of both the cabin [1] (48.4953) and the lower camp [3] (48.4948), even though its note says it is "just below the lower camps". The path cabin → creek → camp makes a V. I wrote legs [2] and [3] without any compass directions.
- [4]-[7] left blank:
  - [4] "Western edge of Quien Sabe Glacier" (48.5030, 6,400 ft) is NORTH of the 7,200 ft bergschrund pin [5] (48.4997). On a glacier that rises north toward the crest, this cannot be right, so pin [4] looks misplaced.
  - [6] "Sharkfin Col". Its note says the col is "just west of Sharkfin Tower" and that the FA "gains the crest here via a loose couloir off Sharkfin". But the couloir beneath Sharkfin Tower leads to the notch EAST of the tower, not to Sharkfin Col at the upper-left (west) corner of the glacier. The note mixes up two different gaps, and I could not tell which one [5] → [6] is meant to reach.
  - [7]: the row itself says the leg to the summit has no published description. The FA reached the ridge by climbing Sharkfin Tower, descending it, and traversing a hanging snow slope. A col-to-summit leg would be invented.
- The approach prose itself calls this "the logical way in, not an established approach".

## wa_nw_face_var_remsberg_variation (Liberty Bell): 2/2 written
- [0] drive written: Blue Lake TH on SR-20, ~1 mi west of Washington Pass, NW Forest Pass, winter highway closure.
- [1] TH to summit written as one leg: Blue Lake Trail, climbers' path, gully split, west bench, 5 pitches to the west summit, then the notch to the main summit.
- Defect: no pins for the bench/route base or the west summit, so the approach and the whole climb are packed into one card.

## wa_one_piece_at_a_time (Cutthroat Wall): 1/3 written ([1])
- [0] already has text.
- [1] TH to wall base written from the row's prose. "Uphill left" heading up-valley means south, which matches the pin.
- [2] left blank. "Cutthroat Wall" (Crag) has the SAME coordinates as [1] "Cutthroat Wall Base", so there is no leg between them. That duplicate pin is a defect.
- [1] is typed "Topout" even though it is the base of the wall: a type defect.

## wa_ottohorn_southeast_route: 5/8 written ([1], [4]-[7])
- [0] already has text.
- [1] roadbed to the cairn and stream at ~1,700 ft, mile 3.5. This matches the prose.
- [2] "Terror Creek ford" at 2,600 ft left blank. The prose puts the only stream crossing AT the cairn (~1,700 ft) and describes no ford above it, so the pin/elevation contradicts the prose.
- [3] "Forested ridge crest, toe of the Barrier" at 3,800 ft left blank. The prose treats the Barrier as the spur ridge whose crest is the 6,200 ft notch. [3]'s note instead describes a ridge crest at 3,800 ft with the Barrier as a "rock rib" crossed next, and the prose has no ridge-crest point at 3,800 ft. These are two different models of the Barrier.
- [4]: I wrote the climb to the Barrier notch at ~6,200 ft, starting from the generic "keep climbing", so it arrives at "The Barrier crossing" (6,100 ft). Note that [4]'s note calls it a "Class 3 traverse into the Terror/Crescent Creek basins", while the prose calls it a notch crest followed by a 250 ft drop to Stump Hollow.
- [6] col has null lat/lng.
- Trailhead pin (48.68276, -121.26928) and approach_logistics trailhead (48.68664, -121.27121) are ~460 m apart. [0] also has no elev.
- The day-1 itinerary mentions "loose ledges at the lower end of Terror Glacier" for crossing the Barrier. That is Terror Basin terrain, not the Crescent Creek route, and may be mixing in another approach.

<!-- b081.report.md -->
# b081 report

## wa_painted_traverse — 3/3 written
- Drive via Mountain Loop Hwy + FS 49 (6.4 mi). Legs from on-file prose plus trip reports (Painted = Red Mountain; Magenta Peak lies between Painted and Black).
- Defect: waypoints stop at Black Mountain while route_breakdown (7 stages) and descent_text continue over Skullcap, Portal and White Mountain to White Pass. The card list covers less than half the traverse.
- Note: reports disagree on the final approach to Black (south-side chute + NW ridge class 3, versus north side 4th class); leg kept to what both share. Prose says "class 4 summit block"; written as class 3-4.

## wa_phantom_peak_south_route — 1/7 (drive-only)
- Index 0: Hannegan Pass Road drive, which matches the pin.
- Defects: (1) waypoint 0's NAME is a whole approach sentence ("...via Hannegan Pass, Chilliwack River Trail, Whatcom Pass and Perfect Pass to the SW side..."). (2) Pins splice two approaches: Luna Camp and the Luna Creek bushwhack (Big Beaver) sit between the Hannegan trailhead and the Crooked Thumb moraine camp (Whatcom side). (3) The approach prose says the Hannegan option goes "Hannegan Trailhead -> Ruth Creek Trail"; the trail is the Hannegan Pass Trail (Ruth Creek is the road). (4) The recorded distance (~32 mi) reflects the Big Beaver line, but the primary trailhead/itinerary is Hannegan (~44 mi per the itinerary's totalNote). (5) Luna Camp bivy note carries a dated "closed as of August 2026" claim.

## wa_plan_9_from_outer_space — 1/4
- Index 0 written (Goodell Creek / Upper Goodell Group Camp drive).
- Legs 1-3 left blank, ordering fault: three pins share one coordinate (48.7716,-121.28356), and the order is Junction, then Summit, then "route start", so the summit comes before the start of the climb. The approach prose also calls the formation "the Blob" while the area is "Ed Wood Memorial Buttress". No leg can be written honestly.

## wa_playing_not_spraying — 2/2
- Drive from the USFS trailhead page (FR 5310-200, which matches the pin 48.5792,-120.4787). The leg follows the on-file prose; going west up Cedar Creek, a turn to the right means north, toward the Silver Horn pin. That is consistent.
- No defects noted, apart from the summit distMi of 7 mi, which includes the climb.

## wa_poltergeist_pinnacle — 4/5 (index 0 already had text)
- Legs 1-4 written from the on-file prose plus the first-ascent report (traverse around the Challenger Arm, then the dike-streaked shield).
- Possible defects: (1) The glacier-crossing pin (48.84,-121.345) lies NORTH of Challenger's summit (approach_logistics 48.8365) and the pinnacle pin lies SOUTH of it (48.8236). That fits "around the Challenger Arm", but the route is an EAST face on the Challenger-Crooked Thumb ridge, so the pinnacle pin may sit on or near Crooked Thumb. It is worth checking against the ground. Leg 4 avoids compass words for that reason. (2) The glacier pin's distMi of 14.5 conflicts with the prose, which puts Perfect Pass at ~16 mi before the glacier. (3) The itinerary says "Chilliwack River ford"; the approach just says "cross".

## wa_primus_peak_south_ridge — 2/2
- Drive: Cascade River Road ~20 mi to the Eldorado parking. Leg 1 compresses the multi-day Eldorado/Inspiration/Klawatti line into one leg, because there are only two pins; no intermediate pins exist for Klawatti Col camp or the notch.
- Minor: the prose says to cross "the Cascade River", while other descriptions call the crossing the North Fork Cascade. It is left unnamed ("the river").

<!-- b082.report.md -->
# b082 report

## wa_ptarmigan_traverse (11 waypoints): 9 written, 2 left blank
- Blank: legs 8 (Spire Point) and 9 (Itswoot Ridge camp). Waypoint 8 is typed Junction, and its note says "landmark near the Dome-Dana col", but the pin (48.3176,-121.0717, 8,264 ft) sits on Spire Point's summit. The traverse crosses the col beside Spire Point, not the summit, so a leg that ends at this card would end somewhere other than the point the card names. Leg 9 starts from that same ambiguous point.
- DEFECT: the Spire Point pin is a summit coordinate and elevation, but the waypoint is typed as a junction on the traverse line.
- DEFECT: the itinerary's Day 4 climbs Dome Peak "via Dome Glacier" from White Rock Lakes. The Dome Glacier route is normally climbed from Itswoot Ridge/Cub Lake, which is where waypoint 10 places it. The itinerary also lists Itswoot as a stop after Dome.
- Minor: descent_text says "Follow Bachelor Creek north"; Bachelor Creek drains west to Downey Creek. descent_text and approach also carry dated road-damage claims ("2025/2026 winter damage").
- Note on wp0 mentions the Downey Creek exit; it is harmless.

## wa_ragged_edge (6): 6 written
- DEFECT (direction conflict): approach_variants.baseFinding says the heather ledge "runs east across the north face" from the cairned saddle. The pins put the route start (-121.51736) and the summit (-121.51799) WEST of the saddle (-121.51451). Leg 4 was written without a compass direction.
- DEFECT: the saddle is named inconsistently. Waypoint 3 and the variants call it Vesper-Wolf, the approach prose says the col between Vesper and Sperry, and the waypoint-5 note says "Vesper-Sperry saddle".
- Minor: approach_variants says "north-face routes are not approached from the lake basin", but the same text and the approach prose both route via Vesper Lake. The Vesper Lake pin elevation is 5,028 ft, while the prose gives about 4,950 ft.
- Two trailhead coordinates differ by about 300 m (wp0 48.02478,-121.47782 vs approach_logistics 48.02389,-121.47414).

## wa_ragged_ridge (2): 2 written
- The array has only the trailhead and Mesahchie summit, so leg 1 covers the whole approach (Easy Pass, Fisher Creek, south-shoulder camp, Mesahchie-Katsuk saddle). approach is null. The rest of the traverse (Katsuk, Kimtah, Cosho) has no pins.

## wa_rampage (3): 2 written (index 0 already had text)
- Minor: Bench Camp spring has distMi 4. The straight line from the pullout is about 1.3 mi, and the prose says about 2 hours, so 4 mi looks high. The summit pin is listed at 7,900 ft while Burgundy Col is given as about 7,720 ft; I did not check this.

## wa_rapple_grapple (2): 2 written
- The itinerary objective says "Climb Rapple Grapple to the Liberty Bell-Concord notch", which is where the descent ends, not the objective. The route_breakdown pitch 2 says 5.7 cracks on a route graded 5.8.

## wa_ridge_traverse_from_east_fury (6): 5 written (index 0 already had text)
- DEFECT: the Access Creek Junction (48.76,-121.14) and Access Col (48.79,-121.2) coordinates are rounded to 2 decimal places and look synthetic.
- East Fury summit, where the route actually starts, is not a waypoint. Leg 4 (Luna Col to Loose Tower Traverse) therefore covers the whole East Fury ascent plus the descent to the saddle. The order of the array is still correct.
- The approach prose has a dated claim: a summer 2026 wildfire closure of the Big Beaver Trail. It may need re-checking.

Worth a look.

- The array also never reaches Icy Peak, which is the route's objective.
- Suspect pins:

- Waypoint 4 (the rope-up point) has null lat/lng.

- Mild prose tension: route_breakdown describes a snow traverse south from the col to the summit block, while the baseFinding describes a ridge that narrows to a class 3-4 knife edge with a false summit. Leg 7 follows route_breakdown plus the summit-block length, and does not mention the knife-edge or false-summit detail.

<!-- b084.report.md -->
# b084 report

Wrote 23 entries across 6 routes. Every index-0 entry that already had text was left "".

## wa_sahale_mountain_sahale_glacier: 3 of 4 written (legs 1-3)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Defect: the summit-block notes disagree with each other. The baseFinding says to trend right to a notch and go around the north/east side of the block. route_breakdown says the chimney is on the south side. The itinerary calls the top "class 2-3", but climbing_route says 3rd-4th plus a 5.0 move. Leg 3 states only what all of them agree on.
- route_breakdown[0] gives 1,700 ft of gain to the pass, the approach says about 1,800. Minor.

## wa_sews_sw_rib: 2 of 2 written
- The drive was confirmed against a trail-guide page: about 31.3 miles from Winthrop, on the left just under a mile past the Washington Pass Overlook, or about 42.1 miles from Newhalem. NW Forest Pass or interagency pass.
- Possible defect: the trailhead pin elevation is 5,400 ft. The trailhead is usually given as about 5,200 ft. The drive text does not state an elevation.
- Leg 1 runs to the summit pin, so it includes "climb the rib's seven pitches". The route has no base-of-route pin.

## wa_sharkfin_tower_southeast_ridge: 3 of 4 written (legs 1-3)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Note: the Boston Basin camp pin is at 6,200 ft. That is the high-camp knoll, not the 5,300-5,700 ft low camp, so leg 1 ends at the higher camp.
- The existing index-0 text makes a dated-sounding claim ("depends on the road being open past a washout"). It was not edited.

## wa_sheep_gap_mountain_scramble: 8 of 8 written
- The Barlow Pass drive and the road walk were confirmed: 200 ft east to the gate, then about 0.72 mi to the trail on the right. The Weden Creek ford is about 1.08 mi up the trail.
- Defect: the approach prose says the Monte Cristo Road walk is "1.5-1.7 miles". Credible sources and the itinerary say about 0.7-1 mi, which matches pin 1 (distMi 1). Leg 1 says about three quarters of a mile.
- Defect: the pin distances run short. The Gothic Basin tarns pin is at distMi 3.8, but the basin is about 4.5-4.6 mi from Barlow Pass (the approach prose itself says 4.5 mi, and the round trip is 9.2 mi). The Weden Creek ford pin at 3,000 ft is also probably high, since the trail starts climbing only after the ford.
- Pins 1 and 2 have null lat/lng.

## wa_sherman_peak_baker_route: 6 of 7 written (legs 1-6)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Defect: the approach and approach_variants say to "bear right at the signed Scott Paul Trail #603.1 junction (which splits left)". A credible trail source has it the other way: Scott Paul junctions at 0.1 mi and 2 mi, both on the RIGHT, and you stay LEFT on Park Butte. The Railroad Grade turn is right at 2.4 mi in both. Leg 2 avoids naming a side at the Scott Paul junctions.
- Defect: the prose says to "ford Sulfur Creek within 0.3-0.4 mi". That source has a bridge over Sulphur Creek right at the start and the Rocky Creek boulder fields at 0.7 mi. The crossing pin also has null coordinates and is at 3,600 ft, which is 200 ft above the 3,400 ft trailhead.
- Defect: the approach and climbing_route say to climb the Easton Glacier "north, angling slightly WEST toward Sherman Peak". Sherman Peak is EAST of the Easton line: the crater-rim pin is at -121.822, Sherman's summit at -121.814, and the Railroad Grade lies further west. Leg 5 says "north" only.
- Pins 1-4 have null coordinates. The Railroad Grade junction pin is at 4,900 ft, but the second Scott Paul junction is at about 4,400 ft only 0.4 mi before it, so 4,900 may be high.
- The itinerary says "roped glacier travel up the Easton Glacier via the Railroad Grade crest". That is mixed up but not used here.

## wa_sherman_peak_baker_squak_glacier: 2 of 2 written
- The drive and approach were confirmed against the land manager's Squak route page: Scott Paul #603.1 about 100 ft from the trailhead, then an unmarked junction north onto a climbers' path to the glacier toe at Crag View.
- Trailhead pin has no elevation.
- Possible defect: route_breakdown sends the line to "the saddle below the Roman Wall" and then to "traverse south of Mount Baker's true summit" toward Sherman Peak. That geometry is doubtful for a Sherman Peak objective, so leg 1 does not repeat it. approach_variants, itinerary and climbing_route are all null on this row, so the record is thin.

<!-- b085.report.md -->
# b085 report

## wa_silver_star_northwest_face (drive-only, 1 waypoint) — 1 written
- Index 0: drive to the SR-20 pullout near mp 165-166, about 4 mi east of Washington Pass.
- Defect: the approach says "the Burgundy Col trail, then traverse southwest to the base of the face". There is no trail, only a climbers' path. Also, the face is on the west side of the peak, while Burgundy Col is the standard route's crossing. Worth a check.

## wa_silver_star_west_face_couloir (drive-only, 1 waypoint) — 1 written
- Index 0: the same drive and pullout as above.
- Defect to check: the approach goes "via the Gato Negro descent gully on Whine Spire" but files the Silver Star / Burgundy Col pullout as the trailhead. The descent sends you back over Burgundy Col. I did not confirm that the Gato Negro gully starts from this same pullout.

## wa_sinister_peak_north_face (2 waypoints) — 2 written
- Index 0: Suiattle River Road (FR 26), 20 mi to the Downey Creek TH (the land manager gives 1,440 ft). Day-use fee or NW Forest Pass; wilderness permit.
- Index 1: the multi-day leg, condensed from the approach_variants and route_breakdown.
- Access note (not put in the text because it is dated): the land manager lists the TH as inaccessible because of Dec 2025 washouts. Suiattle Road is closed to vehicles about 4.5 mi in.
- Possible defect: the "Dome-Chickamin col" is given as about 8,500 ft, higher than Sinister's own summit (8,440/8,444). It may be right, since the col is on Dome's shoulder, but it is worth verifying.
- Small discrepancy: the approach_logistics trailhead coordinates (48.2591,-121.2244) differ slightly from the waypoint pin (48.2595,-121.2229). The TH elevation is 1,450 on file and 1,440 per the land manager.

## wa_skeena26 (3 waypoints) — 1 written (index 1)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Index 1: washout parking to the grassy saddle, taken from the approach_variant.
- Index 2 left "": the card is "Squire Creek Wall (true summit/high point)" with null lat/lng. The route ends on a forested ledge at the top of the buttress, and the true summit is "further up brushy scrambling" with no described line. A leg ending at the card's point is unsupported.
- Defect: the approach prose describes the Waterfall Basin route ("breaking into the open basin roughly 2 hours from the car"). The approach_variant describes a different line to the "west saddle" via two gullies. The two descriptions do not agree.
- Defect: the summit waypoint has no coordinates.

## wa_skookum_peak_twinsisters_scramble (6 waypoints) — 4 written (indices 1-4)
- Index 0 already had text. It contains dated flood claims that the brief discourages, but I did not overwrite it.
- Index 5 (ridge gain to summit) left "" because pins and prose conflict. The prose and the online beta say that from the ridge south of South Twin you go SOUTH over Jaw's Tooth, rappel to the notch, then climb Skookum's north ridge. The summit pin (48.700085) sits NORTH of the ridge-gain pin (48.698629). The row's own peakLat (48.694338,-121.987035) is south of the ridge-gain pin, consistent with the prose. The summit pin looks misplaced by about 0.4 mi.
- DEFECT (wrong identity): the summit note says Skookum is "also known as 'Kloke Peak'". Kloke Peak (formerly Hayden Peak, 6,445 ft) is a SEPARATE peak whose line parent is Skookum (6,534 ft). The descent text's "basin below Hayden" refers to that other peak. The note should not equate the two.
- The type label "scramble" conflicts with the itinerary's roped 5.4 north ridge and the rappel off Jaw's Tooth.

## wa_sloan_peak_corkscrew (3 waypoints) — 2 written (indices 1, 2)
- Index 0 already had text.
- Index 1: Bedal Creek Trail to the flagged climbers' path turnoff at about 3,500 ft (pin agrees: SE of the TH).
- Index 2: turnoff to the saddle, the glacier, the Corkscrew shelf, the gully and the class 4 step, then the summit.
- Defect: the approach_logistics trailhead is the FR 49 / North Fork Sauk climbers' pullout (48.0863,-121.3084, 1,900 ft). The waypoint trailhead is the Bedal Creek TH (48.0701,-121.3753, 2,800 ft). The logistics block describes a different trailhead from the waypoint array (the primary approach_variant is Bedal).
- Minor inconsistencies:
  - The descent text puts the "5,800 ft basin/saddle" in one place, while the Bedal saddle is 5,300 ft and the rock ridge is 5,800 ft.
  - The route_breakdown caches snow gear "near 7,300 ft", while climbing_route puts the glacier-to-rock transition near 7,100 ft.
  - The junction waypoint has no elevation (about 3,500 ft per the prose).

<!-- b086.report.md -->
# b086 report

## wa_south_arete (2/2 written)
- Both written: the drive to Blue Lake TH (MP 161, NW Forest Pass), and the Blue Lake Trail, climbers' path and arete leg to the summit.
- Defect (minor): the itinerary gives a ~1.75 hr approach, but approach_variants gives 2-2.5 hr.

## wa_south_early_winter_spire_direct_east_buttress (2/2 written)
- Both written: the drive to the SR-20 hairpin pullout, then Spire Gully, the last big tree, a traverse right, the N/S notch gully, a traverse left, and 9 pitches to the summit.
- Defect: the descent_text says "hike out via the climber's trail to the Blue Lake Trail". That is the west side, so a party parked at the hairpin needs a shuttle or a road walk. The text does say so, but the route has only one trailhead pin.

## wa_south_early_winter_spire_passenger (2/2 written)
- Both written. The leg ends "Climb the seven pitches to the South Arete and follow it to the summit", because the route tops out onto the South Arete and the descent starts "from the summit".
- Defect: the approach says the South Arete base is "about an hour in", but the South Arete row's own approach says 2-2.5 hr. The itinerary also says ~2 hr. Both of these figures were left out of the text.

## wa_south_early_winter_spire_southwest_couloir (2/2 written)
- Both written.
- Defect: the trailhead waypoint has no elev (it is null). Every sibling row has 5,400.

## wa_south_face (Vasiliki Tower) (1 of 3 empty slots written; index 0 already had text)
- Index 1 (the Ledge Bivy) and index 2 (Burgundy Col) are left blank, under the off-feature pin rule. The Ledge pin is at 48.54, -120.595, a round-number coordinate at 6,000 ft. It sits about 1.6 km SOUTH of the trailhead-to-col line, roughly 1.7 km from the 6,500-ft bench camp (which the Pernod row pins at 48.5513, -120.6104), and is not on the timbered rib north of Burgundy Creek that the approach climbs. The route's prose never mentions "The Ledge" at all: it names a bench camp at 6,400-6,500 ft. The Ledge is real (an online source puts it about 2 miles in), but this pin is not on the approach.
- Index 3 (col to summit) is written.
- Defect: the summit is 7,663 ft (peak elevation per Peakbagger), but the col is 7,800 ft. A tower that "rises directly from the col" cannot be 137 ft lower than the col, so one of the two elevations is wrong. The Pernod row gives the col as 7,770.
- Defect: the Burgundy Col note says the col "separates the Wine Spires from Vasiliki Ridge". The Pernod row's note for the same col calls it a "notch on Silver Star's north ridge". These are not quite the same feature description.

## wa_south_face_2 (Pernod South Face) (3/5 written)
- Index 0 is a creek crossing, not a trailhead. It is written as the short drive/pullout plus the 200-ft drop and the log crossing. Indexes 1 and 2 are written.
- Index 3 ("East-side scree gully traverse") and index 4 (summit) are left blank, because the index 3 pin is on the WRONG SIDE. The prose says the traverse goes around the EAST (Silver Star Creek) side of the ridge below Chianti. But the pin (48.55236, -120.59429) sits about 0.3 km southwest of Burgundy Col: west of the col and west of the Pernod pin, on the Burgundy Creek side the approach comes up. The note itself says "east side". The pin also sits 130 ft above the col on a traverse that should not be climbing above it.
- Defect: the Pernod summit pin (48.5526, -120.5903) matches Mountain Project's coordinate for CHABLIS Spire (48.55259, -120.59032). Pernod lies between Chianti and Chablis (the order runs NW to SE: Burgundy, Chianti, Pernod, Chablis), so this pin is probably on Chablis, not Pernod. (Mountain Project's own Pernod coordinate is wrong too: it is South Early Winters Spire's, 48.5123, -120.654, so do not use it as a fix.)
- Defect: the creek-crossing pin elevation is 4,200, but the prose says ~4,080 ft.
- Defect: the Burgundy Col note says the route "leaves the main Silver Star Glacier trail and works east". Wording only.
- Defect: approach_logistics carries no trailhead coordinates, and there is no pullout waypoint. Index 0 is the creek.

<!-- b087.report.md -->
# b087 report

## wa_south_face_3 (Concord Tower, South Face): 2/2 written
- Drive to Blue Lake TH (pin matches TH). Leg 1 goes via the Concord-Lexington notch and ends at the summit.
- No defects in the pins. distMi 3 one-way is generous next to the itinerary's 6 mi round trip, but the two agree.

## wa_south_face_center (Concord Tower, South Face Center): 2/2 written
- Same drive and approach as above. The leg ends with the route's own line (centre crack, bulge, two corners, summit block).
- Possible inconsistency: the itinerary says it finishes by "joining the North Face route's final pitch", the itinerary also calls it 5.7 A3, and the grade column says 5.8+. route_breakdown instead says it trends left to a tree and bolts. The leg avoids naming the finish pitch.

## wa_south_face_4 (Kangaroo Temple, South Face): 2/2 written
- Drive to the SR-20 hairpin turnout. Leg 1 goes over Kangaroo Pass, drops below it, traverses to the South Face, and goes up 7 pitches to the summit. No pass/fee claimed because the turnout is a highway pullout.
- descent_text is null, though the breakdown says to carry everything over the top. There is no descent information on file.

## wa_south_face_5 (Inspiration Peak, South Face): 2/2 written
- Drive to the Upper Goodell Creek Group Camp, confirmed online (first left after the Goodell Creek bridge, then uphill to the upper group camp). The pin at 48.6828 sits about 1 km north of the highway, which fits the upper group camp.
- Leg 1 compresses a 3-day approach (Goodell trail, Terror Basin camp, icefall, 4 pitches) into one leg because there is no camp waypoint. **Defect: the array has no Terror Basin camp pin**, and the TH-to-summit leg covers 8.5 mi and two days.
- Small defect: the itinerary gives the summit as 7,891', but the summit pin elev is 7,880.

## wa_south_ridge (South Peak, Gunsight Range): 1/2 written (drive only)
- Drive to Downey Creek TH via SR-530 and FS 26, about 20 mi, confirmed with the land manager's directions. Durable wording: "prone to washout closures, check status".
- **Current status:** the land manager reports the Suiattle River Road closed to vehicles about 4.5 mi in (washouts, Dec 2025), so the TH is currently foot/bike-only. This is not written as text because it is a dated status.
- Leg 1 was left blank. It is a 17.5 mi multi-day off-trail glacier approach. The prose is vague and lists the corridor in reverse order ("Itswoot Ridge and Cub Lake and up Bachelor Creek"; the real order up Downey is Bachelor Creek, then Cub Lake, then Itswoot Ridge). The itinerary also offers an alternative Stehekin approach.
- **Defects:**
  - Both waypoint notes are pipeline/meta voice. The TH note says "Corrected - previous waypoints incorrectly placed this route ~59 miles away ... a research error". The summit note says "This coordinate is a disclosed geometric estimate ... not a directly measured fix". Both render to climbers.
  - The summit pin is an estimate, not a measured position.
  - permit is null (a Glacier Peak Wilderness overnight trip).

## wa_south_ridge_2 (Luna Peak, South Ridge): 0 written (omitted)
- Index 0 already has text. Every leg ≥1 is left blank because the array is broken:
  - **Ordering fault:** waypoints 1 and 2 are two ALTERNATIVE crossings ("larger log jam" and "smaller logs, alternate") listed in series, so the leg from 1 to 2 is not a leg anyone travels.
  - The crossings are named "Access Creek crossing", but the pins look like the Big Beaver Creek crossing near the Access Creek confluence. The prose has two crossings: Big Beaver near the confluence, then Access Creek at 3,800 ft.
  - **Leg-arrival conflict:** the final pin is "Luna Peak summit (true/north summit)". The route as scoped in approach, baseFinding and descent_text stops at the FALSE (south) summit and calls the traverse to the true summit "beyond this route". The itinerary, though, includes a roped pitch to the true summit. The row contradicts itself on where the route ends.
- Other gaps:
  - The junction waypoints have no elev or distMi.
  - There are no pins for Luna Camp, the Access Creek basin camp, Access Col or Luna Pass.
  - Taxi savings are given as ~7 mi in the approach and ~6 mi in itinerary.cal.

<!-- b088.report.md -->
# b088 report

## wa_south_ridge_3 (Black Peak, South Ridge) — 8 of 8 written
- All legs written; pins agree with the prose (westward travel Heather Pass -> Lewis -> Wing, col SW of summit).
- Leg 7 (col -> summit) kept minimal on purpose. DEFECT/conflict: `route_breakdown` describes a "broad, loose gully" on the west side and a "~100 ft knife-edge with major exposure on both sides" to the summit, and the itinerary mentions a loose gully west of the crest; `approach_variants.baseFinding` says above the col it is only cairned ridge, a ledge traverse and one 15 ft step, "no other technical ground". The two descriptions of the summit line disagree.
- DEFECT: `descent_text` is garbled: it says "From the south col, downclimb along the cairned ridge ... to the summit pyramid ... followed by a short class 4 step to the true summit" (ascent written as descent).
- Minor: waypoints 4 (Wing Lake outlet) and 5 (Wing Lake camp) share identical coords/elev. Heather Pass junction pin elev 5,900 vs prose's Heather Pass ~6,100 ft. Prose says the left fork at the 1.3 mi junction "continues to Lake Ann/Maple Pass loop" — the left fork is the Lake Ann spur; the loop continues right toward Heather Pass (I wrote the latter).

## wa_south_ridge_4 (Eldorado Peak, South Ridge) — 1 of 4 written (index 0 already had text)
- Legs 1 and 2 left "" — DEFECT: waypoint 1 "Roush Creek Basin / Eldorado Glacier toe" has rounded coords (48.51, -121.125) and elev 5,200 ft. The Eldorado Glacier toe is far higher than 5,200 ft, and the name conflates two different basins (Eldorado Creek basin vs Roush Creek basin). The pin appears to sit in the Eldorado Creek basin, not at the place its name says, so legs into and out of it are blocked.
- Leg 3 (camp -> summit) written from the route_breakdown/approach_variants.

## wa_south_ridge_6 (Aiguille de l'M, South Ridge) — 2 of 2 written
- Drive and single leg TH -> summit (via Boston Basin camp, 5 pitches) written.
- DEFECT: `approach_logistics.trailheadDirection` calls the Boston Basin TH "a different, lower stop than the Eldorado trailhead a mile or so up-road" while `approach` says Boston Basin is up-road of Eldorado; elevations (Boston 3,200 vs Eldorado 2,100) show Boston Basin is higher and farther up the road. The logistics sentence is wrong.
- Note: the array skips the high camp (only TH and summit), so the one leg spans approach + climb.

## wa_south_spur (Whatcom Peak, South Spur) — 2 of 2 written
- Drive confirmed (FR 32, keep left on Ruth Creek Road 5.4 mi to Hannegan CG road end). Leg 1 summarises Hannegan Pass -> Chilliwack ford -> Easy Ridge -> Imperfect Impasse -> Perfect Pass -> south snowfield -> crest.
- DEFECT (minor): trailhead note calls "the Chilliwack River Trail then the Easy Ridge climbers' path" an "alternate approach", but that is the route's own primary approach. Distance mismatch: waypoint distMi 15 vs prose "about 16 miles" to Perfect Pass alone. TH pin (-121.5941) ~350 m from approach_logistics TH lng (-121.5894).

## wa_south_twin_sister_north_ridge — 2 of 2 written
- Drive from SR 542 / Mosquito Lake Road / FR 38 to the gated bridge; leg summarises bike roads -> Orsino Creek -> 5,800 ft saddle -> Sisters Glacier -> north ridge crest.
- Oddity: `route_breakdown` mentions "optional harder rock climbing on Arrowtip Tower" — not mentioned anywhere else in the row; worth checking.

## wa_south_twin_sister_scramble — 2 of 2 written
- Same drive; leg follows the route_breakdown (5,200 ft West Ridge notch, high notch ~6,600 ft, traverse right to the south gullies).
- DEFECTS (minor): trailhead waypoint has no `elev`. Route_breakdown puts "a large dihedral near 6,500 ft" in the southern gullies that are entered above the ~6,600 ft high notch — internally inconsistent elevation. The row is named "scramble" but is the West Ridge / south-gullies line, same approach as the other South Twin routes.

# b089 report

## wa_southeast_face (Sharkfin Tower) — 0 written, omitted
- Index 0 already has text (not overwritten).
- ORDERING FAULT: the summit (i=1) comes before "Sharkfin Tower Southeast Face" (i=2, Route type, same 8,120 ft elevation as the summit). A party reaches the face start (by climbing two SE Ridge pitches and rappelling) before the summit, so the array is out of travel order. All legs left blank.
- The i=2 pin carries the summit elevation (8,120) for the base of a two-pitch face, which is wrong.

## wa_southeast_ridge_se_corner (Mount Shuksan) — 2 of 4 written (drive, TH to Sulphide camp)
- Legs 2 and 3 blank: the "Notch below SE Ridge" pin (48.815, -121.607, 8,500 ft) looks misplaced. It sits about 1.85 km south of the summit yet only ~630 ft below it, while it is only ~1.35 km from the 6,400 ft camp with 2,100 ft between them. The prose puts the notch at the top of the upper Sulphide near the summit pyramid; the pin is likely too far south (or its elevation is wrong). distMi (6.0 to 6.7) also implies 0.7 mi from notch to summit.
- Source mileage on FS Road 1152 to the 1152-014 spur disagrees (3 vs 4.5 mi), so the drive omits it.
- itinerary string mentions a bivy "near Winnie's Slide" (Fisher Chimneys approach) while the primary approach and pins are Shannon Ridge; mixed itineraries, not a pin defect.

## wa_southern_man (South Early Winters Spire) — 2 of 2 written
- Minor inconsistency: TH note says the route shares its "first 4-5 pitches" with the Direct East Buttress; route_breakdown has it breaking left on pitch 4 (3 shared pitches).

## wa_southwest_buttress (Dorado Needle) — 3 of 8 written (legs 3, 4, 5)
- Index 0 already has text.
- Legs 1 and 2 blank: "Eldorado Creek Basin camp" pin (48.52897, -121.171809, 5,650 ft) is ~3.5-4 km west of the high camp and of the Eldorado Creek drainage the approach follows; its note ("after the first two talus fields and a waterfall crossing") also does not match the prose's lower camp in Roush Creek basin at 6,100-6,400 ft. Pin appears on the wrong feature.
- Legs 6 and 7 blank: "Base of Southwest Buttress gully" is stored at 7,900 ft, ABOVE the 7,600 ft Marble Creek-McAllister pass, while its own note and the prose say the gully begins about 700 ft BELOW the pass. Elevation (or the pass's) contradicts the prose.
- i=3 and i=4 have no coordinates; legs 3-4 written from their own notes and the approach prose only.
- approach prose places the trailhead-to-pass distance at ~25.75 km round trip vs itinerary ~16 mi — consistent enough; not flagged.

## wa_sperry_peak_standard — 1 of 3 written (drive-only mode)
- Drive confirmed (Mountain Loop Hwy, Sunrise Mine Road FS 4065, ~2.2 mi to end). Legs blank per mode. Note: i=1 "Lake Elan" (48.0143, -121.5085) is west of the summit pin (-121.5025) — consistent with reaching the saddle from the lake; the ordering-gate failure is upstream.

## wa_spider_mountain_north_face — 1 of 2 written (drive)
- Leg 1 blank: the array jumps from trailhead straight to the summit with no Cache Col, Kool-Aid Lake, Art's Knoll or face-base pins, and the row has no route_breakdown or face description, so a single trailhead-to-summit leg cannot be written with confidence.
- TH pin has no elevation (approach_logistics says ~3,660 ft). Row grade is null.

<!-- b090.report.md -->
# b090 report

## wa_spontaneity_arete — 2 written (legs 1, 2)
- Index 0 already filled; not touched.
- Defect: the "Le Petit Cheval Upper Mountain" topout pin and the summit pin have identical coordinates (48.53569, -120.62207). The route's own breakdown puts 200 to 300 ft of class 3 crest between the end of the technical pitches and the summit. Leg 1 ends at the top of the technical climbing and leg 2 is the crest scramble, which is what the prose says. The topout pin should sit a little below the summit.
- Pin elev 6,386 vs a published 6,480+ ft for the summit (minor).

## wa_spontaneous_distraction — 0 written
- Index 0 already filled.
- Legs 1 and 2 left blank. This is a 4-pitch line on the lower buttress beside the gully. Its own descent says to "scramble back to the top of pitch 3 and rappel", and only parties "finishing higher on the formation" reach the arete's line. The route does not reach the "Upper Mountain" topout or the summit that its waypoints 1 and 2 name, and both of those pins sit on the summit. A leg ending there would be wrong. The waypoints appear to have been copied from Spontaneity Arete.

## wa_summertime — 0 written
- Index 0 already filled.
- Defect: all three pins (the pullout, the "Summertime Crag" topout, and the crag) have identical coordinates and elevation (48.52006, -120.6398, 5,231 ft). The crag and topout pins sit on the highway pullout, which puts them on the wrong feature, so legs 1 and 2 are blank. Two smaller faults: the topout is distMi 0.3 from a point at the same location, and the 4-pitch, 400 ft route tops out at the same elevation as its base. Separately, the descent_text says "The descent beyond the crux pitch is unknown", which is meta, not climber guidance.

## wa_superalpine — 2 written (drive + leg to summit)
- The drive was confirmed against land-manager and trail sources: Mountain Loop Hwy 17.2 mi south of Darrington, FR 4096 about 1 mi past Bedal Campground, then 2 mi (some sources say 3) of steep road to the trailhead at about 2,800 ft. The text says "about 2 miles".
- The trail pin is 48.072 and approach_logistics gives 48.0719, which is negligible.
- Leg 1 ends at the summit through the upper snowfields, which the prose supports ("continuation to the summit was done later").

## wa_sw_couloir_and_face — 3 written (drive, Lake Ann, icefall base); legs 3 and 4 blank
- Leg 3 is blank: the pin "Ice chimney / headwall" names two different features. Its note says it is the vertical ice chimney "in the lower icefall", just above the first crevasse, about 100 to 200 ft above the start. The headwall is far higher, after the rock step, the glacier tongue, the second crevasse and the 60-70° ice. The pin sits 1,000 ft above the base pin (7,200 vs 6,200), which fits neither feature cleanly.
- The on-file prose also disagrees with itself. baseFinding says the crevasse is followed by "roughly 300 ft of steep ice and snow to the base of the headwall". route_breakdown puts the chimney, rock step, tongue, second crevasse and 60-70° ice between them.
- Leg 4 is blank because it leaves that contested waypoint.
- The Lake Ann pin elevation of 4,800 ft is slightly high (the lake is about 4,700 ft), which is minor.

## wa_sw_ridge — 2 written (ferry/shuttle access + leg to summit)
- Index 0 is not a drive. It describes the ferry from Chelan to Stehekin and the shuttle about 11 mi to High Bridge, as the row's own prose does.
- Leg 1 follows the primary Agnes Creek / Blue Glacier variant. The final step from the east-side high camp around to the west side of the towers and up the SW Ridge is supported by a trip report, but it is described only generally. No col was named, because one report says the correct col is ambiguous ("first col south of the south peak, not the second").
- Note: the area is named "Middle Peak" but the waypoint says "Middle Gunsight", and the row has no grade.
- The descent_text says to rappel "the far (east) side". That fits a party camped on the east side, but it means the ascent and descent use opposite sides, which is consistent with the leg.

# b091 report

## wa_swiss_peak_standard_route (drive-only, 1 waypoint) — 1 written
- [0] Ross Dam Trailhead drive written (SR 20 from Burlington, MP 134, NCNP backcountry permit).
- Defect: grade is null; peak location is imprecise per the row's own prose.

## wa_table_mountain_standard_scramble (2) — 2 written
- [0] drive via SR 542 to Artist Point; [1] Trail #681 to the high point, ~1.4 mi.
- Minor inconsistency: the approach calls the high point ~1.4-1.5 mi along the trail and the tarns (1.3 mi) "a natural high point/turnaround". The itinerary calls the true summit an "optional scramble" beyond the tarns. The leg says only "to the high point". Gain also disagrees: 700-725 ft of gain from 5,100 ft gives about 5,825 ft, but the summit pin is 5,744 ft.

## wa_tenpeak_mountain_southeast (8) — 5 written (0 already had text)
- Written: [1] to the Boulder Creek / Boulder Pass trail junction (about 4.1 mi); [2] to the Thunder Creek camp; [3] the sheep path through the alder; [6] the gully; [7] the chimney to the summit.
- [4] and [5] blank. The Thunder Basin high camp has null lat/lng and a contested elevation. The pin says 6,400 ft, the approach and itinerary say the upper basin is ~5,180 ft (lower basin 4,175 ft), and the variant notes admit "three different elevations for that camp". So the legs into and out of it were left blank.
- Conflict: the camp [2] note says the sheep path leaves the trail AT the Thunder Creek crossing. The approach and an outside source say it leaves about half a mile PAST the crossing. Leg [3] says "near the crossing" and does not pick one.
- Elevation defects:
  - The Boulder Creek junction pin is 3,600 ft and the Thunder Creek camp pin is 4,200 ft. Both look far too high for the White River valley floor, which starts from a 2,300 ft trailhead. The prose puts the lower basin, above the creek, at 4,175 ft.
  - The alder hazard pin is 5,000 ft, but the prose says the alder ends and then opens into a lower basin at 4,175 ft. That order is impossible on elevation.
  - The summit is 8,312 ft on the pin; published figures are about 8,281 ft.
- The grade says "+ glacier", but the standard SE route crosses talus and snow, not a glacier. Glaciers appear only on the northern variants.

## wa_tepeh_towers (5) — 2 written (0 already had text)
- Written: [1] the standard Eldorado approach to the East Ridge camp; [2] about 45 minutes NNW on the glacier to the base of the towers. The pin bearing agrees: the base is N, slightly W, of camp.
- [3] blank. "Glacier-to-rock crossing point" has exactly the same coordinates as [2] "Base of Tepeh Towers", so this is a duplicate pin and a zero-length leg.
- [4] blank. The summit pin (48.5504, -121.1218, 8,113 ft) is the GNIS centroid point for the ridge feature, not the climbed spire. The published high point is about 8,394 ft. The pin is about 0.8 km NE of the base pin and NNE of camp, but the route is described as one short pitch plus a scramble above the moat, NNW of camp. The summit pin's height and place contradict the route, so no leg was written into it.

## wa_the_cave_route (Concord Tower, 2) — 2 written
- [0] drive from Winthrop to the Blue Lake TH; [1] trail, climbers' path, notch, 50 ft down the north side, then the 3 pitches to the summit.
- Note defect: the summit note says "Cave Route is an alternative line used when Liberty Bell is crowded". That is odd and unsupported, because the route is on Concord, not Liberty Bell.
- The Blue Lake TH is closer to ~5,200 ft than 5,400 ft. This is minor and was not used beyond "about 5,400 ft", which matches the row.

## wa_the_chalice (Silver Horn, 2) — 2 written
- [0] SR 20 to FR 5310-200 (confirmed by the land manager: 18 mi from Winthrop, between MP 176 and 175, gravel pit); [1] Cedar Creek Trail, the climbers' path, then the line to the summit.
- The descent is self-described as an inference: "not a confirmed fact". The approach_variants hazards also list that. It renders as climber-facing text that admits it is unverified, so it is worth a look.

# b092 report

## wa_the_chopping_block_south_route — 1 written (leg 1)

- Legs 2-3 blank: on-file fields disagree about Terror Creek. `approach` has the ford right at the cairn junction; waypoint 2 places a logjam crossing 0.7 mi on and 800 ft higher (2,600 ft), and `itinerary` day 1 has the path running "south of Terror Creek". Waypoints 1-2 have null coords.
- Legs 3-4 blank: the Barrier Crest pin (48.759013,-121.301766) sits on ground at about 6,430 ft (USGS) but the card says 3,800 ft. `itinerary` puts the Barrier spur at about 6,200 ft.
- Legs 5-6 blank: the South Face Gully pin (48.758509,-121.307305) is NORTH of the summit pin (48.755), not on its south side, and its ground is about 5,610 ft against the 6,300 ft on the card.
- Order conflict: the pins have Stump Hollow as the camp straight after the Barrier crest, then travel back south to the gully and the summit. `itinerary` day 2 instead camps near the Barrier/Terror Basin and crosses the Barrier into Stump Hollow on summit day. `approach` says the crest is followed "several miles" NW to Stump Hollow, but the pins are about 0.6 mi apart.

The card elevation is 1,600 ft; the trailhead ground is about 2,125 ft (1,600 is roughly lake level). One web listing says there is no overnight parking at this trailhead, while Ross Lake Resort directs water-taxi users to park there. I did not state either, but someone should check before a 6-day trip relies on it.

# b093 report

## wa_the_perfect_crime_with_variations — 0 written (omitted)
- Index 0 already has text.
- **Defect: wp1 "Cutthroat Wall Base" is typed `Topout`**, and its note talks about the descent/rappel gully. Name and type disagree, so legs into and out of it (1, 2) are left blank.
- **Defect: wp3 "Cutthroat Wall" (Crag) comes after the route-start ledge** and sits on the same coordinate as wp1. It is a summary pin in a travel slot, so leg 3 is blank too.

## wa_the_pleiades_scramble — 6 written (1–6)
- Index 0 already has text.
- The Trail 685/676 junction turn (right onto High Pass Trail #676, left to the lookout) checks out against the land-manager page.
- Using "third Pleiad = high point, reached via the goat ledge a little over halfway along" checks out against several trip reports.
- **Defect: the route is named "Glacier/Scramble Route"**, but nothing on it crosses a glacier.
- **Defect: wp5 (the notch) has null lat/lng.** Its legs were written from prose only.
- **Minor: the TH pin sits about 0.13 mi north of the isthmus pin.** The road actually ends between the lakes, so the two points are basically the same spot.
- **Minor: the junction distance is "0.5 mi" on the land-manager page but "a quarter mile" in another source.** The text says "a short way up".

## wa_the_pyramid_picket_south_route — 4 written (1–4)
- Index 0 already has text.
- **Defect: wp5 "Degenhardt-Pyramid col" (48.7670, -121.3016) is on the wrong feature.**
  - It sits about 700 m WSW of Degenhardt's summit (48.7714, -121.2931).
  - The Pyramid lies east of Degenhardt, between it and Inspiration (-121.2883), so a Degenhardt–Pyramid col cannot be west of Degenhardt.
  - It is the same coordinate as wa_the_rake_traverse_route's "Crescent Creek Basin High Camp" (48.766899, -121.301569).
  - Legs 5 and 6 are left blank.
- **Possible defect: the summit pin (48.77144, -121.29193) is only about 90 m from Degenhardt's published coordinate.** Check it against The Pyramid's real summit.
- **Prose inconsistency:**
  - The waypoint note and baseFinding say low-5th moves off the col onto the west side, then a Class 3 scramble.
  - The itinerary says the notch is "south of Degenhardt", reached by a glacier crossing below Inspiration/Degenhardt, then a 3rd/4th-class ridge traverse.
- **Minor:** the cairn is at 4.3 mi on the pin but 4.5 mi in the approach prose. The pullout pin and approach_logistics trailheadLat also differ by about 450 m (48.68276 vs 48.68664).

## wa_the_rake_traverse_route — 8 written (0–7)
- The drive (first left after Goodell Creek, left at the T, right branch up to the Upper Goodell group camp) checks out against several trip reports. It matches the TH pin.
- **Defect: the trailhead waypoint has distMi 0.29 instead of 0.**
- **Minor:**
  - The summit pin says 7,840 ft, but the itinerary says 7,869 ft.
  - The Barrier note says you gain the crest "around 6,400 ft", but the camp note calls it a 6,200-ft saddle and the pin says 6,200. The leg avoids naming a crest elevation.
- The approach offers the Terror Basin line or the direct Crescent Creek line. The pins follow Terror Basin plus the Barrier, and the legs follow the pins.

# b094 report

## wa_three_fingers_r1 (North Peak): 1 written (index 0, the drive)
- Legs 1-7 already had text, so I left them alone.
- DEFECT: the summit pin (48.169895, -121.687865) sits on the SOUTH Peak lookout (48.1699, -121.6879). The North Peak is at about 48.1741, -121.6900, roughly 480 m north. That means the pin is on the wrong summit.
- DEFECT: the "Col between Middle and North Finger" pin (48.1670, -121.6902) is south of the South Peak lookout. The real col lies north of the lookout, between about 48.172 and 48.174, so this pin is about 600 m or more off.
- DEFECT: the "South Peak lookout ladders" pin (48.1664, -121.6972) is about 700 m WSW of the lookout it names.
- DEFECT in the array and in the existing legs: waypoints 4 and 5 send the North Peak route over the South Peak lookout ladders ("leaving the lookout..."). The row's own approach and baseFinding say the route goes from Tin Can Gap straight across the glacier to the Middle/North col. The baseFinding even says that standing under the ladders means you are "two towers away". The existing legs 4-6 follow a line the row itself rejects.
- Trailhead elevation is 3,020 ft here but 2,650 ft in the lookout row, at the same pin. A trip report gives about 3,050 ft.

<!-- b095.report.md -->
# b095 report

## wa_trapper_mountain_south_slopes — 2 of 5 written (0 drive, 1 TH to Trapper Lake)
- Legs 2-4 blank: the row contradicts itself about which side of the peak the route climbs. The waypoint-2 note and variant 1 say to climb "the broad south slopes/basin directly above Trapper Lake", but the lake sits NE of the summit, and variant 2 (the primary one) says "the south slopes are gained by going round the peak, not up the side facing you. The face you can see from the lake is the northern one." Pins 2 and 3 lie on the straight lake-to-summit line, which puts them on the NE (lake-facing) side. Per that variant, they are on the wrong flank for a "south slopes" route.
- Variant 1 itself warns that written descriptions and recorded waypoints name different drainages and that the distances don't match the positions.
- Itinerary day 1 lists 2,400 ft gain and 700 ft loss. TH 3,650, pass 5,392 and lake 4,170 imply roughly 1,750 ft gain and at least 1,200 ft loss.
- The leg-1 wording was confirmed online: lowest Pelton Basin tent pad, boot path to Pelton Creek, cairned crossing, inlet stream, then the marsh to the lake.

## wa_tricouni_peak_southwest_slopes — nothing written (index 0 already had text)
- **Ordering fault:** "Thunder Creek log crossing" (i1, distMi 6.4) is listed BEFORE McAllister Camp (i2, 6.6). Two independent trip reports put the crossing AFTER the camp: past the camp, about half a mile on, down to the creek, across a log to the west bank, then north to the old bridge site and the climbers' path. All legs at index 1 and above were left blank.
- **Pin off:** the crossing pin (48.61, -121.085) is about 2.7 km from the McAllister Camp pin. The prose puts the crossing about 900 ft from the Thunder/McAllister confluence, which is where the camp is.
- **Possibly wrong prose:** one trip report climbs the Borealis Glacier on its EASTERN edge to Lucky Pass to avoid a central icefall. The row says "west edge". Another report also says west edge, so it is contested.
- Borealis Lake camp pin (48.578) is south of Lucky Pass and of the summit (48.582), while the viewpoint (48.588) is north. Going viewpoint → lake → pass would pass east of the col and then double back. The lake pin may be misplaced (its note says it is approximate).
- The itinerary calls the summit scramble class 2-3. The approach, variant and breakdown call it class 3-4.

## wa_true_grit_2 — 4 of 5 written
- **Direction contradiction:** the variant says "Traverse east on the goat trail" from the saddle. The start pin (-121.51736) is about 700 ft WEST of the saddle cairns (-121.51451). Leg 2 was written without a direction.
- The approach prose has two different lines from the lake to the saddle: "cross the outlet and follow the obvious talus ridgeline" in the approach, and "stay on the summit trail to 5,600-5,700 then swing north" in the variant. Leg 1 names neither line.
- The saddle is called "Vesper-Wolf saddle" here and in the Ragged Edge row. I could not verify a Wolf Peak there, so the name is not used in the text.
- Leg 4 (topout → summit) left blank. The Topout pin (48.01293, -121.51801, 6214) and the Summit pin (48.012988, -121.517992, 6221) are the same point, about 1 m apart, with two elevations. They are duplicate waypoints.
- The True Grit start pin is identical to the Ragged Edge "original start" pin. The True Grit pin is named the notch, which the Ragged Edge row calls a separate, alternative start, so it may be a copied coordinate.
- The descent sources disagree. descent_text says southeast side, aiming for the "Vesper-Wolf saddle" above Vesper Lake, but the approach puts that saddle on the north side. The itinerary says "south/west talus gully".

## wa_up_in_arms — 2 of 2 written
- Neither waypoint has elev or distMi. The approach is thin, so leg 1 is one sentence of approach plus the climb. The drive was confirmed online (south side of SR-20 between MP 161 and 162).

## wa_vesper_peak_north_face_ragged_edge — 4 of 5 written (index 0 already filled)
- **Same direction contradiction:** approach and variant say "traverse these ledges roughly 800 ft eastward". The start pin is about 700 ft WEST of the saddle cairns. Leg 3 was written without a direction.
- The approach says the trail goes "around Sperry Peak's south flank". I could not confirm which flank, so it was written as "past the pass into the lake basin".

## wa_vesper_peak_standard_route — 2 of 2 written
- Summit pin elevation is 6,108. The row's own climbing_route says 6,214 ft, as do the other Vesper rows (6,214/6,221). The elevation is wrong.
- Summit distMi is 3.1, but Headlee Pass alone is 2.7 mi and the lake is about 3.5 mi (other rows). That is impossible: the summit is about 4.5+ mi.

# b096 report

## wa_waterfall_buttress (2 wp) — 1 written
- [0] already filled. [1] written from the on-file approach variant (roadbed, white-boulder streambed turn-off, alder path, Squire Creek ford, basin, rib left of the waterfall).

## wa_west_face (South Peak, Gunsight Range; 2 wp) — 2 written
- [0] drive: confirmed on the USFS Downey Creek Trailhead page (8 mi north of Darrington to Suiattle River Rd 26, then 20 mi east, just past the Downey Creek bridge, 1,440 ft, NW Forest Pass or interagency pass). The USFS page currently lists the trailhead closed by Suiattle River Road washouts from December 2025. Because that is a dated status, I wrote only a durable "prone to washout closures, check status" line.
- [1] written as a summary of the whole multi-day approach plus the climb, because there are no intermediate pins. It uses the route's own prose: Bachelor Creek, Cub Lake, Itswoot Ridge, the Dome-Chickamin col, the Chickamin Glacier, and the left-side chimney start.
- Note: the summit pin is a disclosed estimate about 0.2 mi south of Middle Peak, which is within tolerance. The row has no descent, gear, or hazard data, as its own variant says.

## wa_west_face_2 (North Peak / North Gunsight West Face; 5 wp) — 2 written ([0], [4])
- [1] and [2] are blank. The "Bachelor Flats (Camp 1)" waypoint is stored at 3,500 ft. Trip reports put the Bachelor Flats forest camp at about 4,500 ft, above a second Bachelor Creek footlog near 4,100 ft. That puts the camp pin about 1,000 ft low, so the legs into and out of it are blocked. The waypoint also has no coordinates.
- [3] is blank. Waypoints 2 ("Chikamin-Dome Col (Camp 2/High Camp)", 8,500 ft) and 3 ("Dome-Chickamin Col", Junction, 8,500 ft) are the same place listed twice, so this leg has no length. That is a duplicated waypoint and should be merged.
- [4] is written from the col to the summit. It uses the on-file variant (descending traverse on the Chikamin Glacier, bergschrund snow bridge, the ramp split with a cairn) and route_breakdown pitch 6 (terrace, then the chimney to the summit). A trip report confirms the 8,500 ft col camp and a roughly 90-minute descending traverse to the base.
- Defects:
  - None of waypoints 1 to 4 have lat/lng.
  - The camp plan disagrees with itself. The variant says the first camp is at Cub Lake/Itswoot Ridge, the itinerary says Day 1 reaches the Cub Lake basin or the col, and the waypoint says Camp 1 is Bachelor Flats.
  - The descent_text says "Total descent time from summit to vehicle is typically 3-4 days", which contradicts the itinerary's 3-day round trip.

## wa_west_ridge_2 (Paisano Pinnacle; 3 wp) — 2 written
- [1] and [2] are written from the approach and variant prose (log over Early Winters Creek, the rib north of Burgundy Creek, the 6,500 ft bench with its spring, the contour south to the chockstone start).
- Minor: the "Bench Camp spring" waypoint has distMi 4, which seems high for a roughly 2-hour, 2 km straight-line approach. The variant also puts a spring in a dry sandy gully on the contour toward the ridge toe, separate from the "spring near the bench camp", so the spring's location is slightly ambiguous. I avoided saying exactly where it is.

## wa_west_twin_needle_south_route (7 wp) — 3 written ([1], [5], [6])
- [1] trailhead to the upper fork: the old Goodell Creek road for about 3.5 mi to the cairned climbers' path. This matches an independent account of a stream crossing and cairn at about 3.5 mi. The fork elevation is stored as 2,200 ft, but trip reports put the turn-off at about 1,700 ft, which the row's own itinerary also says ("steep boot path from ~1,700 ft"). Reported, and I did not state an elevation.
- [2], [3] and [4] are blank. The row misdescribes "The Barrier":
  - The approach prose treats the Barrier as the steep, brushy rib climbed straight up from Terror Creek, which then "drops about 200 ft into Crescent Creek Basin".
  - Climbing sources describe the Barrier as the west wall of Terror Basin. You cross it from the Terror Basin camp (about 5,800 ft) on a steep, loose grassy ledge around 6,100 ft to a crest around 6,400 ft, then make a steep descent into Crescent Creek Basin. The brushy steep climb is the path up to Terror Basin.
  - The Barrier waypoint has no coordinates and is stored at 5,400 ft, below where it is crossed.
  - Sources also disagree on where Terror Creek is crossed: log crossings at about 2,100 ft, versus a path up the spur south of Terror Creek. The pinned crossing at 2,500 ft could not be confirmed.
  - The row has no Terror Basin camp or saddle waypoint. These waypoints need re-research, not a leg written over the top of them.
- [5] (camp to Eye Col) and [6] (Eye Col to summit) are written from the consistent on-file baseFinding and waypoint notes. The "Eye Col gully traverse" waypoint has no coordinates; its stored 7,400 ft is the notch elevation, so the leg ends at the notch.

## wa_western_dihedral (3 wp) — 1 written
- [1] is written from the on-file variant: two cut logs with logging cable, a right turn onto the boot track, a ford, steep old growth, the grassy saddle. Mountain Project's South Face approach confirms it.
- [2] is blank. The summit card says "Squire Creek Wall (true summit/high point)" at 4,958 ft, while the saddle sits at 3,249 ft and the route is 5 pitches and about 700 ft, plus a class 4 scramble to the start. That cannot reach 4,958 ft, so either the summit elevation is wrong or the card names a different high point than where the route tops out. The summit waypoint also has no coordinates.
- Defects:
  - Same trailhead coordinate/elevation conflict as wa_waterfall_buttress (1,900 ft here vs 1,100 ft). The name "Trail #654, end of FS Rd 2040" sits on the washout-parking coordinate.
  - The `approach` prose says the South Face is "one of the walls encountered within that same basin complex" as Waterfall Basin. That contradicts the variant, which reaches it by a separate turn-off at the cut logs on the roadbed and a climb to the grassy saddle.
  - Mountain Project says the base of this route is reached by going left from the saddle to the base of Skeena26 and doing a class 4 scramble to the far western wall. The saddle waypoint's note does not mention that scramble.

Waypoint 2 (Summit) has **identical coordinates and elevation** (48.54975, -120.6435, 6,263 ft). 6,263 ft is the climbing-area pin elevation, not a measured topout. A leg to the base would not arrive at a "Topout", and a leg from one pin to the identical pin is meaningless, so both legs are left "". Known approach, for whoever repairs the pins: no trail; walk up through the woods gaining quickly, then traverse left through open forest and slabby benches to a shoulder at the base of the wall's right side, 1-1.5 hr.

<!-- b098.report.md -->
# b098 — Amphitheater Mountain (6 routes)

Drive (index 0, all 6): the USFS Andrews Creek Trailhead page (3,050 ft; Hwy 20 > CR 1213 West Chewuch Rd 6 mi > FR 51 to end of pavement, turn right) plus its pass/permit rules. Trail leg: the WTA Andrews Creek page and trip reports (#504 ~12 mi to Andrews Pass, keep left ~5.5 mi, right at Peepsight jct, 3 mi down to Spanish Camp guard station, trail on the right just before the cabin 3 mi to Upper Cathedral Lake; total ~18 mi, which fits the ~19 mi measured route).

## finger_of_fatwa — 1 of 5 (index 0 only)
- ORDERING FAULT: i1 is the summit, then i2 topout, i3 junction, i4 climbing area. That is the reverse of the order a party travels, so every leg ≥1 is blank.
- i2/i3/i4 all share one pin (48.97698,-120.19208) — see the shared-pin defect below.

## north_ridge — 1 of 5 (index 0 only)
- ORDERING FAULT: same as Fatwa (summit at i1 before the "North Ridge base" junction and climbing area). Legs ≥1 are blank.
- i2/i3/i4 share the one pin; the "North Ridge base" sits at the same coordinate as the generic "routes area".

## middle_finger_buttress_left_side — 2 of 5 (0, 1)
- Leg 1 written: trailhead to the base above the lake.
- Legs 2–4 blank: i1 (area), i2 (junction "Middle Finger Buttress") and i3 (topout) are ONE identical pin, so the legs between them have no length and no meaning. Topout→summit (i4) is not supported by any source (the descent goes east to a col, not to the summit).
- Approach says to "walk the north shore". Every source puts the wall directly above the lake on its SOUTH side (the north face of Amphitheater), so that shore detail looks doubtful. I wrote no shore side.

## middle_finger_buttress_right_side — 2 of 5 (0, 1)
- Same structure and the same blanks as Left Side.

## pilgrimage_to_mecca — 2 of 5 (0, 1)
- Legs 2–4 blank: the area, junction and base are one identical pin. Base→summit is unsourced (the descent hikes east to a saddle and back to the lake, not to the summit).
- CONFLICT: the `approach` says to walk "around the north side" of the lake, but the variant says to walk "around it on the west side" from a north-side camp. The leg names no side.
- PIN DEFECT: the Ka'aba Buttress pin is the SAME coordinate as Middle Finger Buttress, but Ka'aba is two buttresses to the left of it.

## west_route — 2 of 2
- Leg 1 (trailhead → summit) is written from the row's own baseFinding and breakdown: boot path at ~7,200 ft about half a mile along the trail from the lake, the basin at 8,000 ft, then walk the plateau to its highest point. I left out the compass bearings, because the row's "southeast from the lake" fits poorly with a lake that sits north/northwest of the summit, and I could not confirm it.
- PROSE DEFECT: `approach` says Andrews Creek TH is "~22 miles to the trailhead at road's end". It is at the end of the PAVEMENT; the road continues to Thirtymile, which is the true road end.
- descent_text goes "back into the west basin, then drop to Cathedral Pass and Upper Cathedral Lake". Cathedral Pass lies between Amphitheater and Cathedral Peak (north-east), which fits oddly with a west-side basin.
- Trailhead pin 48.7837,-120.1086 is about 60 m from approach_logistics' 48.7842,-120.1088. Minor.

## Shared-pin defect (5 routes)
Every route-specific waypoint except the summit (Middle Finger area/junction/topout, Ka'aba area/base, North Ridge base) uses 48.97698,-120.19208. That point is ~160 m north of the summit (48.97556), so it sits near the TOP of the north face, not at the base above Upper Cathedral Lake (~7,380 ft). It also cannot be both Ka'aba and Middle Finger. No elevations are stored on these pins.

The existing leg 1, the approach and route_breakdown start at the Robinson Creek TH (about 48.6626,-120.5425, a different trailhead ~1.5 mi away on Harts Pass Road). The loop finishes at Monument Creek, so the array's first point is the loop's exit, not its start. Conflict, so left blank.
- DEFECT: Rolo Pass pin (48.81934,-120.56961) is at a ground elevation of 6,087 ft against the 7,100 ft it claims. It sits ~1.5 km east of the ridge-crest pass above Fred's Lake. Mount Carru's Rolo Pass pin (48.81956,-120.59041) reads 7,109 ft and fits "on the ridge crest overlooking Fred's Lake". This is a cross-route disagreement, and Lost's pin is the wrong one. The existing legs 4 and 5 into and out of it were not touched.
- Note: the existing leg 6 text ("gain either the southwest or the northwest ridge from a camp near the pass") differs from the approach and baseFinding (Butte Pass → Pass Butte → connecting ridge southeast). Worth review.

## wa_mount_ballard_south — 5 of 8 written (idx 0, 1, 2, 3, 6)
- idx 0: drive to the Canyon Creek TH at SR 20 MP 141. Pin matches the land-manager coordinate.
- idx 4 and 5 blank: the Azurite Mine camp pin (48.68278,-120.78111) has a ground elevation of 4,442 ft against the 5,899 ft it claims, which puts the camp pin ~1,450 ft below the camp it names.
- idx 7 blank: summit pin conflict. The wp7 pin (48.68854,-120.75943) and approach_logistics.peakLat/Lng (48.686066,-120.757928) are ~280 m apart. The north benchmark point is lower, and the true summit is ~1,100 ft south. One of these two may sit on the north benchmark sub-summit, and the DEM cannot separate them (8,278 vs 8,281 ft). Not decided.
- DEFECT: wp5 basin distMi 14 and the note "roughly 3 more brush-choked miles above the mine" vs pins only ~0.7 mi apart. The "3 miles" in the approach prose is the push toward Azurite Pass, not the basin.
- Note: land manager lists Canyon Creek trail #754 as unmaintained, whereas the itinerary calls it "6 good miles".

## wa_mount_carru_scramble — 2 of 8 written (idx 3, 4)
- idx 0 blank: approach_logistics names the Monument Creek TH (Lost River Road), while the waypoints, approach prose and primary variant use the Slate Pass / Buckskin Ridge TH. Also, the wp0 pin (48.737833,-120.6785, ground 7,162 ft) is ~0.9 km up the Slate Peak road, not at the Slate Pass trailhead (48.7323,-120.6683, ~6,870 ft).
- idx 1 and 2 blank: the wp1 "Slate Pass" pin (48.744811,-120.659579) has a ground elevation of 5,839 ft against the 6,500 ft it claims. Real Slate Pass is ~6,870 ft and ~1.5 km SW, so the pin is on the wrong feature.
- idx 5 blank: the Shellrock Basin pin has a ground elevation of 6,118 ft against the 6,800 ft it claims. It appears to sit in the upper Eureka Creek meadows rather than the basin, and it is lower than the creek crossing (6,800) it is supposed to climb up from.
- idx 6 and 7 blank: the Lago–Carru saddle pin (48.82876,-120.53984) has a ground elevation of 8,406 ft against the ~7,600 ft col. It is ~800 ft high and on a slope, not the col.
- Note: the trip-report line from Slate Pass is down the West Fork Pasayten, while the row says Middle Fork. Not resolved (those legs are blank anyway).

<!-- b100.report.md -->
# b100 report

## wa_mount_lago_south_slope_south_face (5/5 written)
- All five written. Drive and leg 1 follow the row's own Robinson Creek approach; the Middle Fork junction sequence (Robinson Pass, Whistler Cutoff 1.2 mi below it, Freds Lake/Eureka Creek Trail junction 4.8 mi beyond) was confirmed online.
- DEFECT: the variant marked `primary` ("From Slate Pass down the Middle Fork Pasayten") starts at the Buckskin Ridge/Slate Pass trailhead, but waypoint 0 and `approach_logistics` start at Robinson Creek. The waypoints match logistics, so the drive goes to Robinson Creek.
- DEFECT: that same variant says to follow the Middle Fork "up-valley past the Berk Creek junction". The Middle Fork flows north, and Berk Creek is downstream (north) of the Slate Pass connector, so the correct direction is down-valley.
- Minor: the approach says to continue "past the Eureka Creek Trail junction (~5,190 ft) to Fred's Lake". Two Eureka Creek Trail junctions exist: one 0.5 mi north of the Whistler Cutoff and the Freds Lake turn 4.8 mi north. Read loosely the sentence is right, but it is ambiguous.
- Note: waypoint 4 is Carru's summit, typed `Junction`, on a Lago route. Its pin is correct for Carru. The leg into it is written as the common Lago-to-Carru traverse via the saddle.

## wa_ne_couloir (2/2 written)
- The drive was confirmed online: from Winthrop, East Chewuch Rd, then FR 37 about 13 mi, then left on FR 39 about 7.5 mi. The total of about 26.5 mi matches the row. Leg 1 follows the row's Trail #373, Honeymoon Pass, east-slope traverse and cirque.
- Minor: the itinerary and descent_text cite "the first-ascent report" / "The first-ascent party reported", which is source-naming in rendered prose.

## wa_ne_ridge (2/2 written)
- The drive follows the row and online sources: West Chewuch Rd 6 mi, then FR 51 about 22 mi to the end of the pavement (about 28 mi total). Leg 1 goes Andrews Creek #504 to Andrews Pass, then Spanish Creek, the Boundary Trail and the Cathedral Lakes basin, then the grassy SE slope onto the ridge.
- DEFECT (minor): in the Cathedral Driveway variant, the bivy `permit` text says the permit comes from "the kiosk at the Andrews Creek trailhead". It was copied from the Andrews variant and is wrong for a party starting at Toats Coulee/Cathedral Driveway (the Thirtymile variant has the same copy).

## wa_north_tower_ridge_route (2/2 written)
- Same drive as the NE Couloir. Leg 1 is the row's own approach plus the tower line to the summit. No defects beyond the shared Tiffany notes.

## wa_north_face_left_buttress (Castle Peak, Fight or Flight): 0 written, omitted
- Waypoint 0 is the Manning Park / Lightning Lake trailhead in BC, and its own name says "not a lawful US entry". Leg 1 walks across the international boundary cross-country. Writing a drive or leg would give directions for an unlawful crossing.
- DEFECT: two on-file fields disagree on the trailhead. `approach_logistics` names the Ross Dam Trailhead (48.7278, -121.0628), while waypoint 0 is in Canada. The primary variant is the lawful Ross Lake / Lightning Creek / Freezeout Creek line, so the waypoints encode the non-recommended approach.
- DEFECT: the bivy note on the glacier camp says "Most parties come in from Manning Park, leave the Monument 78 trail…". That presents the unlawful approach as the norm, and the trail it names (Monument 78) differs from the waypoint note (Frosty Mountain).
- Minor: the Harts Pass camp note carries a dated 2026 fire-closure claim.

## wa_ptarmigan_peak_pasayten_scramble (2/7 written: legs 2 and 3)
- Index 0 is blank, which also blanks leg 1. DEFECT: the pin (48.737833, -120.6785, 6,900 ft) sits on the Slate PEAK trailhead/gate (published coordinates 48.7380, -120.6787, about 7,200+ ft). The Slate PASS trailhead it names is at 48.7323, -120.6682, which matches the row's own `approach_logistics`. That is about 1 km off, on a different trailhead.
- Legs 4, 5 and 6 are blank. DEFECT: the "7,800-ft tarn" pin (48.85093, -120.53667) and the "South ridge gain point" pin (48.85010, -120.53544, 8,000 ft) both sit on the SOUTH-EAST flank of Dot Mountain (summit 48.8523, -120.5400, 8,224 ft). They are about 650 m south of the Ptarmigan-Dot saddle (48.8567, -120.5353, about 7,775 ft), which is where the row's baseFinding puts the tarn and the start of the south ridge. A "Ptarmigan south ridge" point south of Dot is on the wrong side of an intervening summit.
- DEFECT: the approach and descent_text put the Tatoosh Buttes junction (Trail #485) BEFORE the Berk Creek junction going down-valley ("passing … Tatoosh Buttes … Continue north to the Berk Creek junction"; on the way out, "south past Tatoosh Buttes"). Measured from the Whistler Cutoff, the Freds Lake/Eureka Creek turn is at 4.8 mi and Tatoosh Buttes at 9.6 mi, so Tatoosh is beyond (north of) Berk Creek.
- DEFECT: the Berk Creek junction waypoint has null lat/lng. Its 4,400 ft elevation conflicts with the Lago row's ~5,190 ft for the Eureka Creek junction. The valley drops from 5,280 ft at the Whistler junction to 4,280 ft at the Boundary Trail 11.6 mi on, so 4,400 ft at mile 4.8 looks too low. Its note also says the trail "forks north toward Freds Lake", but Freds Lake lies east of the valley.
- Minor: the approach calls the trail from Slate Pass "Slate Pass Trail (#498)". The actual sequence is the Buckskin Ridge Trail for 0.9 mi, then the Whistler Cutoff to the Middle Fork, and #498 is likely the Buckskin Ridge number. The approach also says "Turn north (right)", but coming down eastward, north is to the left.
- Approach to the tarn is mixed: the approach says "across the shoulder of Mount Lago (via the Lago–Carru col on some variations)", which is west of Lago and a detour, while the itinerary goes via Points 8207/8165 and Dot.

# b101 report

## wa_remmel_mountain_nw_ridge — 1 of 4 written (index 0)
- Index 0 written: the drive to the Thirtymile Trailhead, the trailhead this pin names and sits at.
- Legs 1-3 left blank because the waypoint array is spliced. Waypoint 1, Four Point Lake, has its own note saying it is "the usual camp for the Southeast Slope, not on the Remmel Lake approach to this ridge". Its lat/lng are also null.
- Defect, trailhead conflict: waypoint 0 is the Thirtymile TH, but `approach_logistics.trailhead` and the primary approach variant are Andrews Creek TH (#504). The waypoint 0 note says to take the Chewuch River Trail #510 from Thirtymile. Both are documented variants, but the primary disagrees with the pin array.
- Defect: `itinerary` and `approach` say "Bushwhack from Remmel Lake south". That fits the pins: the camp at 48.928 is north of the summit at 48.923, and Remmel Lake is north of the peak. No other defect found there.

## wa_remmel_mountain_southeast_slope — 1 of 4 written (index 0)
- Index 0 written: the drive to the Thirtymile Trailhead.
- Legs 1-3 left blank because of an ordering fault. Waypoint 1 is a second trailhead (Andrews Creek), so the array splices the two alternative approaches that the `approach` prose describes. A party never walks from Thirtymile to the Andrews Creek TH.
- Defect, road numbers: `approach` says Thirtymile is the "end of FR 5160" and Andrews Creek is the "end of FR 51". The land manager describes Thirtymile as reached on FR 51, about 29 miles from Winthrop. Andrews Creek is partway up that road, not at its end. I left out FR 5160.
- Note: `approach` says the summit is reached "up-valley" from a cairn near 7,070 ft. Not checked further.

## wa_robinson_mountain_southeast_ridge — 2 of 2 written
- Index 0: the drive via Lost River Road / FR 5400. It matches the row's own approach variant and the land-manager trailhead page.
- Index 1: trailhead to summit, built from the route's prose: #478, the Beauty Creek junction turning right, the tributary crossing, the tarn, the scree, the ridge northwest past Pt 8,280. Summit pin is NNW of the trailhead, which matches.
- Minor inconsistencies:
  - The junction distance is given as 2.5 in the variant and 2.8 in `approach`, so I wrote "2.5 to 2.8".
  - The trailhead elevation is 2,560 ft on the pin and 2,500 ft on the land-manager page.
  - `approach` option (b) says to take the "Harts Pass Road" 9 miles to the same trailhead. That probably describes the same Lost River Road / FR 5400 corridor and adds nothing. I did not use it.

- Defect: the base pin (48.99027, -120.18882) is about 110 m NORTH of the summit pin (48.98926). For a South Face base that is the wrong side. It is also the same coordinate as The Monk pins, and it is labelled "Cathedral Peak South Face / Monk area pin".
- Defect: the existing index 0 drive says Andrews Creek TH is "about 28 miles" from Winthrop. The two Monk rows say "about 23 miles" to the same trailhead, so the drive distances disagree.

## wa_the_monk_le_gibet — 0 written (omitted)

- Defect: the Topout and Base pins share one coordinate (48.99027, -120.18882), and that is also the Cathedral South Face base pin. The array is also missing an Upper Cathedral Lake camp.

## wa_the_monk_odine — 0 written (omitted)
