<!-- b102.report.md -->
# b102 report

## wa_the_monk_scabo: 0 written (omitted from JSON)
- Index 0 already had text, so it was left alone.
- Legs 1 and 2 left blank because the array is out of order. The waypoints run Trailhead, then "The Monk (tower top)" (Topout), then "Cathedral Peak South Face / Monk area pin" (Base). The base comes after the topout. Both later pins also sit on the identical coordinate (48.99027, -120.18882).
- Defect: the topout's distMi is 20, but the approach prose says about 18 miles one way. The base pin has no elevation.

## wa_the_monk_west_cracks_left_crack: 0 written (omitted)
- Same ordering fault as Scabo (base after topout, identical coordinates), so legs 1 and 2 were left blank.
- Defect: the climb is two pitches, and most parties rappel from the top of pitch 2. The "tower top" card therefore names a point the route usually does not reach.
- Defect: the topout card's note describes where the route starts ("left of the two West Cracks lines, lower left of the south face"). That is base information sitting on the topout card.

## wa_the_monk_west_cracks_right_crack: 0 written (omitted)
- Same ordering fault and identical coordinates, so legs were left blank.
- Defect: the topout card's note describes the start, not the top, the same as Left Crack.
- Defect: descent_text says "No descent from the top of the second pitch is documented." The baseFinding in the same row says most parties rappel the route on gear from the top of pitch 2. The row contradicts itself.

## wa_tower_arete: 2 of 2 written
- Index 0 is the drive to Tiffany Springs Campground, via FR 37 and FR 39 from Winthrop or Conconully, with mileages confirmed online.
- Leg 1 covers trail #373, Honeymoon Pass, the traverse south to the cirque, the arete with its left bypass of the vertical step, and the summit slope to the top.
- Small defect: the on-file prose says FR 37 approaches "from Winthrop (north)". Winthrop is to the west, and the drive goes north up East Chewuch Road first. The text written here does not repeat that error.
- Worth a look: the route_breakdown ends at "Tiffany's main slope" rather than the summit. Leg 1 continues up the summit slope to the top, as the summit card's own note describes ("joining the same upper ridge to the summit slope"). If the summit card should be the tower top instead, leg 1 should be cut back.
- Defect: the trailhead pin is named "Tiffany Springs Campground", but approach_logistics.trailhead says "Tiffany Lake Trail". They are the same place (the trail starts across FR 39 from the campground), so this is cosmetic only.

## wa_west_craggy_peak_standard_route: 3 of 4 written (index 0 already had text)
- Leg 1 goes from the trailhead to the creek crossing at 6,400 ft, past the mine, the lower ford and the lake.
- Leg 2 goes from the crossing to the camp knoll at 6,600 ft; the camp pin lies north-west of the crossing, which is consistent.
- Leg 3 goes from camp up the right-slanting chute to the south-ridge col, then to the summit.
- Possible ambiguity: the row mentions two crossings of Copper Glance Creek. One is low, near the mine, where the bridge has been out. The other is at about 6,400 ft in the basin. The "Copper Glance Creek crossing" water pin is at 6,400 ft and about 1.8 miles in a straight line from the trailhead, so it was treated as the upper crossing. The descent_text's "re-fording ... where the bridge is out" refers to the lower one.
- Hike pages put the trail's highest point at about 6,260 ft (the lake), so the crossing at 6,400 ft is presumably off-trail above the lake.

## wa_windy_peak_iron_gate_trail: 2 of 2 written
- Index 0 is the drive from Loomis via County Road 9425, Toats Coulee Road (County Road 4066) and FR 39, then FR 3900-500. The land manager's page confirms the roads, the mileages, the high-clearance note and the NWFP or day fee. The trailhead pin matches the land manager's coordinates (48.9085, -119.90401).
- Leg 1 follows the Boundary Trail #533 north to Sunny Pass, then the Windy Peak Trail west (keep left twice), then the summit spur.
- Defect: the trailhead pin has no elevation.
- Defect: the route_breakdown's only entry reads "All three trails converge before this same final easy scramble to the true summit." It refers to other routes and to "three trails" this route never describes, and the summit spur is a short trail, not a scramble.
- Status note, not written into the text: as of 2026-08-06, land-manager and hike pages list the Iron Gate trailhead as closed until further notice because of the Sinlahekin Fire.

# b103 report

## wa_windy_peak_trail: 1 written (index 1)
- Indices 0 and 2–5 already had text, so they were left alone.
- DEFECT: the pin for waypoint 3, "Windy Peak / Windy Creek Trail Junction" (48.92818,-119.97073, 7950 ft), is about 15 m from the summit pin (48.92820,-119.97056, 8335 ft). That puts the junction pin on the summit, yet it comes before the "Trail High Point" pin (48.92627), which is about 200 m south. The junction pin is very likely misplaced. Leg 3 was already written, so this was not changed.

## wa_wolframite_mountain_scramble: 4 written (indices 1–4)
- Leg 5 (to "Boundary Trail bend toward Apex Pass") and leg 6 (to the summit) were left blank. DEFECT: the bend pin (48.97833,-120.12488) is about 540 m EAST of the Tungsten Mine Camp pin (-120.13227). All the prose says to go about 1 mile WEST on the Boundary Trail toward Apex Pass. The prose then says to go "northeast" from the bend, but the summit lies to the NW of the bend pin. So the pin and the prose disagree.
- DEFECT: waypoints 1 (camp) and 2 (Tungsten Creek ford) have no lat/lng. The ford is at 4,400 ft and the junction is at 4,700 ft only 0.1 mi farther on, which does not add up.
- DEFECT: the approach says "turn right (northeast)" onto Tungsten Trail #534, but the mine camp pin is NW of the junction pin. My leg does not give a turn direction.
- Note: one online source puts a ford of Horseshoe Creek just before the Tungsten junction. A trip report mentions a camp about a quarter mile before a Tungsten Creek ford. Which creek the pre-junction ford crosses is not fully settled. I kept to the names on the cards.
- Note: the mileage to the junction varies between about 8 and 10 mi across sources. route_breakdown[0] names other trailheads (Cathedral Driveway, Iron Gate) mixed together with the Chewuch approach.

## wa_andersons_thumb_standard: 3 written (indices 1–3)
- Leg 4 (Flypaper Pass), leg 5 (Eel Glacier bergschrund) and leg 6 (Mount Anderson summit) were left blank.
- DEFECT (on-file fields disagree): the approach_variants baseFinding says the Thumb stands below and west of Flypaper Pass. It says that if you are in the notch with the Eel Glacier below you, you have "walked past it". The itinerary and descent_text instead send the party over Flypaper Pass and across the Eel Glacier to the spire.
- DEFECT: the last waypoint is "Mount Anderson summit" (47.7211,-123.3317), but this route climbs Anderson's Thumb (peakLat/peakLng 47.7159,-123.3418). The array ends at a summit the route never reaches. Waypoints 4–5 look like they came from the Mount Anderson standard route.
- Minor: the Flypaper Pass pin is at 6,500 ft, but the approach says 6,600 ft. Waypoint 0 says the closed road is about 5.5 mi; the land manager says the washout is 6.5 mi from the trailhead. My leg 1 gives no road mileage.

## wa_buckhorn_marmot_pass: 1 written (index 0, the drive)
- Legs 1 and 2 were left blank. DEFECT: the Marmot Pass pin (47.825049,-123.122453, 6000 ft) is about 70 m from the Buckhorn summit pin (47.825574,-123.121867, 6988 ft). The Marmot Pass pin sits on the summit, not at the pass, which lies to the south and about 1,000 ft lower.
- Minor: the trailhead pin is at 2,600 ft, but the approach says about 2,650 ft.

## wa_chimney_peak_the_chimney: 1 written (index 1)
- Leg 2 (chalet to summit) was left blank. The route's own prose says the off-trail line is not published in detail.
- Note: the index 0 drive, which was already written, contains a dated road-closure claim ("South Shore Road is currently closed at mile 8") and a phone number. It may go stale.

## wa_honeymoon_route: 2 written (indices 1–2)
- No conflicts between the pins and the prose. Royal Lake is NE of the summit, which matches the SW line.
- Minor: route_breakdown[0] calls Upper Royal Basin the "Shelter Rock area". Shelter Rock is a camp on the Big Quilcene and Dungeness side, so this looks like a slip; I did not use it.

<!-- b104.report.md -->
# b104 report

Ground elevations were checked against USGS EPQS (3DEP).

## wa_inner_constance_northwest_buttress (drive-only, 1 waypoint)
- Wrote: index 0, the drive. It matches the row's prose and the land manager's trailhead page (Louella Rd > Palo Alto Rd / FS 28 > FS 2880 > FS 2870 for 8.7 mi > across the Dungeness bridge). I left out the "1.1 mile to the forest boundary" figure because I think Palo Alto Road runs longer than that.
- Defect: the array has only a trailhead pin. There is no Home Lake, boulder camp or summit pin.

## wa_inner_constance_seans_route (2 waypoints)
- Wrote: index 0 (the same drive) and index 1 (trailhead to summit, through Home Lake, the boulder camp and the route, ending on the summit proper).
- Defect: this row's approach_variants says "the Constance Pass trail over into Olympic National Park and **down to** Home Lake". The NW Buttress row on the same peak says Home Lake sits about 500 ft **below** Constance Pass on the Dungeness side and the walk does not cross the pass. I followed the part both rows agree on ("the Constance Pass trail ... to Home Lake").
- Defect: approach_logistics is null even though a trailhead pin exists.
- Note: the summit pin has no distMi.

## wa_lena_lake_to_mt_stone_traverse (drive-only, 7 waypoints)
- Wrote: index 0 only (US 101 MP 318 > Hamma Hamma Rd FS 25, about 7.5 paved miles). The other entries are blank because of the ordering gate.
- Defects: St. Peter's Gate (index 4) has null lat/lng. Upper Lena Lake is pinned at 4,550 ft, but the approach puts it at about 4,000 ft of total gain from a trailhead at about 750 ft, so roughly 4,500-4,750 ft; the two are close but loosely consistent at best. The note on index 2 says the "maintained tread effectively ends here as the Scout Lake way-trail continues off-trail". The approach calls the stretch above Lena Lake a "steep, primitive tread", so the two describe that tread differently.

## wa_mount_anderson_eel_glacier (4 waypoints)
- Wrote: index 1 (road end to Anderson Pass). Index 0 was already filled.
- Blank: index 2 (into Flypaper Pass) and index 3 (out of it). **The Flypaper Pass pin is on the wrong feature.** It sits at 47.710261, -123.337461 with a stated 6,600 ft (prose says about 6,500). The ground there is **5,333 ft**, about 1,270 ft below the pass, which puts the pin down in the Anderson Glacier basin near the tarn. The pass is the notch between Echo Rock and the Anderson massif, higher and farther northwest.
- Minor: the itinerary's day-1 note says "over Anderson Pass to a high camp near Anderson Tarn or Camp Siberia". Camp Siberia is below the pass, on the Dosewallips side.
- The Anderson Pass pin checks out (ground 4,477 vs 4,464) and so does the summit pin (ground 7,336 vs 7,323).

## wa_mount_angeles_standard (6 waypoints)
- Wrote: index 1 (lot to the Sunrise Point spur junction) and index 5 (the crux step to the summit). Index 0 was already filled.
- Blank: indexes 2, 3 and 4, because two pins are badly off in elevation:
  - **Morse Creek overlook spur (index 2):** stated 6,050 ft, ground **5,157 ft** (about 900 ft off). A 6,050 ft point at mile 1.5 also cannot fit a trail that then drops to a 5,216 ft junction and a climbers' path the prose says tops out around 6,000 ft. The stated elevation, the pin, or both are wrong, and the "800 ft of climbing behind you" note depends on it.
  - **Mount Angeles trail fork (index 3):** stated 5,216 ft (consistent with the prose: about 6,000 minus 800), but the pin's ground is **4,771 ft**. It is about 445 ft low, probably off the trail.
- Other conflicts:
  - The Sunrise Point junction is at 0.5 mi on the pin but "about 0.3 mi" in both the approach and the itinerary. I wrote no distance.
  - The climbers' fork is "roughly 2.5 mi" in the approach and climbing_route but 2.3 mi in baseFinding and on the pin.
  - The prose says the climbers' path "splits left (west)", but the summit pin lies northeast of the fork pin. The itinerary says "head north".
  - The hazard pin's ground is 6,138 ft against a stated 6,410 (it is about 130 m from the summit). That is within tolerance for a short leg, so I wrote index 5.

## wa_mount_barnes_scramble (drive-only, 8 waypoints)
- Wrote: index 0 (US 101 about 29 mi west of Port Angeles > Sol Duc Hot Springs Rd about 14 mi to the road end).
- Defects:
  - The "High Divide crest near Bogachiel Peak" pin (index 2) comes after Heart Lake (index 1), but it lies west of Heart Lake (-123.777 vs -123.734) while the route travels east. Its note says the marker "sits on the peak itself", not at the departure point.
  - Childs Glacier (index 5) has null lat/lng.
  - The summit has no distMi.
  - The route_breakdown mentions "a camp near Bear Pass or in Elwha Basin", which mixes the two approaches.
  - The Bear Pass to Barnes geometry is plausible.

<!-- b105.report.md -->
# b105 report

Written: 21 entries across 6 routes.

## wa_mount_cameron_standard (6 wp): 4 written (legs 1-4)
- Index 0 was already filled, so it was left alone.
- **Leg 5 (to the summit) is blank because the row contradicts itself about which summit is the true one.** The waypoint is named "Mount Cameron - East Peak (true summit)", and Peakbagger also lists the 7,190 ft point as the East Peak. But the itinerary says the "main summit" is Class 1-2 up the west ridge, and that the Class 3-4 ground is "reserved for parties continuing the ridge to Cameron's Middle and East peaks". The variant's baseFinding also says Class 3-4 steps mean "you have passed it". Read that way, the true summit comes before the East Peak, so the prose and the card do not agree.
- The route name says "Southeast Slopes", but the row's own variant notes say this is a west-ridge climb from a pass that lies west of the summit (the notes warn about the name).
- The summit pin (-123.33409) is about 420 m west of Wikipedia's coordinate for the peak (-123.32845).
- Waypoint 3 (Cameron Basin camp) has no lat/lng.
- Cameron Pass elevation is given three ways: 6,448 ft (pin), 6,418 ft (route_breakdown) and 6,450-6,500 ft (approach).
- The Cameron Creek meadow crossings pin is at 5,900 ft, only 100 ft below the camp, even though the trail "drops steeply" from Grand Pass. That elevation looks doubtful but I did not verify it.

## wa_mount_carrie_se_route (3 wp): 2 written (legs 1-2)
- Index 0 was already filled.
- The route name is "Southwest Ridge…" but the id says `se_route`.
- Waypoint 1 "Cat Basin / High Divide" is typed Campsite, but the itinerary's camps are Heart Lake and Boston Charlie's. Cat Basin is not a stated camp.

## wa_mount_christie_west (8 wp): 7 written (legs 1-7)
- Index 0 was already filled.
- Low Divide's distMi is 15.3, while the prose says 16-16.7 (and 15.3-16.7 in the variant). I left mileage out of that leg.
- Waypoint 6 combines two places, "North Saddle / Christie Glacier Crossing", on one pin at the saddle. The leg into it ends at the saddle, and the glacier crossing is in leg 7.

## wa_mount_clark_standard (8 wp): 5 written (legs 1-5)
- Index 0 was already filled.
- **Legs 6 and 7 are blank because the Surprise Col pin is on the wrong side of the summit.** Mount Johnson is about 0.45 mi west of Clark, so the Clark-Johnson col sits west of Clark's summit, and Clark's position (0.9 mi south of Walkinshaw, 47.8486/-123.2372) matches the summit pin. But the col pin (47.834688, -123.228399) is about 250 m east-southeast of the summit pin, and the summit sits beyond the col in the direction of travel. The route description also fits a col to the southwest/west: from the col you skirt right on a southwest-facing ledge to a notch in the southeast ridge.
- Pins 4-7 (Upper Royal Basin, Surprise Basin snowfields, col, summit) lie on one straight line with equal steps, so they look interpolated rather than surveyed. The Surprise Basin snowfields pin (6,400 ft, on Clark's southeast flank) is suspect for the same reason. I wrote leg 5 because its text names no position, but the pin should be checked.

## wa_mount_claywood_standard (7 wp): 5 written (index 0, legs 1-4)
- **Legs 5 and 6 are blank because waypoint 5 contradicts itself.** It is named "Upper Cameron Basin high camp", which lies north of Cameron Pass, between Grand Pass and Cameron Pass. But it comes after Cameron Pass in the array, and its note puts it "beyond the pass" in the Lost Basin area at 5,450 ft. So either the name is wrong or the order is wrong. It has no coordinates to settle it. Lost Pass, where the climb actually leaves the trail, has no waypoint.
- Waypoints 3, 4 and 5 (Grand Pass, Cameron Pass, camp) have no lat/lng.
- In the itinerary, day 1 says "drop off-trail into the remote Lost River drainage" below Grand Pass, while the approach goes over Cameron Pass on trail. Day 3 says "head east, dropping into Cameron Basin and over Cameron Pass", which is the wrong order going out. The itinerary also climbs "Claywood's south ridge", while the approach and climbing_route go west-southwest to a step on the west side of the summit block.
- Grand Pass elevation is 6,488 ft here and 6,450 ft on the Cameron row. I left the elevation out of the legs.

## wa_mount_constance_finger_traverse (3 wp): 3 written (index 0, legs 1-2)
- Drive confirmed online: Dosewallips Road leaves US-101 at Brinnon and runs about 9.2 miles to the bermed washout. No pass is required to park.
- There is a small internal inconsistency: route_breakdown says the South Chute gains about 1,200 ft to a notch near 6,560 ft, while the approach says about 1,900 ft from the lake to 6,600 ft. Those can both be true if one counts from the lake and the other from the chute's foot. I left the figure out.
- The itinerary says the washout is "4-5 miles below the old TH", but the old trailhead is about 5.45 miles past the washout. That does not affect the legs.

# b106 report

## wa_mount_constance_north_chimney — 3/3 written
- 0 drive (Brinnon, Dosewallips Rd FR 2610, 8.5 mi to washout, NWFP/ATB), 1 roadbed + Lake Constance path, 2 lake to summit via South Chute, notches, traverse, North Chimney.
- Defect: `approach` calls the Lake Constance climbers' path "signed"; the sibling Constance rows call it "unsigned". The text avoids both words.
- Defect (minor): the `approach` says the washout is reached via "about 8.5 miles of FR 2610", which matches. The pin elev 550 vs prose "~600 ft" is minor.
- Note: this row puts the summit-block finish on the block's NORTH side (North Chimney). The terrible_traverse row's breakdown finishes up the block's SOUTHEAST corner. They may be different finishes, but the two rows describe the same summit block differently.

## wa_mount_constance_north_chute — 2/2 written
- Only TH + summit pins, so leg 1 compresses the whole line: road, lake trail, Avalanche Canyon past the South Chute to the North Chute, then the traverse and summit block.
- No defects beyond a missing Lake Constance waypoint, which its siblings have.

## wa_mount_constance_terrible_traverse — 3/3 written
- Defect (minor): the saddle drop is "~150 ft" in sibling rows and "about 175 ft" in this row's breakdown, so the text says "drop a little".
- Defect: `descent_text` says "no mandatory rappelling ... it is a walk-off/downclimb descent", but the summit block has an optional rap. It is consistent, just a loose use of "walk-off" on a route with fatal-fall downclimbing.

## wa_mount_constance_west_arete — 3/3 written
- Leg 2 goes from the lake via Avalanche Canyon to Crystal Pass and up the arete. There is no Crystal Pass pin, so the canyon direction could not be checked against pins. The summit pin is NE of the lake, which is consistent.
- Note: the `approach` says to stay "climber's-right ... passing below the Cat Ears" while `approach_variants` says to go around the lake's EASTERN shore. The north_chimney row says the NORTH shore. The text avoids naming a shore.

## wa_mount_cruiser_nw_face_corner — 1/4 written (drive only)
- Legs 1-3 left blank because the ORDERING is broken. "Northwest Face/Corner (route pin)" (index 3) comes AFTER the summit (index 2) and sits on the summit's exact coordinates.
- Index 1 "Base of Alpha (ridge gain)" has null lat/lng. Neither the row's approach nor the South Corner row's approach mentions Alpha; both describe Needle Pass and Beta Peak, so this junction's name is unsupported.
- Defect: the TH note says "Staircase area is currently closed post-2025 Bear Gulch Fire". The sibling South Corner TH note says the road and developed area reopened July 8, 2026, with wilderness trails still closed. The two notes contradict each other, and both are dated status claims. A current WTA page still shows the Flapjack Lakes trail closed "until further notice" due to Bear Gulch Fire damage.
- Defect: `route_breakdown` gives ~80 ft of climbing while the itinerary schedules 3 hours "6-9 AM" for it. The itinerary also routes the climb from a Gladys Divide/Needle Pass high camp, which the approach does not mention.

## wa_mount_cruiser_south_corner — 3/3 written
- Index 1 "Needle Pass" has null lat/lng, so the couloir bearing ("southeast" from just below Gladys Divide) could not be checked against a pin. That bearing comes from both the row's prose and an online route description.
- The trail mileage is 3.7-3.8 mi on NF Skokomish and 3.4-4.1 mi on Flapjack inside the row; the text uses about 3.7 and about 4.
- Closure: the trail beyond Staircase is closed after the Bear Gulch Fire (see above). The directions are written durably, without status claims.

## wa_mount_duckabush_standard — 0 written (omitted)

<!-- b108.report.md -->
# b108 report

## wa_mount_johnson_standard — 8 of 8 written
- All legs written from the row's approach / approach_variants / route_breakdown; drive confirmed against an online turn-by-turn (Louella Rd > Palo Alto Rd > FR 2880 > FR 2870).
- Defects / inconsistencies (not fixed):
  - Base camp disagrees across fields: Royal Lake note calls Royal Lake "Basecamp for the Johnson/Clark climb"; approach says most parties camp at Upper Royal Basin (~5,600 ft); itinerary day 1 camps "near Imperial Tarn at roughly 5,800 ft". Online sources put the upper basin at ~5,700 ft.
  - Approach says "From Royal Lake continue south up-valley" to Upper Royal Basin, but the pins run almost due west (47.8320,-123.2107 -> 47.8313,-123.2214). The leg was written without a compass direction.
  - Royal Creek Camp (i=2) and Narrow ledge traverse (i=6) have no coordinates.
  - Approach says the upper basin lies "below Mount Deception's northeast face" while the Upper Royal Basin note says it is below the north faces of Johnson and Clark — loose wording, worth a look.

## wa_mount_la_crosse_scramble — 4 of 6 written (drive + legs 1-3); legs 4, 5 blank
- Leg 4 (to La Crosse Pass) and leg 5 (pass to summit) left blank:
  - **La Crosse Pass pin is on the wrong spot.** Pin 47.6859,-123.2907 sits at the summit's latitude ~1.6 km EAST of Mount La Crosse; the pass is published at ~47.7038,-123.3150, roughly 1.3 mi NORTH of the summit (summit 47.6847,-123.3122 checks out). Pin is ~2.7 km off.
  - **The row contradicts itself about direction from the pass:** approach_variants baseFinding says leave the pass "north" and follow "the broad crest running north out of the saddle"; route_breakdown says the ridge runs "roughly a mile south from near LaCrosse Pass to the summit". The ground supports SOUTH (the summit is south of the pass), so the baseFinding prose (which renders) likely sends climbers the wrong way.
  - distMi is impossible: junction 16.7 -> pass 23.1 (6.4 mi, the full length of the La Crosse Pass Trail to the West Fork Dosewallips), while the same row says the Duckabush side climbs ~3,000 ft "in about a mile". The pass is only ~1.5 mi above the junction.
- La Crosse Pass Trail Junction (i=3) has no coordinates.
- **Two approaches spliced across fields:** the itinerary climbs the peak from La Crosse Basin / Marmot Lake near O'Neil Pass (an 11-mile day 2, "near Fisher's Notch"), not via La Crosse Pass as the waypoints, approach_variants and descent do. Total mileage (~48 mi RT) reflects the basin approach.
- Ten Mile Camp note says it is the "last established campsite before the trail turns south for La Crosse Pass" — the pass is north of the Duckabush.
- Trailhead elev 270 ft looks low for the Duckabush trailhead (I left it out of the text).
- approach_logistics peak coord (47.684656,-123.311864) differs slightly from the summit pin; harmless.

## wa_mount_lawson_standard — 6 of 8 written (drive + legs 1-5); legs 6, 7 blank
- Drive via North Shore Road (South Shore Road route used by some sources was avoided). Mileages confirmed with the park's trail log (Wolf Bar 2.5, Wild Rose 4.9, Elip 6.5, Sixteen Mile ~12.2).
- Legs 6 (to North Ridge Gully) and 7 (to summit) left blank: **the North Ridge Gully pin (47.6517,-123.6053) sits ~1.4 km WEST-SOUTHWEST of the summit (47.6563,-123.5885)**, i.e. on the west/southwest flank, while the row describes a gully on Lawson's north side gaining the North Ridge.
- Crossing point disagrees with online trip reports: the row puts the ford/leave-trail point at Sixteen Mile (~12.2 mi), but a climbing trip report crosses on a log ~10 mi up, "a few hundred feet below the big avalanche fan coming down from the north face". Itinerary says "~12 miles ... below Lawson's north-face avalanche fan" — conflating the two. Worth confirming where parties actually leave the trail. Ford waypoint (i=5) and Wild Rose ford (i=2) have no coordinates.
- Descent says re-ford "Elip and Wild Rose Creeks" — consistent; no other issues.

## wa_mount_lincoln_standard (drive-only) — 1 of 8 written (drive)

- Defects noticed:

The chockstone-gully pin (47.5443) is also south of the summit. Both look misplaced for a route that comes from Flapjack Lakes to the north.

  - permit field is null.

<!-- b109.report.md -->
# b109 report — waypoint directions

## wa_mount_mystery_standard — 5 of 8 written (idx 1-5)
- idx 0 left "": it already has text.
- idx 6, 7 left "": **pin on the wrong side of the peak.** Waypoint 6 is named "South Ridge notch", and descent_text calls it the "south ridge notch" (the high-route exit continues from it over Gunsight Pass, which is south of Mystery). But the pin (47.8018,-123.2197) is about 650 m **north** of the summit (47.7959,-123.2214, which matches the published summit coordinate). The legs into and out of it are blank.
- Minor: the existing idx-0 text says the trailhead is at "about 2,500 feet", but the pin says 2,600 ft. Junction to Royal Creek Camp is 2.4 trail-mi by distMi, but the straight line between the pins is about 2.5 mi. That is marginal, but it means the trail would be shorter than the straight line.
- The junction note says the "Maynard Burn Trail continues straight". I did not repeat that, because I could not confirm the trail layout at the footlog.

## wa_mount_norton_scramble — 3 of 6 written (idx 1-3)
- idx 0 left "": **waypoint 0 is Whiskey Bend, but the row's approach, itinerary and descent all say vehicles stop at Madison Falls.** The Olympic Hot Springs Road has been closed to vehicles at Madison Falls by a washout, and the current status pages still say so. A drive to Whiskey Bend would be false, and a drive to Madison Falls would lead to a different trailhead than the pin. The waypoint list is missing the Madison Falls start and the roughly 7-mi road walk.
- idx 4, 5 left "": **the route geometry contradicts the pins.** The prose says the climb leaves the Hayes River Guard Station camp straight up Norton's "east face", which rises 3,200 ft in under a mile, and the summit day is 4.2 mi round trip. But the guard station pin (47.8093,-123.4539) sits at the confluence about 3.7 mi NNW of the summit (summit pin correct, 47.7625,-123.4138). The off-trail pin (47.7865,-123.4343) is NW of the summit, on the north or northwest side, not the east face. The east face rises from the Hayes River **upstream** of the confluence, so a party would have to go up the Hayes drainage first, and nothing on file describes that leg.
- **Elkhorn distMi 14.3 is wrong.** The row's own itinerary (11.5 mi) and bivy notes ("about eleven miles") disagree with it, and so does the pin geometry: 2.2 mi straight-line from Mary's Falls at 8.8. I wrote no mileage into the Elkhorn leg.
- The prose's "Camp Wilder" placement varies: the itinerary has Elkhorn/Camp Wilder together, while the bivy notes put Camp Wilder past Hayes.

## wa_mount_noyes_standard — 2 of 2 written
- I wrote the drive to the North Fork Quinault trailhead because it matches waypoint 0 and approach_logistics. **Flag:** the approach, itinerary and descent all present Whiskey Bend/Elwha as an equal alternative, so check that this is acceptable under the different-trailhead rule.
- **The route_breakdown contradicts the variant.** The breakdown sends the party from Low Divide up the Skyline Ridge Trail over Promise Creek Bench (5,340 ft). The variant baseFinding says to cross the head of Seattle Creek from Low Divide or Seattle Basin up to the Noyes–Meany col. The approach also names "Noyes-Meany **or** Seattle-Noyes col". Leg 1 names neither the Skyline trail nor a specific col.
- There is only a trailhead pin and a summit pin, with no Low Divide camp pin, so leg 1 has to cover a roughly 20-mile, two-day route in one card.

## wa_mount_olson_standard — 8 of 8 written
- **Sundown Pass note and variant are wrong about the Six Ridge Trail junction.** The note says the pass is the "junction with the Six Ridge Trail heading east", and the variant prose says the same. The row's own itinerary and descent_text and an online trail description all agree: the trail goes past Lake Sundown and climbs to **Six Ridge Pass**, where the Six Ridge Trail begins. Leg 5 follows the itinerary and descent_text.
- The variant calls Lake Sundown "a short spur off the pass", but it lies on the through-trail about 0.3-0.4 mi beyond the pass. Leg 4 avoids the word "spur".
- Waypoints 1, 2, 5 and 6 have null coordinates and waypoint 0 has no elevation (the trailhead is about 1,100 ft), so legs 5-7 could not be checked against pins.
- Mileage conflict: the itinerary's day 1 is 8 mi to McGravey Lakes/Belview, but the variant puts Sundown Pass alone at 7.2 mi, and McGravey and Belview are well east along Six Ridge. The hazards list says "two unbridged fords", but only one ford is described or pinned.
- The summit is 5,292 ft by pin and 5,289 ft in the prose (trivial).

## wa_mount_olympus_blue_glacier — 4 of 4 written
- The approach prose orders "first 10.5 miles to Lewis Meadows ... continue past the Olympus Guard Station (~9 mi)", which lists the guard station after Lewis Meadow even though it is at a lower mileage. Leg 1 lists them in mileage order.
- Snow Dome (waypoint 2) has null coordinates.
- The approach says Glacier Meadows to the summit is "4-6 more miles", while the pin distMi gives 4.5 (17.5 to 22). That is consistent, just noted.

## wa_mount_olympus_west_ridge — 4 of 4 written
- **Snow Dome high camp distMi 17.45 equals the Glacier Meadows mileage.** Snow Dome is about 2 mi of glacier beyond the meadows, so it should be about 19.5. The coordinates are null.
- The itinerary has the party camping at Glacier Meadows and crossing to Snow Dome on summit day, while the waypoint and variant call Snow Dome the high camp. The leg text works either way.
- The variant says the crossing to the ridge toe is "essentially level / without gaining or losing much height". But Snow Dome is at 6,700-6,800 ft and the moat at 7,260 ft, about 500 ft of gain, so I left out the word "level".
- Worth a look: the "West Ridge" moat pin (47.8104,-123.7045) is about 1.1 km **northeast** of the West Peak summit. It matches the prose ("head southwest from Snow Dome"), but it is an odd toe position for a ridge named "west". I did not block on it.

# b110 report: waypoint directions

## wa_mount_pershing_standard: 6 of 7 written
- [0] left "" because it already had text.
- [1]-[6] are taken from the row's own notes and baseFinding. The pins step west and upward in a consistent order.
- Defect: the existing [0] text, plus `approach`, carry a dated status claim ("briefly closed during the 2025 Hamma Fire, though that closure has since been rescinded"). The brief says to avoid this kind of claim.

## wa_mount_pulitzer_standard: 2 of 2 written
- [0] is the drive to the Sol Duc road end. The pin matches the Sol Duc trailhead.
- [1] sums up a multi-day leg in one card: Sol Duc River Trail, Heart Lake, High Divide, the Catwalk, the Bailey Range cross-country, Ferry Basin, the Ferry-Pulitzer col, and the east ridge.
- Defect: the array has only 2 pins for a roughly 20-mile, 2-3 day approach. It has no Heart Lake, Catwalk or Ferry Basin camp pins, although the itinerary names them all.
- Defect: `route_breakdown` says parties "did not reach the true summit", and `descent_text` reverses "from the false summit". The summit card does not say there is a false summit.

## wa_mount_queets_south: 6 of 6 written
- [3]-[5] avoid naming a compass ridge because of the conflict below.
- Defect: the route is called "South Slopes", and its prose calls the climb the "SE ridge". But the Dodwell-Rixon Pass pin is about 2.5 km due NORTH of the summit, and the hazard pin lies between them. The ridge climbed from the pass is the NORTH ridge, which is the standard YDS 2 line on the peak. The route name, the waypoint [4] name ("SE ridge ...") and the prose are mislabelled. The legs I wrote follow the pins, which fit the north ridge with its east-side scree traverse.
- Defect: the Elwha Basin waypoint [2] has lat/lng null.
- Defect: the date of the Elwha road closure differs between rows. This row says "May 2021 washout", while wa_mount_seattle_south says "closed since November 2017". Not resolved here.

## wa_mount_seattle_south: 2 of 4 written ([0] drive, [1] to Low Divide)
- [2] and [3] left "" because of a pin on the wrong feature. A trip report on this exact itinerary (Skyline trail from Low Divide, which drops to Seattle Creek and then climbs about 1,000 ft to Seattle Meadows at 4,550 ft) puts Seattle Meadows on the south/southwest flank, west of Low Divide. The route then climbs scree on the peak's WEST side and crosses a ridge to the east side. The "Seattle Meadows" pin (47.7273, -123.5645) sits only about 0.75 km west of the Low Divide pin, about 1 km EAST-southeast of the summit, on the slope directly above Low Divide. It is very likely not at the meadows.
- Defect: the summit-route prose conflicts with itself. `baseFinding` and `descent_text` say west-side scree, then cross a ridge, then east side, then the east ridge. Itinerary day 3 says "ascend talus on the peak's eastern side". `route_breakdown` [2] says west slope, then the SW couloir, then the "south ridge/east side to the (slightly higher) south summit".
- Defect: the waypoint [2] note says "per route beta". That is source/pipeline voice on screen.
- Note: South Shore Road is reported washed out, so the drive uses North Shore Road, which matches the pin.

## wa_mount_skokomish_standard: 3 of 3 written
- The waypoints (Putvin TH, Lake of the Angels, summit) agree with the standard approach and with the pins.
- Defect: the Putvin trailhead is placed wrongly. The waypoint [0] note, `approach` and the variant say "just past the lower Lena Lake trailhead across a concrete bridge". The trailhead is about 4.5 miles past the Lena Lake trailhead: Lena Lake is at about 7.5 mi, where the pavement ends, and Putvin is at about 12 mi. My [0] drive gives the mileages instead.
- Defect: the primary approach_variant still says "the route's waypoint list describes a different one", and the Mildred variant says it is "recorded because the route's own waypoints describe this approach". Both are stale now that the waypoints follow Putvin, and both are meta-commentary that reaches the screen.
- Defect: the Mildred variant's hazards cite a "Bear Gulch Fire closure area", which is a dated status claim.

## wa_mount_steel_first_divide: 1 of 1 written (drive-only)
- [0] is the Staircase drive (SR 119, FR 24, right at the junction, 1.2 mi to the ranger station). The pin matches Staircase.
- Note: the North Fork Skokomish trail is currently listed as closed between the Staircase Rapids loop and Home Sweet Home after the Bear Gulch Fire. That leg is not part of this card, and I made no status claim.

<!-- b111.report.md -->
# b111 report

## wa_mount_steel_standard — 1 of 7 written (index 0 drive)
- Legs 1-6 left blank: **ordering fault.** The array runs junction (18.3 mi) -> hazard "below Marmot Lake" -> Marmot Lake (21.3) -> Amphitheater, and the Amphitheater note itself says "From camp, drop back roughly 3 mi to the creek junction". The lake is listed after the turnoff, and the climb goes back from it. The row's own prose also disagrees on the route: the primary variant's baseFinding says "if you ... arrive at Marmot Lake you have walked past the creek", while the itinerary camps at Marmot Lake and "leaves the trail near camp".
- Elevation defect: the hazard "Steep push below Marmot Lake" (3,700 ft, 20.3 mi) is lower than the junction before it (3,900 ft, 18.3 mi), yet its note says the stretch "gains elevation fast".
- Ten-Mile Camp distMi 10.7 vs itinerary 10 mi (minor).
- `permit` field contains "Source: NPS Olympic National Park fees/wilderness-reservations pages." This is a source citation in a field that may render.
- Drive checked against a trail-guide page (Duckabush Rd, pavement ends 3.6 mi, bear right onto 2510-060 at about 6 mi).

# b112 report

## wa_mount_wilder_scramble (drive-only): 1 of 7 written
- Index 0: the drive to Madison Falls, then the ~7 mi walk or bike to Whiskey Bend. The NPS road-conditions page still lists Olympic Hot Springs Road and Whiskey Bend Road as closed to vehicles because of the washout, so the closure is long-standing and still in force. Indexes 1-6 are blank (drive-only mode).
- DEFECT, prose contradicts pins: the approach says to climb "north/northeast" from Camp Wilder and that Camp Wilder sits "directly below the peak's steep south face". The pins put the summit (47.7677,-123.5198) about 4.5 km due WEST of Camp Wilder (47.7667,-123.4601), at nearly the same latitude. Upper Elwha Basin and the scree pins are also about 4.7 km west. Either the direction in the prose is wrong or the camp pin is.
- Hayes River Guard Station and Camp Wilder are typed `Junction`, not `Campsite`.

## wa_mount_worthington_standard: 8 of 8 written
- The drive follows Louella Rd, Palo Alto Rd / FS 28, FS 2880, then FS 2870 for 12.5 mi. A recreation pass is required (confirmed).
- DEFECT, Silver Creek footlog pin: hiking descriptions put the crossing "shortly after" the trailhead. The pin is about 0.9 mi (1.46 km) in and at 4,100 ft, 800 ft above the 3,300 ft trailhead. That would leave only 270 ft of gain over the next 2.1 mi to the mine at 4,370 ft on a trail that climbs evenly. The elevation and position look wrong; the leg text does not depend on them.
- DEFECT, minor: the "Buckhorn Lake basin" campsite pin (47.838028,-123.11322) sits on the lake itself (published lake coordinate 47.837982,-123.113284, about 5,113 ft). Its note says the camp is 200 ft above and south of the lake, yet the pin's elevation is 5,300 ft.

- Note: the itinerary describes a different upper line (gully straight to a rock band, then right onto the plateau, no col). The approach and climbing_route go via the Iron-Worthington col. The pins follow the col line and then the rock band near the summit, and I followed the pins and the approach.

# b113 report

## wa_se_ridge_aka_shield_wall (2 waypoints) — 2 written
- [0] drive (SR-119 9.4 mi to the T, right on FR 24 1.5 mi, left on FR 2419 6.6 mi to the road end; NW Forest Pass). Matches the row's own approach_variants and the pin (road-end Mount Washington TH, 3,026 ft).

## wa_sundial_northeast (8 waypoints) — 0 written (index 0 already filled)
- Legs 1-7 left blank: **ORDERING FAULT.** Shelter Rock (index 2) is listed BEFORE Royal Lake (index 3), and its note says it lies "just below Royal Lake". On the ground it is PAST the lake: the trail reaches Royal Lake at 7.2 mi, then passes Shelter Rock, then a footlog, then Arrowhead Meadow, then Upper Royal Basin. The Shelter Rock note tells a climber the wrong thing, and the pin has no coordinates.
- Pin defect (probable): Arrowhead Meadow (47.8347, -123.2160) and the Upper Royal Basin tarns (47.8376, -123.2204) are placed NORTH-west of Royal Lake (47.8320, -123.2107). Upper Royal Basin lies upstream, SOUTH/south-west of the lake (Royal Creek flows north). The sibling row wa_the_incisor_scramble puts Upper Royal Basin at 47.8299, -123.2194, which is south-west of the lake. The two rows disagree about where the same basin is.
- The note on index 1 ("after the first easy mile along the Dungeness River") is fine.

## wa_the_brothers_traverse (1 waypoint, drive-only) — 1 written
- [0] US 101 14 mi north of Hoodsport, left on Hamma Hamma River Road (FR 25) at MP 318, about 7.5 paved miles to the Lena Lake TH; NW Forest Pass. Consistent with the row's trailhead and pin.
- Note (not used): route_breakdown[0] aims for a notch "in the South Brother's southeast ridge at about 6,100 ft" and then the North Brother, while approach/baseFinding describe reaching the North Peak side via a wide gully north from camp. The breakdown's first pitch wording ("South Brother's southeast ridge") reads oddly for a north-to-south traverse that starts on the North Brother. Worth a look.

## wa_the_fin_scramble (7 waypoints) — 0 written (index 0 already filled)
- Legs 1-6 left blank: **SPLICED ARRAY.** The route leaves the Gladys Divide trail near 4,400 ft, SHORT of the divide, and heads SE to Sawtooth Pass. Yet the array lists Gladys Divide (index 3) and Needle Pass (index 4) as stops, and the Needle Pass note itself says "this route does not cross it". Sawtooth Pass, the actual key point, has no waypoint.
- Pin geometry vs prose: the baseFinding says The Horn is one tower SE of The Fin ("turn back northwest one tower"). The Horn row's summit pin (47.5582, -123.3211) is SSW of The Fin pin (47.5595, -123.3203), and only about 150 m away. That is close enough to be within pin error, but it is worth checking.
- Spike Camp junction pin (47.5462, -123.3761) sits about 2.7 km WEST of Flapjack Lakes. That is plausible for the river trail, but unverified.

## wa_the_horn_scramble (8 waypoints) — 0 written (index 0 already filled)
- Legs 1-7 left blank: **ORDERING FAULT.** "Black and White Lakes junction waterfall" (index 3) is listed AFTER Flapjack Lakes (index 2). Its own note says it is "roughly half a mile below Flapjack Lakes", which means it is passed on the way UP. Its pin (47.5535, -123.3224) is also about 1.5 km ESE of Flapjack Lakes, up at the Sawtooth crest. That is not on the trail below the lakes, so the pin is on the wrong feature.
- Pins 3-6 carry interpolated-looking coordinates (e.g. 47.553507352941175), so they are probably synthetic. The "~4,400 ft pond" pin (47.5549, -123.3220) sits SOUTH of Sawtooth Pass and right under the ridge. The pond is on the Gladys Divide trail NE of the lakes, so this pin is likely wrong.
- Geometry conflict: Sawtooth Pass (47.5568) is placed SOUTH of both The Horn (47.5582) and The Fin (47.5595). Yet the route traverses from the pass "under The Fin's west face" to a notch south of The Horn, and The Horn is described as SE of The Fin. With these pins, The Fin lies beyond The Horn when seen from the pass, so the traverse under The Fin cannot happen in the order written.
- Index 0 elevation is 875 ft, but the row's approach says ~800 ft and the Fin row puts the same trailhead at 785 ft.

## wa_the_incisor_scramble (8 waypoints) — 7 written (index 0 already filled)
- [1]-[7] written from the row's approach plus the Royal Basin trail sequence (junction a few steps before the Royal Creek footlog; Maynard Burn junction; park boundary; talus; campsites; Royal Lake 7.2 mi; Shelter Rock; footlog; Arrowhead Meadow; unbridged stream; Upper Royal Basin ~5,700 ft).
- Defects:
  - Index 1 (Royal Creek / #832 junction) elev 2200 is BELOW the trailhead's 2500, but the trail climbs upstream from the trailhead. The elevation is wrong.
  - Index 2 (Maynard Burn junction) has distMi 2.5 and elev 3000. That junction is only about 0.2 mi up Trail #832 from index 1, so it is about 1.2 mi from the trailhead, not 2.5. The pin itself, about 0.5 km from index 1, is consistent with 0.2 mi. The distMi and elevation are what is wrong.
  - Index 3 has null coordinates.
  - The Incisor's elevation is listed as 7,440 ft (and at least one other listing shows 7,350 ft). I left the leg text free of a number.
  - approach_logistics names the trail "#833.2", while the waypoint names "#833". This is cosmetic.

<!-- b115.report.md -->
# b115 report

All 6 routes written; 11 entries in total, with none left blank.

- **wa_castle_peak_tatoosh_la_villa** (2/2): the drive plus the leg to the summit. The leg ends at a ledge about 10 ft left of the true summit, then a step right to the top, which matches the route's own descent prose.
  - Defect: `approach` carries a dated status claim, the 2026 Plummer Peak Fire closure ("effective August 31, 2026 until further notice"), which says Castle's south side is inside the closed area. It will go stale. `approach_variants` does not mention it, and the waypoint text leaves it out.
- **wa_castle_peak_tatoosh_southeast_face** (2/2): the same dated fire-closure paragraph is in `approach`.
  - Minor: `approach` calls Pinnacle Saddle "just south of Pinnacle Peak". The saddle sits between Pinnacle and Plummer, so it is southwest or west of Pinnacle's summit.
  - Minor: La Villa's summit pin (46.75745, -121.72859) and this route's summit pin (46.757752, -121.728328) are about 40 m apart for the same summit.
- **wa_classic_route_2** (2/2): the drive was confirmed with land-manager and trail pages (the trailhead is 1.5 mi east of Reflection Lakes, about 3 mi from the Paradise junction).
  - Minor: `approach` says "about 1 mile... to Snow Lake". The trail is about 1.25 mi to Snow Lake, which is what the Fantasy Falls row says.
  - Minor: "skirt the west/north shore" is vague, so the leg does not use it.
- **wa_east_ridge_8** (2/2): the waypoint 0 pin (46.7682, -121.7312) and `approach_logistics.trailheadLat/Lng` (46.76861, -121.72969) are about 130 m apart. Both are at Reflection Lakes, so this is not a problem.
  - Minor: `approach` says about 1,500 ft of gain for the trailhead-to-col segment. That does not fit a 4,880 ft trailhead and a col at roughly 6,000-6,200 ft (about 1,100-1,300 ft).
- **wa_fantasy_falls** (1/1, drive-only): drive written.
- **wa_frying_pan_whitman_glaciers** (2/2):
  - Defect: `approach` has a dated status claim: the Fryingpan Creek trailhead lot and spur trail close from late September 2026 for the bridge project (2027-2029). The current notice from the National Park Service (NPS) closes the lot and spur trail only through Oct 31, 2026, during preparation work, with the Wonderland Trail reached from White River Campground. Main construction runs 2027-2029, and closures in those seasons are not yet stated. The row's wording ("for the length of the project") goes further than the notice. The drive text leaves the closure out.
  - Minor: the waypoint 0 note says "Wonderland Trail junction near the White River entrance". The trailhead is about 3 mi past the entrance, at the Fryingpan Creek bridge.
  - The waypoint 1 note says "glacier permit required". The `permit` field says climbing registration, so the two use different terms.

# b116 report

## wa_gilbert_peak_conrad_glacier: 0 of 2 written (route omitted from the JSON)
- **Defect: the trailhead pin is on the wrong feature.** Waypoint 0, "Trailhead (Conrad Meadows / South Tieton area)", is at 46.5164,-121.263. The land manager's Conrad Meadows / South Fork Tieton trailhead is at 46.508793,-121.280865, 4,044 ft, which is the same pin the sibling Meade Glacier route uses. The two points are about 1.6 km apart (NE, down the valley), so I left both the drive and the leg out of that waypoint blank.
- The waypoint has no elevation, and neither waypoint has a distMi.
- There are only 2 pins (trailhead and summit), and nothing marks the Barren Lake, Conrad Glacier or 7,200 ft saddle sequence that the climbing_route describes.

## wa_gilbert_peak_meade_glacier: 2 of 2 written
- The trailhead pin matches the land-manager coordinates exactly. The drive matches the row's approach_logistics.
- No defects found.

## wa_gilbert_peak_west_route: 1 of 2 written (index 0 blank)
- **Defect: the row names two different trailheads.** Waypoint 0 is "Berry Patch Trailhead" and its pin (46.4669,-121.5264) does sit at Berry Patch, about 0.4 mi past the Snowgrass lot. But approach_logistics.trailhead is "Snowgrass Flat Trailhead", and the approach says "Berry Patch or Snowgrass Flat". Berry Patch is a separate trailhead (the Trail 95 start, on spur 2150-040), not another name for Snowgrass (the Trail 96 start, on spur 405). Under the brief I wrote no drive.
- I wrote leg 1 neutrally ("Join Snowgrass Trail #96 ..."), and every route field agrees on it: route_breakdown, approach and descent.
- Minor: the route_breakdown says "PCT southbound (heading east here)" to Cispus Basin. I left the compass heading out.

## wa_ives_peak_r1: 7 of 7 written
- The trailhead pin matches the published Snowgrass Trailhead coordinate (46.4642,-121.5190). Legs 1-3 agree with the published trail log: the Goat Creek bridge just under 2 mi, the bypass on the right at about 3.5 mi, the Lily Basin #86 junction at 4.1 mi, and the PCT less than a mile and about 400 ft beyond.
- **Defect: the trailhead alias is wrong.** approach and approach_logistics say "Snowgrass Trailhead (aka 'Berry Patch')". Berry Patch is a different trailhead about a quarter mile further along FR 2150, and the waypoint 0 note correctly calls it the overflow lot.
- **Defect: the Lily Basin junction elevation looks wrong.** Waypoint 2 is stored at 6,250 ft, but the published elevation of the Lily Basin #86 junction at lower Snowgrass Flat is about 5,800 ft. That leaves only 150 ft between it and the PCT junction pin (6,400 ft), while the published climb between the two is 400+ ft.
- Possible defect: the Goat Creek crossing (waypoint 1) is stored at 5,400 ft. The trail drops to the creek from a 4,650 ft trailhead and then climbs 1,100 ft over 2 miles to reach 5,800 ft, which puts the creek nearer 4,700 ft. Worth a terrain check.
- Note: the itinerary describes a different line (over Old Snowy's summit, then down its south ridge to the col). The approach and the pins follow the direct line from the PCT to the col. The two are not contradictory, but they are not the same plan. My legs follow the approach and the pins.

## wa_lane_peak_r1 (Zipper): 1 of 2 written (leg 1 blank)
- **Defect: the topout pin sits on the summit.** The waypoint "Lane Peak - Zipper couloir" (type Topout) is at 46.75795,-121.75298, 6,012 ft. That is the peak's own summit coordinate and summit elevation (peakLat/Lng 46.757985,-121.752965). The Zipper tops out at the dead-tree notch on the EAST ridge, below the summit. The leg would have to end at a point the pin is not on, so I left it blank.

## wa_lane_peak_r2 (Fly): 1 of 2 written (leg 1 blank)
- **Defect: the same summit pin as the Zipper.** The "Lane Peak - The Fly couloir" Topout has the identical pin, which is the summit at 6,012 ft. The Fly tops out at the WEST-ridge notch. Two couloirs that finish on opposite ridges cannot share one point, so leg 1 is blank.
- Drive for both Lane routes: the trailhead pin and the approach_logistics agree (Reflection Lakes / Pinnacle trailhead, with Narada Falls as the winter start).

<!-- b117.report.md -->
# b117 report

## wa_lane_peak_r3 (2/2 written)
- 0: drive Nisqually Entrance -> Narada Falls lot (winter gate/chains). 1: whole route (Reflection Lakes road, under Denman, first funnel, couloir, rappel into Zipper, summit).
- Defects: none found. Pins consistent (summit S-SW of the lot).

## wa_liberty_cap_ptarmigan_ridge_finish (4/4 written)
- 0: the Mowich Lake drive is written with the closure stated (SR 165 Fairfax Bridge permanently closed April 2025; NPS: no vehicle access until further notice). Reviewer may prefer "" here, since nobody can drive this today.
- 1: Spray Park -> Flett Glacier -> saddle E of Observation Rock -> crest -> high camp -> Mowich Glacier schrund -> face -> Liberty Cap. 2: Liberty Cap -> crater rim. 3: Emmons-Winthrop down to Camp Schurman.
- Defects:
  - Waypoint 2 NAME is pipeline commentary: "Columbia Crest (historic high point; overtaken by the crater's southwest rim per an Aug. 2024 GPS survey as the icecap thins)". It should be just a place name. Its elev "14389" also matches neither the historic 14,411 nor the 2024 survey's ~14,399.6.
  - The primary baseFinding says Columbia Crest "is not part of this climb", but the waypoint list routes through it. That reads as an optional traverse, not a contradiction.
  - Index 0 has no elev. Elevations are stored as strings ("14112") on some pins and as numbers on others.
  - Trailhead (Mowich) and exit (White River via Schurman) are different by design (traverse); itinerary agrees.

## wa_little_tahoma_cowlitz_ingraham_glaciers (2/2 written)
- 0: drive to Paradise. 1: Muir route to 8,600, east traverse through the Cathedral Rocks notch, across the Ingraham to the ramp, gully/chimney to the summit. The glacier above the ramp is deliberately not named (see the conflict below).
- Defects (prose conflict, not resolved in text):
  - approach_variants.notes says the route "exits the upper Frying Pan Glacier's corner". route_breakdown[2] says the glacier above the couloir is the Whitman ("From the Paradise side this is the Whitman Glacier"). The Fryingpan is on the east side, so the variant note is likely wrong.
  - The baseFinding says "If you are looking for a notch between two glaciers with the Whitman below you, you are on the other route". But route_breakdown[1] has this route gain "the saddle between the Ingraham and Whitman glaciers" and then climb the Whitman. These contradict each other.

## wa_little_tahoma_east_shoulder (1/8 written; all legs blank)
- 0: drive SR 410 -> White River Entrance -> 3 mi to the Fryingpan Creek bridge lot. It includes the NPS-confirmed closure: the Summerland trailhead closed Sept 28 2026 for the bridge replacement, project through late 2029; the alternative is the Wonderland Trail from White River Campground.
- Legs 1-7 are blank because of an ORDERING FAULT. "Little Tahoma Peak" summit (i=6, 11,138 ft, 7.54 mi) comes BEFORE "East shoulder saddle" (i=7, 10,800 ft, 7.8 mi). The shoulder is reached on the way up, before the summit.
- Other defects:
  - Summerland (i=2) has null lat/lng.
  - "Toe of Fryingpan Glacier" (i=3) is at 7,900 ft, but the prose puts high camp/Meany Crest at 7,000-7,500 ft and the glacier step ~300 ft above it. The itinerary says high camp is at "~7,900 ft" and calls it "near Whitman Crest", which disagrees with the approach's 7,000-7,350.
  - Fryingpan Creek crossing pin is at 5,200 ft / 3.2 mi, yet the prose says ~1,400 ft of gain from a 3,800 ft start (~5,200 ok). Summerland at 5,950 is only 750 ft higher; plausible, just noted.
  - Trailhead elevation is given as 3,800, 3,816 and 3,900 in different fields.
  - Trailhead distance is "2.9 mi past the entrance turnoff" in the note but "past the entrance station" in approach_logistics. NPS says 3 mi past the entrance.
  - The approach prose's dated closure sentence ("from late September 2026... 2027-2029") matches NPS.

## wa_mount_adams_lava_glacier_headwall (2/2 written)
- 0: Randle FR 23 -> FR 2329 past Takhlakh Lake (~6 mi), or from Trout Lake (USFS: ~23 mi FR 23 + ~5 mi FR 2329). 1: Trail #113 -> PCT -> High Camp Trail #10 -> lava fields past the North Ridge -> Lava Glacier -> headwall -> summit.
- Defects: approach_logistics peakLat/Lng (46.202621, -121.4906384) differs slightly from the summit pin (46.202494, -121.490746), by about 15 m, which is trivial. The itinerary day 1 says high camp is "below the headwall, between the North Ridge and Lava Ridge", while the approach/variant camp at High Camp / Adams Creek Meadows (6,900 ft). That is two different camp locations.
- Mileage note: USFS gives FR 2329 as ~5 mi, other sources ~6 mi from the FR 23 junction. I wrote "about 6" for the Randle side. The Trout Lake figure (~24 mi) is the USFS one.

## wa_mount_adams_lyman_glacier (2/2 written)
- 0: same drive as the Lava headwall. 1: #113 -> PCT -> #10 -> High Camp -> east/southeast past the Lava Glacier toe and Lava Ridge -> Lyman base 8,000 ft -> crux chute -> summit.
- Defects: the itinerary objective says "North Lyman Glacier" while the climbing_route allows either lobe (minor). There is no route_breakdown (null).

<!-- b118.report.md -->
# b118 report

**wa_mount_adams_mazama_glacier_headwall** (4 waypoints): 3 written (legs 1-3). Index 0 already had text, so it was not touched.
- Defects: Sunrise Camp's card says 8,500 ft, but every prose field says about 8,300 ft. The leg avoids stating either. The "Mazama Glacier Headwall" junction is set at 11,600 ft, while climbing_route puts the headwall at about 11,300 ft, which looks like the top of the headwall. The leg says "around 11,300 ft veer right onto the headwall". Sunrise Camp and the Headwall have no coordinates (lat/lng null). The existing index-0 drive contains a dated, status-like claim ("closed outright in some years"); it is acceptable but worth knowing about.

**wa_mount_adams_north_ridge** (4): 2 written (the drive and the leg to High Camp). Legs 2 and 3 left blank.
- DEFECT, pin on the wrong feature: "North Ridge Base" (46.275, -121.52, claims 8,000 ft) has a USGS ground elevation of **5,211 ft**. It sits about 8 km north of the summit, in forest between the trailhead and the PCT junction, so it is short of and north of High Camp. That is not the foot of the ridge, which lies south-east of High Camp. Legs into and out of it are blank. High Camp has no coordinates. The approach says "High Camp" is 6,900 ft but the card says 7,000. That is a minor mismatch.

**wa_mount_adams_northwest_ridge** (1, drive-only): 1 written (Killen Creek drive). The route's name is "North Ridge (West Face)" but its id is northwest_ridge, a possible naming mismatch. Its route_breakdown is three stub sentences.

**wa_mount_adams_south_climb** (2): 2 written (the drive, and the full trailhead-to-summit leg drawn from the row's own prose). No defects noticed. The trailhead pin (46.136024, -121.495265) and approach_logistics (46.1359, -121.4977) agree to within about 200 m.

**wa_mount_adams_wilson_glacier_headwall** (3, drive-only): 1 written (Cold Springs drive).
- DEFECT, the row contradicts itself about the approach: approach and approach_variants say Round the Mountain Trail EAST from the ~1 mi junction, explicitly NOT toward the Lunch Counter ("if you are climbing toward the Lunch Counter... this route is already behind you"). But the itinerary day 1 camps at the Lunch Counter, and day 2 traverses from there toward the Roosevelt Cliffs. The descent also conflicts: descent_text says the South Climb, while the itinerary offers "North Ridge (more direct)". The approach also says the Round the Mountain Trail goes "onto Yakama Nation land... ends at Hellroaring Canyon", which needs a tribal permit that the permit field does not mention.

**wa_mount_rainier_curtis_ridge** (3, drive-only): 1 written (White River drive).
- DEFECT, ordering: the summit (Columbia Crest) is index 1 and Camp Schurman (the descent camp) is index 2. There are no pins for Glacier Basin, St. Elmo Pass or a Curtis Ridge camp.

<!-- b119.report.md -->
# b119 report — Mount Rainier (6 routes)

## wa_mount_rainier_edmunds_headwall — 2/2 written
- [0] Mowich Lake drive (SR 165, Fairfax Bridge, Mowich Lake Road). It states the indefinite closure of the Fairfax Bridge (closed to all traffic since 22 Apr 2025, no public detour; the NPS directions page still lists Mowich as inaccessible). Consider whether a drive to an unreachable trailhead should show at all.
- [1] One leg covers the whole route, Mowich Lake to Liberty Cap.
- Defect (minor): approach_variants says "the Wonderland Trail through Spray Park". The Spray Park Trail is an alternate to the Wonderland and not the Wonderland itself. I wrote "Spray Park Trail".
- Note: the waypoint 0 note and the approach also describe a trailhead that currently has no public road access.

## wa_mount_rainier_emmons_glacier — 3/5 written
- [0] The White River drive. [1] The Glacier Basin Trail, keeping right at the Emmons Moraine junction. [2] Glacier Basin to Camp Schurman by the Inter Glacier, Camp Curtis and the south side of Steamboat Prow.
- [3] and [4] were left blank because of a **pin defect**. The "Emmons Glacier Corridor" pin (46.848, -121.735, 11,800 ft) sits about 0.4 km SOUTH of Columbia Crest's latitude and about 1.9 km east of it, between the crater and Little Tahoma, near the upper Emmons/Ingraham divide. The Corridor runs from Camp Schurman (46.870) toward the crater (46.852) at about 10,200–11,600 ft. A point on that line near 11,000–11,600 ft would sit near 46.86, -121.745, so the pin is about 1.5 km off the line. Its stored 11,800 ft is also above the 11,600 ft top of the Corridor given in route_breakdown.
- Minor: the Camp Curtis elevation varies within the row: about 9,000 ft (approach), 8,500–9,000 ft (variant) and about 8,700 ft (breakdown).

## wa_mount_rainier_fuhrer_finger — 2/2 written
- [0] The Paradise drive by SR 706 and the Nisqually Entrance. [1] One leg covers the whole route to Columbia Crest.
- The row has no clear defect. The approach says to follow the Wapowety Cleaver crest to camp, and the breakdown says to follow the Wilson's left edge and then cut right to the cleaver. These are compatible.

## wa_mount_rainier_fuhrer_thumb — 2/2 written
- [0] The Paradise drive. [1] One leg covers the whole route.
- **Defect:** the approach and approach_variants say "South-southwest from trailhead via Nisqually Moraine" and "drop southwest onto the Nisqually Moraine". The Nisqually Glacier lies WEST/NW of Paradise; the standard drop is from Glacier Vista. SSW is the wrong direction.
- Possible defect: the approach puts camp at "Lower Castle camp" or near The Castle. The Castle is usually associated with the Kautz approach (Turtle snowfield), west of Wapowety Cleaver, while the Finger row puts the same camps on Wapowety Cleaver at the edge of the Wilson. I avoided naming The Castle.
- The grade is null.

## wa_mount_rainier_gibraltar_ledges — 3/3 written
- [0] The Paradise drive. [1] The Muir Snowfield. [2] Cowlitz Cleaver, the ledges, the Chute, Camp Comfort and the crater.
- Minor: climbing_route step 3 calls the chute "roughly 50°" while the baseFinding says 45°. climbing_route also says the ledges run along the "southern flank" while the baseFinding says "southwest-facing". This is cosmetic.

## wa_mount_rainier_ingraham_direct — 2/3 written ([0] already had text and was not touched)
- [1] The Muir Snowfield. [2] Muir to Columbia Crest by Cathedral Gap and Ingraham Flats.
- **Defect (side of the crater):** the waypoint 2 name "Columbia Crest / SW Crater Rim" and route_breakdown step 5 "to the SW crater rim" contradict climbing_route step 5, which says the route reaches the SOUTHEAST rim near Guide Rock. Southeast is correct for the Ingraham/DC line. My leg names no rim side.
- **Defect in the existing [0] text and approach_logistics:** "nearest supplies are in Ashford, 18 miles away". Ashford is about 6 miles outside the Nisqually Entrance, and Paradise is about 18.5 miles inside it (6.5 miles to Longmire plus 12 to Paradise), so Ashford is about 24 miles away. The "$30 park pass" and "Open June-September" are also dated claims.
- Minor: the crevassed band sits at 11,400–11,800 ft in climbing_route step 2 and at 11,800–12,300 ft in the approach variant.
- route_breakdown entries are bare fragments ("rope up; cross the Cowlitz Glacier to the gap").

<!-- b120.report.md -->
# b120 report

## wa_mount_rainier_kautz_glacier — 1 written (index 0)
- 0 (drive to Paradise) written. Indexes 1-4 already had text, so they were left alone.
- Defect: waypoints 1-3 (Glacier Vista, Wilson Gully, Camp Hazard) have null lat/lng.
- Defect: the itinerary's day 2 says to descend via the DC to Paradise, but descent_text describes reversing the Kautz as the default and the carryover as the alternative. These are only framed differently, not truly contradictory.

## wa_mount_rainier_kautz_headwall — 2 written (0, 1)
- 0 is the drive to Paradise. 1 is one leg from Paradise to Columbia Crest (approach, Camp Hazard, the two ice steps, the upper glacier).
- Defect: the itinerary says "Climb the headwall ice pitches... Continue up the ice chute". The baseFinding says the headwall is a different line from the Kautz ice chute, so if you end up in "the chute" you are off route. The leg avoids naming the chute.
- Note: the waypoint 0 note mentions the Comet Falls alternative start. That is fine, since the drive written goes to the Paradise pin.

## wa_mount_rainier_liberty_ridge — 3 written (0, 1, 2)
- The drive goes to the White River Campground climbers' lot (D loop). The legs follow the row's prose: Glacier Basin Trail, St. Elmo Pass, Winthrop, Curtis Ridge, Carbon, Thumb Rock, east of the Black Pyramid, bergschrund, Liberty Cap.
- No pin defects: the pins for St. Elmo Pass and Liberty Cap check out.

## wa_mount_rainier_mowich_face — 0 written (omitted)
- Drive left blank: the only public road to Mowich Lake (SR 165, Fairfax Bridge) has been closed permanently since April 2025, according to the row, so a drive to this trailhead would point climbers at a road they cannot use. Whoever owns the row should decide what the start of this route is now.
- Leg 1 (Mowich Lake to Liberty Cap) also left blank. That is because the first point has no usable access, not because the line is in doubt.
- Defect: the approach prose says "follow the Wonderland Trail roughly 3-4 miles through Spray Park". From Mowich Lake, Spray Park is reached on the Spray Park Trail, which leaves the Wonderland near the lake. The Wonderland itself goes via Ipsut Pass.
- Defect: the waypoint 1 name "Liberty Cap (inferred route top-out)" is pipeline voice and renders to the climber.

## wa_mount_rainier_nisqually_icefall — 2 written (0, 1)
- 0 is the drive to Paradise. 1 is the single leg from Paradise to Columbia Crest via Glacier Vista, the Nisqually (Wapowety Cleaver on the left), the lower bergschrund near 9,900 ft, the icefall, the 13,000 ft saddle, the Point Success saddle and the crater rim.
- Possible conflict: route_breakdown[0] says the first-ascent line started from the Nisqually cirque "reached from Camp Muir", while approach, approach_variants and baseFinding all give the Glacier Vista drop-off. I followed the majority (current-practice) approach, but the breakdown's history may confuse readers.
- Minor: the approach says to drop onto the glacier "past Glacier Vista/Pan Point", while baseFinding says to drop off at Glacier Vista.

## wa_mount_rainier_ptarmigan_ridge — 1 written (index 0)
- 0 is the drive to the White River Campground. The row says White River is now the only start a car can reach.
- ORDERING FAULT: waypoint 1 is Camp Schurman, which the row's own bivy data calls a descent camp ("not an ascent camp"), yet it is placed between the trailhead and Liberty Cap. The ascent goes via St. Elmo Pass, the Winthrop, Curtis Ridge and the Carbon to the ridge, not via Schurman. Legs 1 and 2 are left blank.
- Defect: the waypoint 2 name "Liberty Cap (commonly the route's summit point)" is pipeline voice.
- Defect: the approach text carries a dated status claim ("road-status page (updated September 18, 2026)") and names a source. It renders to the climber.
- Check: the itinerary's White River approach goes up the Carbon Glacier "toward the base of the ridge", but route_breakdown starts from a high camp below the Ptarmigan ice cliffs and drops onto the Mowich Glacier, which describes the west-side line. Someone should confirm how a White River party actually reaches the Ptarmigan high camp.

<!-- b121.report.md -->
# b121 report

## wa_mount_st_helens_worm_flows — 2/2 written
- [0] drive: I-5 Exit 21, SR 503 to Cougar, FR 90, then FR 83 about 6 mi to Marble Mountain Sno-Park, plus the Sno-Park and climbing permits. Checked against the USFS Worm Flows trailhead page.
- [1] trailhead to the rim: taken from the row's approach and climbing_route.
- Minor defect: the pin's elev is 2,680 ft, but the prose says 2,800 ft (variant: 2,700-2,800). Waypoint 1 is named "Crater Rim", but its pin and elev (8,363) are the true summit. The leg ends at the high point to match the pin.

## wa_open_book_2 — 2/2 written
- [0] drive: Nisqually Entrance, then Stevens Canyon Road 2.7 mi past Reflection and Louise lakes (or 16 mi from the Stevens Canyon Entrance). Confirmed against NPS and WTA.
- [1] the approach from the row, plus the Open Book start. Pins agree: the summit is S of the trailhead, and the gully is on the NW side.
- Minor: the pin is at 4,550 ft, but approach_logistics says ~4,500 ft. Trailhead lng is -121.7072 in the waypoint and -121.7076 in approach_logistics (trivial).

## wa_mount_rainier_sunset_ridge — 0/2 written (omitted)
- DEFECT, pin on the wrong feature. Waypoint 0 is named "West Side Road gate / Dry Creek Trailhead", but its pin (46.7424, -121.8995) is where Westside Road leaves the Nisqually-Longmire road, about 1 mi past the Nisqually Entrance. The WTA "Westside Road" trailhead uses exactly that coordinate. The Dry Creek barricade is ~3 mi farther up the gravel road (NPS, WTA). Both legs touch this waypoint, so both are blank.
- Prose error, same family: the approach and variant say the Dry Creek gate is "about a mile past the Nisqually entrance". That mile is the distance to the road JUNCTION. The gate is ~3 mi up Westside Road from there.
- Prose imprecision: "walk the road roughly 3.5 miles to Round Pass, then the South Puyallup River Trail". NPS gives 3.8 mi gate to Round Pass. The South Puyallup trailhead is ~4.2 mi from the gate, or via a 0.5 mi shortcut trail from Round Pass.
- Structure: there are only 2 waypoints. Leg 1 would be the whole multi-day climb. There are no camp, cleaver or amphitheater pins.

## wa_mount_rainier_tahoma_glacier — 0/2 written (omitted)
- The same Westside Road pin defect as Sunset Ridge: the pin is at the road junction, not at Dry Creek.
- DEFECT, two approaches spliced. The approach, the variant and route_breakdown[0] use the Tahoma Creek Trail to the Wonderland near 5,600 ft and Emerald Ridge. The itinerary (days 1-2, 4) uses the South Puyallup Trail, St Andrews Park and the Puyallup Cleaver, dropping to the glacier at 9,600 ft. The baseFinding mixes both. Leg 1 could not be written without picking one.
- The approach carries a dated status claim: "the park lists the Tahoma Creek Trail as closed". This is worth re-verifying or removing.

## wa_mount_rainier_willis_wall — 0/3 written (omitted)
- [0] already had text, so it was not overwritten. That text carries a dated closure narrative (Fairfax Bridge, April 2025) in a drive field. It reads as status, not directions.
- DEFECT, ordering. Waypoint 1 is Camp Schurman (9,440 ft, Steamboat Prow), but the row's ascent never goes there. Approach, variant and itinerary all go White River, Glacier Basin, St Elmo Pass, across the Winthrop, onto Curtis Ridge and the Carbon Glacier. Schurman appears only in the descent. The array mixes descent and ascent points. Legs 1-2 are blank.
- The approach also cites a fire closure "effective September 5, 2026" of the Wonderland Trail. It is dated status in prose.

## wa_old_snowy_mountain_r1 — 0/7 written (omitted; [0] already filled)
- DEFECT, ordering and wrong junction facts. Sources (USFS Trail 96 page, Oregon Hikers, WTA) agree the Snowgrass Trail reaches the Bypass Trail #97 junction first (~mile 4), then Snowgrass Flat, then the Lily Basin Trail #86 junction (~4.7), then the PCT (~5.4). The array puts "Snowgrass Flats meadows" (whose note says Trail 86 splits there) BEFORE the "Trail 97 junction". WTA's Bypass Trail coordinate (46.4890, -121.4770) matches pin 2, but that trail's high point is only ~6,000 ft. That conflicts with pin 2 sitting above the 6,400 ft meadows pin. Under the ordering rule, every leg is blank.
- Prose errors: the approach says the bypass junction is at "roughly 2 miles"; sources say ~4. It also lists "the Trail 97 junction (west to Cispus Basin/PCT) and the Trail 86 junction" after the flats. That contradicts its own earlier bypass junction and the real order. Waypoint 2's note says 97 "forks west"; Oregon Hikers has it on the right going up.
- route_breakdown[1] says the PCT goes around Old Snowy's "west/south flank". Approach and variant say the west flank.

<!-- b122.report.md -->
# b122 report

## wa_plummer_peak_r1 (6 waypoints): 5 written
- Index 0 already had text, so I left it blank. Legs 1-5 are written from the row's own approach and climbing_route. They agree with the pins: saddle, then SW, then the summit.
- Defect (minor): WP1 and WP2 have null lat/lng.

## wa_point_success_south_side (8 waypoints): 0 written, omitted
- Only index 1 was empty: Dry Creek gate to Indian Henry's.
- Defect: two trailheads disagree. WP0 is the Westside Road / Dry Creek (Tahoma Creek) trailhead, and the waypoint distMi values are measured from it. But approach, approach_logistics and approach_variants all name Longmire via the Wonderland Trail. WP0's own note says the Tahoma Creek corridor is not an NPS-listed access and its trail is reported closed. I wrote no leg 1, as the brief requires.
- Defect: the later legs are already filled and describe the Longmire/Mirror Lakes line. So the array mixes a Dry Creek start with a Longmire-described approach.
- Defect: WP3 (Point 6631 camp) has null coordinates.

## wa_scramble_route, Eagle Peak (3 waypoints): 2 written (drive + trail to the saddle)
- Leg 2 (saddle to summit) is blank because the row contradicts itself on direction:
  - approach_variants.baseFinding says the summit is "roughly a quarter mile EAST along the ridge" from the saddle.
  - climbing_route says to "turn back west/left for the final scramble".
  - The pins put the summit about 170 m almost due NORTH of the saddle pin, which is about 0.1 mi rather than a quarter mile.
  - Chutla Peak lies east of Eagle Peak, so a summit east of the Eagle-Chutla saddle also looks wrong.
  - The saddle pin may itself be misplaced, since it sits directly south of the summit rather than east of it.
- Note: the itinerary calls it the "Eagle Peak-Chutla saddle" at 3.6 mi, while the trail figure is 7.2 mi round trip. These are consistent.

## wa_southwest_scramble, Pinnacle Peak (2 waypoints): 2 written
- I wrote the drive to Reflection Lakes and one leg covering trailhead, saddle, east boot path, chute and summit. The pins agree: the summit is NE of the saddle.
- Defect (minor): the array has only a trailhead and the summit. The saddle (an obvious junction) and the base of the chute are not pinned.

## wa_the_roof, Unicorn Peak (2 waypoints): 2 written
- I wrote the drive and one leg from the trailhead to the summit via Snow Lake, Unicorn Col and the start of The Roof.
- Defect (minor): this route's summit pin (46.74591, -121.70119) differs by about 150 m from the summit pin in wa_unicorn_peak_r1 (46.74455, -121.70120). Both differ from the peakLat in approach_logistics (46.746305).

## wa_unicorn_peak_r1 (7 waypoints): 7 written
- Every leg follows the row's approach and climbing_route, and the order is consistent with the pins.
- Defect (minor): the "Summit block" pin (46.7448) is north of the "Unicorn Peak summit" pin (46.74455). So the base of the horn is plotted beyond the summit, although the two are only about 30 m apart.
- Note: approach_logistics trailheadLng is -121.7076, while the WP0 pin is -121.7071, which is negligible.

Drive check: the Stevens Canyon Road turn is about 15.4 mi from the Nisqually Entrance, and the Snow Lake trailhead is about 3.6 mi east of that turn (land-manager hiking page). I gave no mileage to Reflection Lakes, because the row's 1.4 mi figure looks short against these numbers.

<!-- b123.report.md -->
# b123 report

All five rows have no on-file prose (approach, itinerary, breakdown, etc. all null), so every entry comes from online research and well-established beta.

- **wy_cathedral_traverse** (drive-only, 1 waypoint): wrote 1 of 1 (the Lupine Meadows drive). Defect: the route has just one pin, a trailhead. A traverse with no summit or tower pins has nothing to draw past the parking lot.
- **wy_east_face** (Teewinot, 2 waypoints): wrote 2 of 2. Defect: the summit pin's elevation is 12,273 ft, but Teewinot stands at about 12,325–12,330 ft. That is roughly 55 ft low. The coordinates are correct.
- **wy_owen_spalding** (2 waypoints): wrote 2 of 2. Defect: the summit pin's elevation is 13,700 ft, but the Grand Teton is 13,770 ft. That is 70 ft low. The trailhead pin (43.73457) sits about 170 m south of the other Lupine Meadows pins in this batch (43.7359–43.7361). That is minor, but the same lot is placed three slightly different ways across these routes.
- **wy_southwest_couloir_3** (Middle Teton, 2 waypoints): wrote 2 of 2. Defect: the summit pin's elevation is 12,756 ft, but Middle Teton is about 12,804 ft. That is roughly 48 ft low.
- **wy_cirque_of_the_towers_traverse** (drive-only, 1 waypoint): wrote 1 of 1 (the Big Sandy drive). I kept the gravel section general ("follow the Big Sandy signs") because the junction sequence is complicated and several approaches are in use. Defect: the area is Pingora, but the row has only a trailhead pin. The roughly 8.5 mi hike in via Jackass Pass is not represented.

The Lupine Meadows trailhead elevation varies across the pins: 6,742, 6,749, 6,770 and 6,780 ft for the same lot.
